### [1] [Xemo-Talker: Unlock Emotions Explicitly for Audio-Driven Talking Portrait Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14700)
*Chaolong Yang,Yinuo Guo,Kai Yao,Yuyao Yan,Jie Sun,Guangliang Cheng,Shibin Wu,Bin Dong,Kaizhu Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Xemo-Talker的新方法，能更精确地控制音频驱动说话人动画中的情感表达，同时提升了嘴型同步和推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音频驱动说话人技术在情感控制方面依赖隐式调节，导致控制间接且有限，而且在整体动作空间中进行情感监督又会削弱嘴型同步的准确性。作者希望解决精细情感与嘴型同步难以兼顾的问题。

Method: 作者发现情感相关特征主要分布于动作空间中非主成分部分，于是提出先用主成分训练稳定的中性说话运动，再用轻量情感分支在非主成分空间施加判别性情感监督。同时设计了三重损失函数（类间分离、类内紧凑、非主成分对比）增强情感控制能力。

Result: 方法在情感分类准确率、嘴型同步和推理效率方面都达到了当前最佳，在真实视频的基准下表现接近真实。

Conclusion: Xemo-Talker能够在保持嘴型精确同步和高效推理的前提下，实现对情感状态的精确操控，为音频驱动说话人技术的落地应用提供了有力支撑。

Abstract: Precise emotion control in audio-driven talking heads remains a challenge due to the reliance on implicit emotion regulation in existing systems, which often leads to indirect and insufficient control. Additionally, training with explicit emotion-related losses across the entire motion space poses significant difficulties due to the inherent trade-off between accurate lip synchronization and fine-grained emotion control. In this paper, we reveal a key finding: although emotional cues are distributed throughout the motion space, concentrating discriminative supervision on less-principal components achieves a better emotion-lip synchronization balance, as principal components mainly encode high-energy articulation and pose variations. Building on this insight, we propose Xemo-Talker, which first learns a neutral speech-to-motion mapping for stable articulation and lip synchronization, and then introduces a lightweight emotion branch guided by less-principal subspace supervision. To enhance emotion control, we design a Tri-Loss consisting of inter-class separation, intra-class compactness, and less-principal contrastive learning. Given an audio input, a reference image, and an emotion label, Xemo-Talker achieves state-of-the-art emotion classification accuracy while maintaining competitive lip synchronization and high inference efficiency, with performance approaching that measured on real videos.The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/chaolongy/Xemo-Talker.

</details>


### [2] [Periocular Soft Biometrics: A Survey and Applications to Multimedia Forensics and Disinformation Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14701)
*Fernando Alonso-Fernandez,Kevin Hernandez-Diaz,Josef Bigun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 这篇论文综述了基于眼周区域的软生物特征（如性别、年龄、种族）估计算法及其在取证、身份识别、监控以及合成媒体检测中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在许多需要识别身份的场景下，面部其他区域可能被遮挡，传统生物特征难以提取，而眼周区域常常可见，能提供宝贵的辅助身份信息，因此需要系统性地总结相关研究。

Method: 论文梳理了公开数据集、从手工特征到深度学习方法的发展趋势，并对当前性别、年龄、种族预测的研究现状和主流方法进行了系统性综述。

Result: 系统总结了各类方法的性能，展示了深度学习方法的进步，介绍了多种在实际多媒体取证和检测中的应用案例，比如在监控视频中筛选人口属性和合成数据一致性检测。

Conclusion: 作者指出该领域在数据集偏差、跨域泛化、公平性及伦理、面向取证的基准数据集等方面仍面临诸多挑战，未来需进一步研究解决这些问题以增强实用性。

Abstract: Soft-biometric attributes such as gender, age, and ethnicity provide valuable ancillary evidence when full identity recognition is not feasible, supporting applications in forensic investigation, identity verification, surveillance, or detection of synthetic and manipulated media. Among biometric modalities, the periocular region is a robust source of soft-biometric cues, as it often remains visible when other parts of the face are occluded, a frequent condition in forensic evidence and surveillance footage, and can be captured across a wide range of acquisition conditions. In this paper, we provide a survey of demographic attribute estimation from periocular images, covering publicly available datasets, methodological trends from handcrafted descriptors to deep learning architectures, and the state of the art in gender, age, and ethnicity prediction. We discuss use cases relevant to multimedia forensics and disinformation-detection applications, including demographic filtering in surveillance footage, age verification, and the detection of demographic inconsistencies in synthetic data. We also highlight open challenges, including dataset bias, cross-domain generalisation, fairness, ethical aspects, and the lack of forensic-oriented benchmarks.

</details>


### [3] [Deep Analog: Open-Set Film Emulation with Reference-Conditioned 3D LUTs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14702)
*Yitong Mu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可针对任意参考胶片帧进行开放集胶片拟真（film emulation）的方法，使用参考图像预测3D LUT进行风格迁移，实现快速、可泛化的胶片效果迁移，并解决了现有方法中的专家崩溃（gate collapse）和残差缩放崩溃问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有胶片拟真方法通常受限于闭集：只能在训练时定下有限的LUT风格库，难以对任意新的胶片风格进行迁移。同时，现有的Mixture-of-Experts方法由于训练机制问题，导致模型实际只能利用单一专家，且缺乏对新风格的泛化能力。此外，提升运行速度与可用性，也是实际应用需求。

Method: 本文首先分析了基于LUT专家组合（mixture of experts）方法的失效原因，并通过引入熵正则化提升多LUT利用率。进一步，提出参考驱动的StyleLUTNet，直接从参考影像预测单一3D LUT，实现开放集迁移，并使用自监督方式在程序生成的色彩变换数据上训练。为提升真实性，还引入直方图对齐与物理驱动的光学模拟模块，包括多尺度颗粒与通道辉光。同时，提出正则与重建损失的权衡原则以防止新型崩溃现象。

Result: 在350对自监督样本上的实验显示，色彩阶段PSNR达22.05 dB、SSIM为0.925，完整流程PSNR为21.72 dB、SSIM为0.923。色彩路径1080p下推理仅需5.2ms（192FPS）且可导出标准化.cube LUT用于编辑软件。方法在泛化与速度上均优于传统方法。

Conclusion: 提出的StyleLUTNet及Deep Analog流水线能够快速高效地将任意参考胶片风格迁移到数码图像上，突破了传统方法闭集泛化与运算速度瓶颈，实现了自监督、无须成对训练数据的开放集迁移，并总结了LUT训练中正则化与重建损失的新原则。

Abstract: Film emulation reproduces the look of an analog film stock on a new digital photograph. We target its open-set form -- matching any reference film frame from a single example -- with a 3D lookup table (LUT) predicted from that reference. Real-time image enhancement predicts per-image weights over a fixed bank of 3D LUTs and blends them. We show this is a gated mixture of experts and inherits its failure: trained end-to-end against reconstruction, the gate collapses onto a single expert, so a bank of K LUTs delivers the capacity of one. An entropy term, the enhancement-setting analogue of mixture-of-experts load balancing, restores utilization and recovers about 1 dB PSNR. The deeper constraint survives: a fixed LUT basis is closed-set, freezing the achievable looks at training time. We therefore discard the basis and predict a single 3D LUT as a residual from a reference image (StyleLUTNet), trained by self-supervision on procedurally generated color transforms. The conditional design removes the gate and generalizes open-set to unseen film stocks without paired data or retraining. Around this color backbone we build Deep Analog, a film-emulation pipeline that adds histogram-based tone matching and a physics-informed optical renderer -- multi-scale grain and per-channel halation driven by parameters an inverse network regresses from the reference. On 350 self-supervised pairs the color stage reaches 22.05 dB PSNR / 0.925 SSIM and the full pipeline 21.72 dB / 0.923; the color path runs in 5.2 ms at 1080p (192 FPS) and exports a portable .cube LUT for standard editing tools. A second degeneracy in conditional LUT training -- residual-scale collapse -- shares the root cause and yields a general principle: auxiliary regularization must stay subordinate to reconstruction.

</details>


### [4] [On Cross-Validation for Hyperparameter Optimization of Deep Learning Image Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14705)
*Ljubomir Buturovic*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文比较了三种超参数优化（HPO）方案在小样本深度学习图像分类中的性能估计误差，发现交叉验证方法（cross-validation）在医学图像的小样本下表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习图像分类模型的超参数优化极大影响模型性能，尤其在医学影像领域，因为数据样本通常较小，目前对如何获取用于HPO的可靠验证信号缺乏系统实证分析。作者希望明确不同验证策略对于模型性能估计误差的影响，为实际应用提供指导。

Method: 方法上，作者统一了搜索空间、采样方法、训练流程、模型架构与测试集变量，分别对三种HPO策略（固定划分验证集、每轮洗牌划分验证集、5折交叉验证）进行实验，覆盖二分类医学影像（RSNA肺炎、HAM10000皮肤病变）和自然多分类（Tiny ImageNet）数据集，在不同开发集规模和两种主流骨干网络（ResNet-18和ViT）下评估其性能。性能以验证集与测试集AUROC的绝对误差（AEE）衡量。

Result: 结果显示，在医学数据集上，交叉验证AEE最低，优势在小样本场景尤为明显，随着样本增加该优势减弱，且上述趋势对多重假设调整稳健。在Tiny ImageNet上，三种方案AEE均接近零。测试集AUROC值各方案间差异不大。固定验证集AEE略低于洗牌验证集，但这一结论不够一致。

Conclusion: 作者建议，在小样本医学影像分类场景下，如有算力条件，优先采用基于交叉验证的HPO方案，以获得更可靠的模型测试性能估计。

Abstract: Hyperparameter optimization (HPO) can materially affect the performance of deep learning (DL) image classifiers, but there is little empirical guidance on how to derive the validation signal that drives it, especially for the small sample sizes common in fields such as medical imaging. We compared three HPO protocols in terms of {\em
  absolute performance-estimation error} (AEE; the absolute difference between the winning configuration's validation AUROC and its test AUROC): fixed holdout (F), reshuffled holdout (R), and 5-fold cross-validation (C). The search space, sampler, training procedure, architecture, and test set were held identical across protocols. We evaluated the protocols on three public datasets spanning two regimes: binary medical imaging (RSNA pneumonia radiographs and binarized HAM10000 skin lesions) and 200-class natural imaging (Tiny ImageNet), across a range of development set sizes $n$ and two backbones (ResNet-18 on all datasets, Vision Transformer (ViT-S/16) on RSNA). On the medical datasets, every point estimate favored cross-validation over both holdout protocols, with reductions in AEE largest at small sample sizes and diminishing as $n$ increased. This pattern remained robust under conservative family-wise adjustment. On Tiny ImageNet, AEE was negligible under all three protocols. Test AUROC was generally similar among protocols. Fixed holdout had lower mean AEE than reshuffled holdout in 11 of 12 medical conditions, although this secondary finding was less uniformly supported. For small-sample medical image classification, we recommend cross-validation-based HPO when computational resources permit because it trades additional computation for a more reliable development-time estimate of subsequent test performance.

</details>


### [5] [Equilibrium Forcing: Adaptive Video Generation Without Noise Conditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14706)
*Hansen Jin Lillemark,Alex Rojas,Zachary Novack,Runqian Wang,Yilun Du,Yian Ma,Taylor Berg-Kirkpatrick,Rose Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的视频去噪生成模型Equilibrium Forcing（EqF），无需噪声水平条件，能够实现更灵活和自适应的数据采样与生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于Diffusion和Flow Matching的视频生成方法受限于固定的训练目标和静态采样过程，难以依据数据动态调整推理流程，影响生成视频的质量和一致性。作者希望通过新的方法克服这些局限。

Method: EqF将视频去噪生成分为训练和推理两个模块，且不依赖噪声水平条件；推理时可以根据生成样本反馈自适应地调整采样步骤，从而提高生成效果。

Result: 在多个具挑战性的视频生成基准上，EqF在视频质量和一致性方面优于传统基于噪声水平条件的方法。分析显示，去除噪声水平条件使EqF在推理时能更好依赖数据反馈进行优化。

Conclusion: EqF为视频生成带来了更高灵活性及更好的数据自适应能力，能超越现有主流去噪视频方法，对视频生成领域具有重要意义。

Abstract: Standard autoregressive video generation algorithms based on Diffusion and Flow Matching rely on rigid training objectives and static sampling schedules, limiting inference procedures from adapting to the data. We introduce Equilibrium Forcing (EqF), a simplified framework for video denoising generative models without noise level conditioning. EqF pioneers modular training- and inference-time designs for noise-unconditional generation that decouple learning the denoising field from sampling. This flexibility allows for inference-time algorithms that operate in a closed loop by adapting to feedback from the sample, improving video quality and consistency on challenging autoregressive video generation benchmarks. Extensive analysis elucidates exactly how removing the noise level conditioning enables EqF's data-dependent inference properties to surpass the performance of standard noise level-conditional denoising video methods.

</details>


### [6] [PE-CSNet: An equivariant network architecture with learnable patch-based sparse representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14708)
*Kai Li,Haitao Long,Bo Zhang,Haiwen Zhang,Zhi Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PE-CSNet是一种结合深度展开与可学习稀疏变换的补偿感知重建新架构，能够自适应任务，显著提升重建精度并加快计算速度，在CS-MRI和CS-CDP任务上达到最新最优效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前压缩感知（CS）在医学成像、遥感等领域应用广泛，但高质量CS需设计特定稀疏变换及优化算法，依赖专家经验且参数调优繁琐，制约了实际应用。

Method: 提出一种基于块坐标下降（BCD）算法展开的深度网络PE-CSNet，将传统预设的patch稀疏性推广为可学习稀疏变换，采用端到端训练以自动学习模型与求解参数，并引入随机等变性训练策略提升数据效率。网络还提供了参数共享的简化版本，并讨论了其收敛性。

Result: PE-CSNet在CS磁共振成像（CS-MRI）和编码衍射图样（CS-CDP）等任务上达到当前最高精度和快速重建速度，超越了传统方法和已有深度展开方法。

Conclusion: PE-CSNet简化了稀疏变换与模型参数的设计过程，通过端到端优化实现了高效、鲁棒的CS重建，在高精度和高速度场景下展现出极大潜力。

Abstract: Compressive sensing (CS) enables accurate signal reconstruction from sparse measurements and is widely applied in medical imaging, remote sensing, and image compression. However, designing an effective, task-specific sparse transform and the corresponding optimization procedure for high-quality CS remains challenging. This process typically requires expert domain knowledge and laborious parameter tuning. To address this issue, we present a Patch-based Equivariant deep unrolling architecture, termed PE-CSNet, for accurate CS recovery. While traditional CS methods generally use predefined patch-based transform sparsity, we generalize this idea by incorporating learnable transform sparsity that adapts to the specific CS task through an optimization-driven process. Specifically, we first establish a generalized patch-based CS model, which we solve via a block coordinate descent (BCD) algorithm. The BCD solver is then unrolled into a deep neural network, where all parameters of both the CS model and solver are learned through end-to-end training. To improve data efficiency, we introduce a stochastic equivariant training strategy that exploits the patch-wise structure of the network, enabling PE-CSNet to learn effectively even from limited data. We further provide a simpler, parameter-shared version of PE-CSNet and briefly discuss its convergence as an iterative solver. For practical applications, the network uses stage-specific (non-shared) parameters to enhance its expressive power and thereby improve its performance. On the tasks of CS magnetic resonance imaging (CS-MRI) and CS coded diffraction patterns (CS-CDP), PE-CSNet achieves state-of-the-art accuracy with fast computational speed, outperforming traditional methods and existing deep unrolling methods.

</details>


### [7] [Path2ST: Hierarchical Cell-Tissue Grounded Cross-Modal Translation for Spatial Transcriptomics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14710)
*Ruochen Liu,Wei Lou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的深度学习方法（Path2ST），可通过H&E染色切片图像高效准确地预测空间基因表达，用于替代昂贵的空间转录组学实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究利用H&E染色切片预测空间转录组学，但通常只将其视为普通图像，未充分利用细胞与组织层次的生物学结构信息。本工作旨在更好地结合影像与生物学内涵，实现更精准的空间基因表达预测。

Method: 提出Path2ST框架，包括：(1)层次化细胞-组织条件机制，将细胞与组织层次的特征信息融合形成条件信号；(2)基于层次语义词表的自回归表达合成，实现从粗到细的一致预测；(3)提出SpectraLoss损失函数，覆盖基因表达排序、转录爆发建模与细胞类型语义对齐。

Result: 在三个公开数据集上的实验验证，Path2ST优于现有方法，在预测精度和空间一致性方面都达到了最新最优水平。

Conclusion: Path2ST兼顾生物学层次信息，有效提升了H&E切片对空间基因表达的预测能力，具有实际应用潜力。相关代码已公开。

Abstract: Predicting spatial gene expression from hematoxylin and eosin (H\&E)-stained images offers a cost-effective alternative to spatial transcriptomics (ST). However, existing methods treat H\&E images as generic visual inputs and ignore their intrinsic biological hierarchy, where spatially organized cell types collectively form functional tissue microenvironments that govern local gene expression programs. To bridge this gap, we formulate H\&E-to-ST prediction as a cross-modal semantic translation task and propose Path2ST, a hierarchically grounded autoregressive framework featuring three key components: (i) a Hierarchical Cell-Tissue Conditioning mechanism that fuses explicit and implicit cellular features with tissue-level semantic representations to construct hierarchical conditioning signals; (ii) a Scale-Adaptive Autoregressive Generation process over a hierarchical semantic vocabulary, enabling coarse-to-fine, biologically consistent expression synthesis; and (iii) SpectraLoss, a full-spectrum objective that jointly enforces ordinal fidelity, models transcriptional bursts, and aligns semantic structures with cell types. Extensive experiments on three datasets demonstrate state-of-the-art performance, validating that Path2ST generates highly accurate and spatially coherent transcriptomic profiles. The related code is released at https://github.com/RuochenLiu23/Path2ST.

</details>


### [8] [Local Gains and Fixed-Assignment Set Losses in Shared Set Decoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14717)
*Ze Zhang,Yang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在ResNet-50 DETR系列模型中，对查询-关系的删除操作如何影响特定slot的表现以及整体预测集的效用。通过对710对图像-关系单元分析，揭示删除带来的局部改善和整体损失之间的权衡观测。结果显示删除影响因模型和读出方式不同而表现各异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于理解对模型特定slor的精确编辑（如删除某个关系）对整体模型表现的影响，厘清局部干预与全局预测效用间的矛盾及其原因。

Method: 以ResNet-50 DETR系列两个模型为对象，记录并分析710对图像-关系单元，通过对比目标删除与配对对照删除（对照为选择不同接收体），采用复合对比分析删除操作的影响，并考查重新配对和不同读出操作对结果的影响。

Result: 在两个模型中，目标减对照的对比表现为本地正效应，但在固定分配下为负效应；具体表现与模型有关，且重新配对后统计有所变化。此外，不同编辑方式（硬删除 vs 质量保持编辑）在重新配对前后影响不同。

Conclusion: 本地干预成功并不能代表对整体预测集的影响，删除操作的效应依赖于选择条件及读出方式，没有发现统一不变的干预机制，不同模型间预测影响也有差异。

Abstract: A query-relation deletion can improve the edited slot while reducing the utility of the prediction set that contains it. We study this tension in two related ResNet-50 DETR-family checkpoints using recorded, selection-conditional evidence from 710 paired image-relation units per checkpoint. The primary comparison subtracts a matched active control, which deletes the same leader source at a different recorded recipient, from the selected target deletion. It is therefore a composite contrast rather than a same-recipient placebo.
  The target-minus-control contrast is locally positive and fixed-assignment negative in both checkpoints. The opposite-sign pattern occurs within 302/710 DETR units and 460/710 DINO units. After rematching, the corresponding counts are 285/710 and 433/710. Rematching and native selection absorb enough of the mean loss for DETR intervals to cross zero, whereas DINO intervals remain negative, so persistence across readouts differs by checkpoint. A fixed-map comparison between hard deletion and a mass-preserving edit also differs before rematching. That comparison is conditional on the outcome-blind map and does not establish same-dose transport.
  Local intervention success therefore does not determine the consequence for a jointly decoded set. The supported conclusion is selection-conditional deletion sensitivity whose persistence depends on the readout and intervention operator. We do not identify an intervention-invariant edge mechanism, detector-level degradation, population prevalence, or the value of a training-time regularizer.

</details>


### [9] [VideoGAIA: A Benchmark for General AI Assistants on Agentic Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14718)
*Fan Zhang,Guangming Yao,Jinyang Wu,Hao Wu,Zheng Lian,Xinyu Geng,Jingdong Chen,Yi Yuan,Pheng-Ann Heng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了VideoGAIA基准，用以更高难度地评测多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）的视频理解能力，尤其针对先进模型单轮问答任务逐渐饱和的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视频理解基准任务准确率已接近90%，评测能力趋于饱和，无法区分更先进MLLMs的智能水平。需要更复杂、多轮对话和工具增强的新型任务，以推动模型向更智能体化和通用人工智能方向发展。

Method: 作者提出VideoGAIA基准，将视频理解设计为多轮、带有工具调用和多模态证据整合的交互过程。该基准共包含271个人机共设复杂真实任务，并对每个任务由三位专家独立验证正确性和难度。

Result: 即使是当前最先进的MLLMs如GPT-5.5和Kimi-K3，在VideoGAIA上的准确率仍低于60%，显著低于以往任务，显示任务难度和区分力极高。

Conclusion: VideoGAIA作为高质量、多轮工具增强的视频理解任务集，可以有效衡量和推动下一代MLLMs在更复杂的智能体型视频理解上的能力进步。

Abstract: Video understanding is a fundamental task for evaluating the capabilities of multimodal large language models (MLLMs). However, existing leading models have already achieved approximately 90% accuracy on the Video-MME leaderboard, suggesting that conventional single-turn video understanding tasks are becoming increasingly saturated and insufficient for assessing the intelligence of advanced MLLMs. Towards this end, we introduce VideoGAIA, an agentic video understanding benchmark for general artificial intelligence (AI) assistants. Moving beyond one-shot video question answering, VideoGAIA formulates video understanding as a multi-turn, tool-augmented interaction process, where models must iteratively perceive videos, invoke external tools, gather complementary information, and integrate multimodal evidence across turns. VideoGAIA contains 271 model-human co-designed tasks covering diverse and complex real-world scenarios. Each video-question-answer instance is independently verified by three human experts to ensure both correctness and appropriate difficulty. All evaluated MLLMs, including frontier models such as GPT-5.5 and Kimi-K3, achieve less than 60% accuracy on VideoGAIA, highlighting its value as a high-quality and timely benchmark for evaluating next-generation MLLMs. We hope that VideoGAIA will facilitate the transition from conventional video understanding toward agentic video understanding.

</details>


### [10] [DeCo-MIL: Debiased Counterfactual Reasoning for Long-Tailed Whole Slide Image Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14719)
*Xiaoxiao Li,Xitong Ling,Jiawen Li,Weiming Chen,Zhenyang Cai,Xidong Wang,Tian Guan,Benyou Wang,Yonghong He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种新的多实例学习方法DeCo-MIL，能够有效解决病理全切片图像(WSI)分析中嵌套的双长尾分布问题，在多个数据集上取得了SOTA表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WSI分析中，样本类别分布通常具有严重长尾特性，不仅在切片间存在类别数量不均（外部长尾），在每张切片内部，诊断证据也具有长尾分布（内部长尾）；这种双长尾耦合导致模型对稀有类别的识别性能低下。

Method: 论文提出DeCo-MIL方法，针对内部长尾，通过对切片内patch进行聚类并与正常原型替换，使用反事实推理与频率纠偏，突出关键诊断证据，去除冗余信息；对外部长尾，通过anchor分层、伪bag构建与尾类过采样提升稀有类别的有效监督，结合一致性正则化保持数据原始组织形态。

Result: 在三大长尾WSI基准数据集上，DeCo-MIL在尾类识别和整体分类性能上均取得了最新的最好成绩。

Conclusion: DeCo-MIL能有效缓解双长尾现象，提升长尾WSI分析准确性，尤其在稀有类别识别方面有显著提升，对实际病理辅助诊断有重要意义。

Abstract: Multiple instance learning (MIL) is widely used for weakly supervised whole slide image (WSI) analysis. However, under long-tailed distributions, MIL-based WSI analysis faces a nested dual long-tail: an inter-slide class long tail and an intra-slide long tail of instance-level discriminative evidence. The two long tails are coupled: tail classes have few training slides, while their limited diagnostic evidence is concentrated in a few patches and obscured by abundant within-bag redundancy. This coupling biases models toward head classes and degrades rare-class recognition. To address this, we propose DeCo-MIL for long-tailed WSI analysis, which jointly alleviates the nested dual long-tail through frequency-debiased counterfactual reasoning. For the inner long tail, DeCo-MIL clusters patches into tissue-morphology anchors, replaces each anchor with its matched normal prototype to perform a counterfactual intervention, and estimates its counterfactual contribution to the ground-truth class using class-frequency-corrected predictions. These contributions guide redundancy masking to preserve scarce discriminative instances. For the outer long tail, DeCo-MIL constructs anchor-stratified pseudo-bags from redundancy-reduced bags and combines tail-aware oversampling with consistency regularization, increasing effective supervision for tail classes while preserving tissue-morphology composition. Extensive experiments on three long-tailed WSI benchmarks demonstrate that DeCo-MIL achieves state-of-the-art performance in both tail-class recognition and overall classification.

</details>


### [11] [AeroGround: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Aerial-Ground Collaborative Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14721)
*Shenghong Yi,Lin Zhang,Muzian Li,Jiakang Yuan,Haoyu Zhang,Peng Ye,Jiayuan Fan,Huafeng Qin,Tao Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文引入了AeroGround基准，用于评价视觉-语言模型（VLM）在空地协作理解与推理任务中的表现，并揭示了现有模型与人类之间的显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管VLM被广泛应用于无人机的理解与推理任务，现有基准大多仅关注空中视角，缺乏对现实中常见的空地协作场景（如救援、基础设施巡检）的系统研究。

Method: 作者构建了AeroGround基准，包括基于仿真的空地视角多模态数据集（约2.9万组观测）和2250个高质量问答实例，涵盖跨视角对应、空间理解与推理。通过对16个预训练VLM及其改进版本进行实验评测，分析模型在上述任务中的优势与不足。

Result: 最优VLM模型的平均准确率为54.4%，显著低于人类的93.3%；揭示了现有模型在复杂空地协作推理中的明显短板。

Conclusion: AeroGround为空地协作智能体系统的VLM研究与发展提供了基础，并有助于推动更具能力的协作模型设计。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have been widely employed in understanding and reasoning tasks for unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs). Existing UAV benchmarks primarily focus on aerial-view scenarios. However, whether current VLMs can perform well on understanding and reasoning tasks in aerial-ground collaborative scenarios which are practical in real-world applications like rescue and infrastructure inspection remains underexplored. To address this gap, we introduce AeroGround, a comprehensive benchmark for evaluating VLMs in aerial-ground collaborative reasoning. AeroGround is built upon a simulated aerial-ground dataset containing approximately 29,000 multimodal observation groups from diverse open environments, and provides 2,250 high-quality question-answering instances covering cross-view correspondence, spatial understanding, and reasoning. Experiments on 16 pretrained VLMs, together with two domain-adapted variants, reveal a substantial gap between current models and human performance: the best model achieves an average accuracy of 54.4%, whereas humans reach 93.3%. By systematically revealing the strengths and limitations of existing models in aerial-ground collaborative reasoning, AeroGround provides a foundation for developing more capable aerial-ground collaborative embodied intelligence systems.

</details>


### [12] [Braided Vision Transformer for Stroke Detection in Multi-view Retinal Fundus Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14722)
*Aysen Degerli,Mika Hilvo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于视网膜眼底图像的脑卒中检测方法，采用创新的Braided Vision Transformer（BViT）模型，在自建数据集上检测脑卒中和短暂性脑缺血发作，效果优于常规视觉Transformer。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑卒中仍是重要的致死和致残原因，需快速、准确检测。传统神经影像高成本、侵入性强，视网膜眼底成像因能反映脑血管状况、成本低、无创且便携，成为理想的替代筛查方法。

Method: 作者首次将视觉Transformer应用于眼底图像脑卒中检测，引入BViT模型，可从左右眼的中心凹与视神经头为中心的多视角图像中提取特征，同时捕捉不同视角间的联系，从而获取更全面的视网膜生物标志物信息。

Result: 在自建Stroke-Data数据集上，BViT模型用于脑卒中检测的AUC达0.75，优于传统视觉Transformer模型。

Conclusion: BViT模型能够有效结合多视角眼底图像特征，提升了脑卒中筛查的准确性，显示了视网膜眼底影像在无创、便携、快速脑卒中检测中的应用潜力。

Abstract: Stroke remains a leading cause of mortality and morbidity worldwide, emphasizing the importance of its accurate and immediate assessment. Retinal fundus imaging has emerged as a promising modality for stroke assessment, as the retina reflects cerebrovascular and neurological risk factors. Contrary to conventional neuroimaging techniques, retinal fundus imaging offers a non-invasive, cost-effective, and portable alternative for rapid screening. This paper explores the feasibility of retinal fundus imaging for stroke and transient ischemic attack (TIA) detection using macula-centric and optic nerve head-centric views captured from both eyes. Our study introduces, to the best of our knowledge, the first vision transformer model for retinal fundus imaging in stroke assessment, offering a novel approach for capturing retinal patterns. Thereby, we propose the Braided Vision Transformer (BViT) model, which extracts representative features from the given multi-view images while simultaneously capturing inter-view relationships across both eyes, enabling a more informative understanding of retinal biomarkers associated with cerebrovascular events. Experiments conducted on our collected Stroke-Data dataset demonstrate that BViT achieves an AUC score of 0.75 for stroke detection, outperforming regular vision transformers.

</details>


### [13] [A Vision Transformer for ECG-Based Detection of Left Ventricular Systolic Dysfunction Across Multiple Clinical Sites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14723)
*Burcu Ozek,Aruna Mohan,David Vorchheimer,Daniel Weiss,Eyal Kedar,Tamar Sobol,Or Zilbershot,Fatemeh Afghah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本论文利用视觉Transformer深度学习模型，通过分析12导联心电图自动检测射血分数降低（LVEF≤40%），实现了较高的诊断准确性，并具备良好的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 射血分数降低常无症状且难及早发现，传统心电图由于缺乏特异性波形，未能充分用于筛查，这导致许多患者在出现严重心力衰竭后才被诊断。研究希望利用心电图作为前置筛查工具，通过深度学习提升早期发现率。

Method: 从三家美国医疗系统七个分院收集10,142名患者的12导联心电图数据，采用视觉Transformer模型对每个心跳独立分析，训练模型识别射血分数降低患者，并在独立外部队列（4,092例患者）进行性能验证。

Result: 模型在独立外部队列中AUC 0.88，灵敏度81.2%，特异度81.0%，阴性预测值高达97.8%。各亚组（性别、种族、合并症等）均有较高灵敏度，但老年、房颤或心肌病患者特异度相对较低。可视化注意力图显示模型主要关注QRS波群。

Conclusion: 该方法可辅助心电图成为筛查射血分数降低的高效工具，尤其适合多样化人群的初筛，为推荐超声心动图提供依据，有助于早期发现和管理心衰风险患者。

Abstract: Reduced left ventricular ejection fraction (LVEF) is frequently asymptomatic and often detected only after advanced heart failure develops. Electrocardiograms are recorded routinely yet underused for this condition, because reduced LVEF has no single diagnostic waveform. We trained an ensemble of vision transformers from scratch to detect reduced LVEF ($\leq$40%) from 12-lead ECGs, analyzing each heartbeat individually, using 10,142 patients across seven sites in three US health systems. In a held-out external cohort of 4,092 patients from three geographically independent US clinical sites at a real-world reduced-LVEF prevalence of 8.72%, the model achieved an AUROC of 0.88 (95% CI 0.86-0.89), sensitivity 81.2%, specificity 81.0%, and negative predictive value 97.8%. Sensitivity remained high across sex, race, ethnicity, and comorbidity subgroups, while specificity was lower in older patients and those with atrial fibrillation or cardiomyopathy. Beat-level attention maps provided interpretability into the model's predictions, showing consistent focus on the QRS complex rather than the P wave. These findings support the potential of routine ECGs as a scalable first-pass triage step to identify patients who should undergo echocardiography for reduced ejection fraction across diverse patient populations.

</details>


### [14] [Privacy-Preserving Dataset Curation for Kuala Lumpur Urban Traffic: Grounded Vision-Language Detection with Spatial Vehicle-Context Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14724)
*Mohammed Abdul Al Arafat Tanzin,Rudzidatul Akmam Dziyauddin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本论文提出了一套针对在马来西亚吉隆坡收集的交通视频数据自动匿名化的框架，显著提升在复杂热带城市环境下的隐私保护效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂热带城市（如吉隆坡）在交通视频采集中，高摩托车密度、暗色车牌、大幅倾斜摄像头及强烈眩光导致传统PII匿名化手段失效，亟需新方法确保数据隐私。

Method: 采用移动自行车平台采集交通视频数据，并提出框架，将Grounding DINO（开放集视觉-语言变换器）与创新的空间车辆ROI包络引擎结合，通过保证车牌中心点在合法车辆范围内来减少误报，同时自动模糊人脸、头部和车牌。

Result: 在1,266帧数据初步评估中，框架成功率约95%，主要失败情况为目标过小、严重遮挡或角度极端，但极大地抑制了环境误报。

Conclusion: 该公开框架及演示笔记本为隐私敏感型数据集预处理提供了自动化、可审计的方案，既保障数据隐私，又保留场景上下文，为后续视觉任务打下基础，但正式法律合规仍依赖更广泛治理。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of intelligent transportation systems and autonomous driving relies heavily on multi-modal urban traffic datasets. However, curating high-fidelity video imagery in complex tropical urban environments---specifically Kuala Lumpur, Malaysia---presents severe challenges for Personally Identifiable Information (PII) anonymization due to high motorcycle density, dark acrylic license plates, dynamic camera tilt, and extreme tropical glare. We propose an automated anonymization framework tailored for the Kuala Lumpur Road Dataset, captured via a mobile cycling platform at 2 FPS. We document how legacy Haar cascades and YOLOv8 fail under these conditions---generating false positives on background elements while missing rotated or occluded targets. Our architecture resolves this by integrating Grounding DINO---a zero-shot open-set vision-language transformer---with a novel Spatial Vehicle Region of Interest (ROI) Containment Engine. By requiring license plate centroids to reside within validated vehicle boundaries, the pipeline suppresses environmental false positives while automatically obfuscating faces, heads, and license plates. An initial evaluation on 1,266 frames demonstrates a $\sim$95\% success rate, with remaining failures restricted to small, heavily occluded, oblique, or ambiguous targets. Coupled with temporal persistence mechanisms and an automated quality-control auditor, the framework minimizes privacy-related false negatives while preserving scene context for downstream vision tasks. While formal legal compliance depends on broader governance procedures, this publicly available pipeline and demonstration notebook provide an auditable preprocessing stage for privacy-aware dataset curation.

</details>


### [15] [Spatial Attention Noise Masking for Causally Sufficient Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14725)
*Benjamin Formby,Kuang-Ching Wang,D Hudson Smith*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种创新的因果解释方法，通过动态掩蔽输入图像以解释计算机视觉模型的决策过程，同时保持分类准确率和模型鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习在医疗、安防和自动驾驶等高风险领域应用广泛，但其预测结果缺乏可解释性。而现有的解释方法多为被动且主要是相关性解释，无法满足对因果性的需求。此外，现有效果不佳的主动因果方法也缺乏对输入特征的责任归因。

Method: 提出了一种基于空间注意力的噪声掩蔽框架，包括UNet风格的mask生成器以及ResNet18编码器和线性分类器，同时对原图和掩蔽后的图像进行分类训练；通过稀疏和平滑化正则项约束生成的mask，保证掩蔽后与原特征表征一致。

Result: 生成的特征归因掩码在五个分类任务中实现了高忠实度解释，遮挡大量图像信息仍能保持接近基线的分类性能，并对背景置换及自然对抗样本等分布变化具备强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 所提方法不仅在解释性方面与当前主流特征归因方法竞争，还额外提供了强因果解释能力，有助于构建更可信赖的深度学习视觉系统。

Abstract: We present a novel causal approach to interpretability for computer vision models that dynamically masks the input image prior to classification. The interpretability of deep learning predictions is critical in high-stakes fields such as medical imaging, security, and autonomous driving. Most interpretability methods are applied passively to already trained models, which typically result in correlational rather than causal explanations. Existing causal interpretability methods are limited to post hoc analysis, weakening the causal claims. Additionally, existing active methods generally lack explanations that explicitly assign responsibility to input features. This work proposes a spatial attention noise masking framework that provides causal explanations about the features sufficient for the prediction. The proposed framework consists of: 1) a UNet-style mask generator, and 2) a Resnet18 encoder and linear classifier that classifies both masked and unmasked versions of an input image. The generated masks are regularized to be sparse and spatially smooth, while masked image embeddings are constrained to remain consistent with embeddings from the corresponding unmasked images. The resulting masks can be interpreted as feature attribution maps that are competitive with related interpretability methods while additionally providing strong causal explanations of model predictions. Quantitative evaluations demonstrate mask faithfulness, near-baseline classification performance across five classification tasks despite substantial masking of image information, and robustness to distribution shifts such as background swapping and natural adversarial examples. Qualitative comparisons further demonstrate mask behavior and competitive interpretability relative to state-of-the-art feature attribution methods.

</details>


### [16] [Low Cost Two-Stage Fabric Defect Detection at the Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14727)
*Rasel Hossen,Diptajoy Mistry,Mosaddek Hossain Kamal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种低成本的纺织品缺陷检查级联系统，并将其部署于NVIDIA Jetson Nano，实现端到端自动化检测。该系统由一个高召回率但误报率相对较高的自编码器异常检测器（Stage 1）与YOLOv5n检测器（Stage 2）组成，既提高了缺陷捕获能力，又兼顾实时性。实验表明，主要加速贡献来自图像解码与推理并行而非真正由级联结构带来。系统更适合用作AI辅助初筛，而非完全自动判定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前低收入国家的制衣行业中，自动化面料瑕疵检测设备昂贵，经济适用型中小型纺织厂通常依赖人工，急需更低成本、高实用性的自动检测方案。由于缺陷在受控生产下稀疏，仅在可疑帧上用高性能检测器的级联结构成为自然而经济的选择。

Method: 系统分为两级：第一级为小型卷积自编码器，利用解码器注意力门、边缘权重重构损失与YOLOv5n特征蒸馏，快速筛查异常帧；第二级用YOLOv5n对疑似帧进一步检测。模型在Jetson Nano端部署，采用TensorRT FP16加速。性能在一个249张图片的独立测试集上评估，并与单一YOLO流程以及单纯自编码器做比较。

Result: 在20张瑕疵图像、229张非瑕疵图像条件下，第一级可召回全部20例（置信区间95%：0.83-1.00），假阳性为49.3%，与纯自编码器对比假阳性降低19.3%（显著性p=0.011）。推理速度由串行YOLOv5n的9.86 FPS提升至级联并行的13.45 FPS，但其中91%的加速归因于JPEG解码与推理并行，级联本身仅带来5.1%的推理减少。转发主要受假阳性限制，高达85%的转发为误报。

Conclusion: 级联结构的加速效果实际较有限，更大加速受制于数据通路优化。系统适用于AI辅助初筛、优先级分流，而非终极自动验收。级联速率的评测需谨防数据通路未受控的影响。

Abstract: Fabric inspection in the garment industries of low-income economies remains largely manual, and commercial vision systems are priced beyond most small and medium mills. Because defects are sparse under controlled production, a natural response is a cascade: screen every frame with a cheap anomaly detector and invoke a full detector only on suspicious frames. We build such a cascade for four knit-fabric defect classes and deploy it end-to-end on an NVIDIA Jetson Nano with TensorRT FP16. Stage 1 is a compact convolutional autoencoder with decoder attention gates, an edge-weighted reconstruction loss, and feature-level distillation from a frozen YOLOv5n teacher; Stage 2 is YOLOv5n, invoked only on flagged frames. On a 249-image benchmark disjoint from detector training (20 defective, 229 non-defective), Stage 1 at a recall-prioritised threshold flags all 20 defective images (95% CI 0.83-1.00) at a false-positive rate of 49.3% (113/229), reducing false positives by 19.3% relative to a plain autoencoder (p=0.011). The parallel pipeline reaches 13.45 FPS against 9.86 FPS for a sequential YOLO-only loop. Our central finding comes from decomposing that 1.36x: 91% of it is attributable to overlapping JPEG decode with inference rather than to the cascade, which contributes only a 5.1% inference reduction at the measured forwarding rate p = 0.534. We further show that forwarding here is false-positive-limited rather than prevalence-limited - 85% of forwarded frames are false alarms - and quantify the 29-45% inference reduction attainable under tighter calibration. We report this as a caution for cascade speedups measured without controlling the data path, and position the system as AI-assisted triage rather than autonomous acceptance.

</details>


### [17] [Do CNNs Internally Represent Real and Fake Images Differently? A Hidden-Layer Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14729)
*Moumita Sen Sarma,Pascal Hitzler,Eugene Y. Vasserman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了CNN对真实图像与伪造/合成图像内部表示的差异，发现两者在隐藏层激活模式上存在显著不同，并指出这些差异可用于改进假图像检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着伪造和合成图像的激增，目前尚不清楚深度神经网络如CNN是否以相同方式处理真实与虚假图像。理解其内部表征差异有助于解决假图像检测难题。

Method: 研究在场景识别任务中，使用训练好的CNN模型，提取真实及由Stable Diffusion等生成模型生成的伪造图像的密集层激活。利用神经符号方法为神经元赋予语义标签，对真实-伪造图像的配对激活模式进行统计比较，同时引入不同数据集、CNN架构及图像降质（如JPEG压缩、模糊）做对照实验。

Result: 实验显示，伪造图像在CNN隐藏层中激活的神经元分布与真实图像存在区别，这种差异不仅仅是由图像质量劣化导致。在不同模型与设置下结果一致。

Conclusion: CNN对真实与伪造图像存在不同的内部表示，为基于此开发更有效的假图像检测方法提供了理论依据，未来可利用该现象提升检测性能。

Abstract: Fake/synthetic images are increasingly prevalent, but it remains unclear whether Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) process real and fake images in the same internal manner. This work examines the hypothesis that CNNs represent real and fake images differently, such that fake images induce different hidden-layer activation patterns even when semantic content is preserved. The hypothesis is evaluated in scene recognition settings using trained CNN models. Dense-layer activations are extracted, and neurosymbolic methods assign semantic labels to selected neurons. For each real test image, corresponding fake images are generated with similar semantic content using object-label-guided text-to-image and image-to-image generation based on Stable Diffusion variants. Paired real-fake activation patterns are then compared statistically. Additional experiments with another dataset, CNN architecture, generative model, and JPEG/blur degradation analysis assess robustness. Results suggest that fake images evoke different hidden-neuron activations, and these differences are not explained only by simple image degradation. Overall, the findings indicate that real and fake images differ in CNN hidden-layer activation behavior at least in some settings, which opens the door for follow-up work on making use of this different behavior to improve fake image detection.

</details>


### [18] [IP Protection in the Era of Visual Generative AI: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14730)
*Zhuan Shi,Shunchang Liu,Alireza Dehghanpour Farashah,Qian Yang,Han Yu,Cao Yang,Chaochao Chen,Yuping Yan,Yaochu Jin,Golnoosh Farnadi,Lingjuan Lyu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种关于视觉生成AI知识产权保护的二维分类方法，更系统、直观地梳理和评估已有技术防御方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉生成AI快速发展，产生大量知识产权风险（包括未授权学习、复制、提取、滥用和再分发）。已有防护技术已提出，但现有综述容易忽略各方法真正的保护意图，因此需要更合理、清晰的分类和综述。

Method: 作者提出以“控制逻辑”为主轴（信息泄露控制、生成行为约束、归属与问责），以“数据与模型资产”区分维度，构建二维分类体系。用该体系系统回顾技术方法，并将评估协议与防护目标对应，评述未来挑战。

Result: 构建了一个系统化的综述框架，对现有防护技术进行梳理，总结评估方法与目的的一致性，归纳了技术发展中尚存的挑战。

Conclusion: 该工作为视觉生成AI领域学者提供了更清晰、系统的知识产权保护技术全景图，有助于识别技术空白与未来研究方向。

Abstract: The rapid evolution of visual generative AI has introduced a wide range of intellectual property risks, spanning the unauthorized learning, reproduction, extraction, misuse, and redistribution of protected data and model assets. To address these risks, a growing body of technical defenses has been proposed. However, existing surveys typically organize this literature by lifecycle stage or technical mechanism, which can obscure the protective intent of different methods. This survey presents a two-dimensional taxonomy for IP protection in visual generative models. The primary axis is a Control Logic View, which classifies methods into Information Exposure Control, Generative Behavior Constraint, and Attribution & Accountability according to the risk variable they regulate. The secondary axis distinguishes Data IP from Model IP as cross-cutting asset dimensions. Under this framework, we systematically review protection methods, align evaluation protocols with protection objectives, and discuss open challenges including proactive model-level safeguards, standardized evaluation, robustness against adaptive attacks, and explainable evidence. This survey aims to offer a principled, systematic, and easy-to-follow overview for both new and experienced researchers in visual generative AI IP protection.

</details>


### [19] [Emergence of Transfer Learning towards Specific Identification of Alzheimer's Disease A Prospective Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14731)
*Soumik Podder,Chandramouli Haldar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文综述了迁移学习（TL）在阿尔茨海默病（AD）识别中的应用，重点讨论其优势、局限与可解释性AI的融合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前深度学习和机器学习在AD神经影像数据的检测中存在过拟合等问题，尤其在数据有限的情况下表现不佳。因此，有必要探索更有效的方法提升模型泛化能力和解释性。

Method: 文章系统回顾了使用迁移学习对AD进行分类、识别和疾病进展预测的研究，评价了其在数据有限时提升诊断准确率的能力，并首次将可解释性AI纳入TL的应用讨论中。

Result: 迁移学习通过利用大规模数据集上的预训练模型，在AD检测中展现出提升诊断准确率的能力，尤其是在样本量有限的情境下；但在影像预处理有限或异常变化时准确率仍受影响。

Conclusion: 结合可解释性AI的迁移学习有望解决目前AD诊断模型在数据不足和模型透明性方面的挑战，为未来相关疾病的智能检测提供了有价值的方向。

Abstract: Worldwide, millions of senior citizens are suffering from Alzheimer disease abbreviated as AD, a well- versed form of dementia. AD is featured by amnesia, intellectual disability, and difficulty with consciousness. DL and ML models are undoubtedly explored to identify AD related patterns on large dimensional neuroimaging data but they need global optimization and are suffering from overfitting issue that might yield dissatisfactory result in testing data set. DL overcomes the issue by convolution of input image with kernel but any sudden change in the MRI image or human manipulation, limited pre- processing of the images can mislead CNN in achieving highly accurate detection. Transfer Learning (TL) has proved itself in AD diagnosis by utilizing pre-trained models on large data sets to guide novice model in a new neuroimaging dataset. This review provides an inclusive glimpse of TL implication in classification, identification including the conversion of AD. Keeping in view, we have assessed the strengths and limitations of TL in improvising diagnostic accuracy even with limited data. The uniqueness of the present review is the incorporation of explainable AI in TL based AD diagnosis system. Finally, it can be claimed that the review will guide the new re-searchers in the area of TL induced neurodegenerative disease detection.

</details>


### [20] [From Dense Prediction to Visual Editing: Structured Supervision for Unified Image and Video Creation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14740)
*Zhefan Rao,Bin Zou,Haoxuan Che,Xuanhua He,Chong Hou Choi,Yanheng Li,Rui Liu,Qifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种统一的图像和视频创作模型，利用稠密视觉监督显著提升了结构保持与编辑的精确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前生成模型常仅基于语义条件训练，缺乏对局部结构和时序一致性的显式监督，导致在精准编辑和结构保持方面表现有限。作者旨在解决这一痛点。

Method: 将深度图和表面法线的预测任务作为图像形态去噪目标，引入稠密结构化视觉监督，并通过MMDiT骨干统一支持多个任务。同时提出Mutual Context Attention法和成对视频数据构建，结合渐进式训练策略提升模型结构保持与时序编辑能力。

Result: 单一模型在统一系统比较中取得最高总分4.15，加入稠密监督后，OpenVE Overall分数由3.98升至4.06，本地编辑分数由3.92升至4.18。

Conclusion: 面向感知的稠密视觉监督能有效向下游创作迁移结构知识，尤其提升本地编辑的精确性与结构保留能力，但不追求成为最强的稠密结构预测器。

Abstract: Unified image and video creation requires a model to follow diverse instructions while preserving identity, geometry, and temporal structure from visual context. However, semantic-only conditioning and creation-only training do not explicitly supervise the local structure needed for precise, temporally consistent editing. We therefore formulate depth and surface-normal prediction as image-form denoising targets, using these dense tasks as structured visual supervision within the same creation interface. Our framework decouples semantic interpretation from spatially aligned visual injection while sharing one multimodal diffusion transformer (MMDiT) backbone across all tasks. Mutual Context Attention (MCA), a paired-video data-construction procedure, and a progressive training curriculum then connect the learned structural cues to temporally localized editing and reference-conditioned creation. A single checkpoint obtains the highest overall score in the reported comparison of unified systems (4.15); adding dense supervision improves OpenVE Overall from 3.98 to 4.06 and Local Add from 3.92 to 4.18. These results support a deliberately bounded conclusion: perception-oriented dense supervision transfers useful structural knowledge to downstream creation, especially editing locality and preservation; we do not claim superiority as a standalone dense predictor.

</details>


### [21] [PolyComp: A Polycube-based Benchmark for Compositional 3D Spatial Reasoning in Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14741)
*Siddharth Patel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了PolyComp基准，用于考察AI模型在复杂空间推理与视觉识别方面的能力，并对当前主流大模型的表现进行了评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉推理基准大多依赖人工设计或标准任务，难以全面检验模型的构形推理能力。本工作旨在以多样化、自动生成且可验证的几何问题集对主流模型进行更细致、公正的评估。

Method: 作者设计了一个包含四大几何类别、共120个问题的基准集。每个问题要求模型判断四个选项中哪组多立方体能拼合形成目标实体，分三种图像展示方式（单图或多图），并测试GPT-5.6 Sol、Claude Fable 5、Gemini 3.1 Pro Preview的表现，统计其准确率、成本以及不同条件下的表现差异。

Result: 在360个题目中，GPT-5.6 Sol最高准确率为50%，Claude Fable 5为39.4%，Gemini 3.1 Pro Preview仅27.5%，略高于随机猜测（25%）。在几何类别之间的表现差异大于展示方式之间的差异。

Conclusion: PolyComp能有效区分当下大模型在复杂空间推理上的能力，并显示当前主流大模型在此类任务上的表现依然有限。作者公布了完整问题集，便于后续研究者重复实验与优化模型。

Abstract: We introduce PolyComp, a procedurally generated and verified benchmark that stresses visual recognition and compositional spatial reasoning. In each problem, a model must identify which of four options shows a pair of polycube components that can be combined to form a target solid. The benchmark contains 120 problems across four geometry families, and each problem has three different presentation formats using either a single image or multiple images. The random guessing baseline is 25%. Across the three presentations (360 presented problems per model), GPT-5.6 Sol with max effort attains 50.0% accuracy (95% problem-cluster CI 43.3-56.7%) at a mean cost of \$0.951 per presented problem, Claude Fable 5 with max effort attains 39.4% (33.1-46.1%) at \$0.701, and Gemini 3.1 Pro Preview with thinking level high attains 27.5% (22.8-32.5%), near the 25% random guessing baseline, at \$0.350. The observed accuracy spread across geometry families is larger than across presentation formats. We present a problem development and evaluation protocol, cost and token accounting, and release the 120 problems.

</details>


### [22] [Beyond Boundary Noise: Aggregated Aleatoric Uncertainty Fails to Capture Presence Ambiguity in 3D Lung Nodule Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14766)
*Simon Baur,Arne Schernich,Ekin Böke,Wojciech Samek,Jackie Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文系统性评估了深度学习医学影像分割中aleatoric不确定性对疾病存在模糊性的反映能力，发现主流熵基不确定性方法不足以作为临床判断工具，提出监督式模糊性分支表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习医学分割需安全部署，对不可约数据模糊性的估计尤为关键。然而，目前基于熵的不确定性度量主要反映像素级边界误差，是否能区分病变是否存在的临床模糊性，尚未明确。

Method: 在LIDC-IDRI与外部LNDb数据集上，采用四种分割架构、MC Dropout和深度集成，全面比较像素熵聚合与新增轻量级、冻结特征驱动的模糊性分支方法在病灶存在性模糊性度量中的表现。

Result: 熵值主要刻画分割边界噪声与细微画法差异，但难以准确反映病例级异议。提出的监督式模糊性分支，无论在模型、数据集还是评价指标上，均显著优于所有基于熵的传统方案，并媲美或超过特殊歧义建模方法。深入分析还发现分割主干特征能够编码病例模糊性。

Conclusion: 目前医学分割常用的aleatoric不确定性方法（熵值聚合）并不能有效反映临床关心的病例级歧义，建议在安全关键应用中不应仅依赖此类评价，宜采用能挖掘特征空间模糊信息的专门分支。

Abstract: Uncertainty estimation is critical for the safe clinical deployment of deep learning in medical image segmentation, with aleatoric uncertainty theoretically designed to capture irreducible data ambiguity. However, whether entropy-based measures reflect clinically meaningful ambiguity, i.e. case-level disagreement about whether a pathology is present at all, remains poorly understood. Contrary to most prior work, which focused on pixel-wise boundary disagreement, we systematically evaluate how well aleatoric uncertainty captures presence ambiguity. Our evaluation spans 3D lung nodule segmentation across four architectures with Monte Carlo dropout and deep ensembles, on LIDC-IDRI and an external validation cohort (LNDb). We find that entropy-based uncertainty maps align with boundary noise and minor drawing variation but carry insufficient discriminative signal for presence ambiguity. In contrast, a lightweight supervised ambiguity head trained on frozen segmentation features substantially outperforms all entropy-aggregation-based baselines across architectures, metrics, and both cohorts, and matches or exceeds methods that explicitly model ambiguity under disagreement supervision (Probabilistic U-Net, Annotator-Confusion 3D-UNet). A qualitative feature-space analysis shows that presence ambiguity is already encoded in the frozen encoder features of pixel-wise-trained networks, only to be discarded by the segmentation output and its entropy aggregation. Our findings expose a fundamental mismatch between the theoretical promise of aleatoric uncertainty and its practical behavior, and suggest that practitioners should not rely on entropy-based uncertainty as a proxy for clinical ambiguity in safety-critical applications.

</details>


### [23] [NARRATE: A Multimodal Real-World Australian Driving Dataset for Human-Centred Explanations in Automated Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14767)
*Ashkan Yousefi Zadeh,Zishuo Zhu,Xiaomeng Li,Andry Rakotonirainy,Sebastien Glaser,Ronald Schroeter,Patricia Delhomme,Zahra Mehraban*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NARRATE是一个真实驾驶环境下的多模态自动驾驶数据集，收集了来自经验丰富驾驶员的多维度事件及自然语言解释，用于人性化和可信度更高的自动驾驶解释模型训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前驾驶数据集多为仿真、传感器自动生成或事后观察者注释，缺乏直接来源于司机自身对行为的解释，不利于自动驾驶系统提供让乘客信任、易于理解的决策解释。

Method: NARRATE数据集来自澳大利亚公共道路，由35名经验丰富的驾驶员和教练完成。总计包含2050个驾驶事件，采集了同步的视觉、本地化、运动轨迹和LiDAR数据。每个事件均配有车内或行车后的自由文本解释。数据集还包含动作标签、六大类及32个细分类别的情景标签，以及驾驶员解释中的情境感知（SA）注释。通过四个基准任务（SA分类、情景识别、驾驶行为分类、解释生成）评估其可训练性。

Result: 实验结果显示，基于驾驶员语言，数据集结构具备可学习性；但细粒度情景识别与自动解释生成仍具有挑战性。

Conclusion: NARRATE为自动驾驶解释模型的人性化和情境感知提供了新基准，有助于构建更易于理解、值得信赖的自动驾驶系统解释。

Abstract: Automated vehicles must explain their decisions in ways that passengers can understand, monitor, and trust. Existing language-annotated driving datasets are mostly observer-written, post-hoc, simulation-based, or generated from sensor inputs, rather than elicited from the driver performing the action. We introduce NARRATE, a multimodal real-world Australian driving dataset comprising 2,050 annotated events from 35 experienced drivers and driving instructors on public roads. Each event is grounded in synchronised visual, localisation, motion, and LiDAR streams and paired with in-vehicle and/or post-drive free-text explanations. NARRATE provides action labels, scenario-context labels spanning six high-level and 32 fine-grained categories, and span-level Situational Awareness (SA) annotations over driver explanations for Perception, Comprehension and Projection. Four benchmark tasks (SA, scenario-context, driver-action classification, and explanation generation) show that this structure is learnable from driver language, while fine-grained context recognition and explanation generation remain challenging. NARRATE paves a path towards more human-centred and domain-aware explanation models for automated driving.

</details>


### [24] [Uncertainty Identifies Difficult Samples Across Methods: A Multi-Task Study on a Heterogeneous Skin Lesion Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14768)
*Leon Koole,Jiapan Guo,Matias Valdenegro-Toro*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文探讨了皮肤病变分类器的不确定性量化（UQ）方法，比较不同UQ技术在准确率、校准性和风险覆盖率上的表现，发现Deep Ensembles在校准和不确定性分解方面表现最佳，且根据不确定性进行选择性转诊能有效减少错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 皮肤病变分类器在一些关键病例上可能自信地给出错误结果，临床上识别何时不应信任模型预测与预测本身同样重要，因此需要可靠的不确定性量化方法来指导实际应用，特别是用于筛查和转诊流程。

Method: 研究使用了来自多个ISIC来源的合并数据集，采用共享主干和两个联合训练的分支：一个二分类（良恶性）头和一个五分类诊断头。比较了五种常见的不确定性量化方法（MC Dropout、DropConnect、Flipout、Deep Ensembles、DUQ），主要考察准确率、校准性、不确定性分解和风险-覆盖关系等指标。

Result: 不同UQ方法对于难例的识别几乎没有差异（单个样本的不确定性排序高度相关，相关系数0.54~0.91），方法差异主要体现在校准和不确定性分解上，Deep Ensembles在这些指标上表现最佳。此外，通过基于不确定性挑出不确定样本进行选择性转诊，能有效去除大量错误预测。

Conclusion: 不确定性量化是皮肤病变分类决策中很有价值的工具；方法之间对病例难度的判断相似，但Deep Ensembles在校准和不确定性解释性上更优，基于不确定性可实现有意义的案例转诊，为实际临床部署提供了支持。

Abstract: Skin lesion classifiers can be confidently wrong on the cases that matter most, so knowing when a prediction should not be trusted is clinically as useful as the prediction. We study uncertainty quantification on a dataset pooled from many ISIC sources, with a shared backbone and two jointly learned heads: a binary malignant versus non-malignant head and a five-class diagnostic head. Five UQ methods (MC Dropout, DropConnect, Flipout, Deep Ensembles, DUQ) are compared on accuracy, calibration, uncertainty decomposition, and risk-coverage. Difficulty is largely method-agnostic: even methods with narrow entropy distributions rank the same samples as hard (per-sample entropy correlations of $0.54$ to $0.91$). The choice of method matters more for calibration and uncertainty decomposition, where Deep Ensembles is the clear winner, than for finding difficult cases. The ranking is also good enough that deferring the most uncertain cases removes a disproportionate share of errors, supporting uncertainty-based selective referral, evaluated here in-distribution only.

</details>


### [25] [Artificial Intelligence as a Tool for Combating Child Labour: A Real-Time Edge Vision Pipeline for Child Detection and Age Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14770)
*Mark Nowak*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究提出了基于实时计算机视觉的新型儿童劳工监测系统，通过深度学习模型识别并跟踪儿童，显著提升监测的准确性与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通过定期家访和访谈的儿童劳工监控系统严重漏检，难以及时且全面地发现实际从事劳动的儿童，因此需要更加实时、自动化的技术手段提升监测能力。

Method: 构建了包括多任务人体与人脸检测（CerberusDet框架，YOLO26x主干）、级联年龄估计（MiVOLO v2与专家儿童年龄模型）、ByteTrack追踪、ArcFace和DINOv2身份重识别，以及轨迹级数据融合等多项计算机视觉模块的实时管道，并实现了在FP8 TensorRT上的高效运行。

Result: 新系统在检测精度（mAP@0.5从0.390提升到0.683）、儿童年龄估计（平均绝对误差降至1.944年）、实时性（嵌入式硬件上超过两倍实时速度）等方面均大幅优于旧有基线；在代理视频检测出634名儿童候选者（对比前代的285）；津巴布韦农场实地测试中，通过参数调整将检测率提升了36倍，并有效减少了假阳性报告。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了深度学习驱动的实时视觉系统提升儿童劳工监测准确性和效率的巨大潜力，但也指出了训练和量化过程中的挑战，并强调了数据保护和人工校验机制的重要性。

Abstract: An estimated 138 million children remain in child labour worldwide, and the monitoring systems used by affected sectors, built on periodic household visits and interviews, systematically under-detect them. We present a real-time computer-vision pipeline, built and operated solely as a research prototype, that studies the feasibility of giving Child Labour Monitoring and Remediation Systems (CLMRS) a continuous, presence-based evidence channel. The pipeline combines a multi-task person and face detector (YOLO26x backbone in the CerberusDet framework), cascaded age estimation pairing MiVOLO v2 with a child-specialist model for ages 0-12, ByteTrack tracking, ArcFace and DINOv2 re-identification, and track-level fusion producing reviewable per-person records. The detector raises person mAP@0.5 from 0.390 to 0.683 over the previous-generation baseline; the child specialist reaches 1.944 years MAE on children-only validation, where widely used open-source stacks err by 18-23 years. FP8 TensorRT compilation yields a 1.77x speedup at +0.002 years MAE, bringing the pipeline above twice real-time on embedded hardware. On 26.8 hours of proxy video the system finds 634 unique child candidates versus 285 for its predecessor. We further report a seventeen-day unattended field pilot on a farm in Zimbabwe (38.7 million frames, six cameras) evaluated against a daily attendance register: software tuning improved detection yield 36-fold, and identity consolidation under a simultaneity veto cut over-reporting from 9.1x to 1.8-3.9x with zero proven-false merges. We document training and quantisation failures alongside successes, and the data-protection and human-in-the-loop safeguards such a system requires.

</details>

However, the lack of large, publicly available datasets with high-quality labels has limited the development of AI models for LI-RADS characterization. *Manwen Liao,Xinyu Lian,Jian Mao,Kaixu Chen,Li Luo,Jinghao Yan,Wanshui Gan,Qiao Yu,Weitian Zhang,Chunhua Shen,Guang Chen,Bo Dai,Xudong Xu,Zhaoyang Lyu*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于部分的3D生成方法难以扩展到拥有大量零件的复杂对象，因为生成详细几何时，所需的令牌数和显存消耗急剧增加，阻碍了实际应用。

Result: 方法可支持最多300个零件、序列长度达256k的对象生成。结果显示，在网格质量上，MegaParts优于现有的自回归和扩散生成基线，同时令牌压缩不仅提升了扩展性，也提高了生成几何的保真度。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的令牌高效建模为大规模部分感知3D生成提供了优于扩散方法的新范式，兼具结构可控性和高质量。

Abstract: Part-aware 3D object generation is essential for graphics applications such as controllable modeling, editing, and articulation, where objects are represented as coherent assemblies of semantic parts. However, existing part-aware generation methods, do not scale well to highly complex objects. As the number of parts increases, generating detailed geometry becomes prohibitively expensive in token length and memory. We introduce MegaParts, a scalable autoregressive 3D generation framework to address this challenge by combining structured sequence modeling with a token-efficient vector-quantized shape tokenizer. Our tokenizer learns discrete latent representations for part-level geometry by minimizing token usage subject to high-fidelity reconstruction, enabling adaptive-length tokenization based on geometric complexity. On top of this compact representation, we train a large language model to generate object bounding boxes, part bounding boxes, and part shape tokens within a unified structured sequence. Combined with efficient long-context training strategy, our token-efficient formulation scales to objects with up to 300 parts and sequence lengths up to 256k tokens. This substantially extends the scale of part-aware 3D generation while preserving compositional structure and enabling fine-grained part-level control. Our method achieves higher mesh quality than baseline autoregressive and diffusion models, showing that compressed discrete part tokens improve not only scalability but also the achievable fidelity of generated geometry. These results suggest that LLM native token-efficient autoregressive modeling is a compelling alternative to diffusion for large-scale part-aware 3D generation. The project page is available at https://expmaster.github.io/megaparts_webpage.

</details>


### [28] [Qwen-Video-Edit: Instruction-Based Video Editing by Repurposing an Image Editing Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14790)
*Yunpeng Bai,Yossi Gandelsman,Michaël Gharbi,Qixing Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于指令的视频编辑新方法，通过利用强大的图像编辑器直接操作视频VAE潜变量，无需对视频生成模型进行大幅改造，实现高效的视频编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前主流的视频编辑需基于大规模视频生成模型，训练和适配成本高昂。作者希望探索低成本、高效率地将成熟图像编辑能力迁移到视频编辑，简化流程并提高泛化能力。

Method: 以Qwen-Image-Edit为核心，作者将视频VAE潜变量帧按瓷砖排列为虚拟大图像，复用图像编辑器的位置编码，并用轻量级投影层连接两类潜空间。系统初始化后将整段视频嵌入为图像，并在Ditto-1M的编辑三元组任务上微调，可选用Wan~2.2去噪提升时间连贯性。

Conclusion: 复杂的视频潜变量训练虽投入很多，但单帧潜变量与图像空间接近，因此成熟的图像编辑先验可以借助极小的适配，用于高效视频编辑，极大降低成本。

Abstract: Instruction-based video editing is commonly built on video-pretrained generative backbones: a video diffusion transformer is adapted, at considerable cost, to condition on a source video and an editing instruction. In this report we explore a different route and show that a strong instruction-based image editing model can edit videos by operating directly on video-VAE latents. Starting from Qwen-Image-Edit, we arrange the latent frames of a Wan~2.1 video VAE as tiles of one large virtual image, reuse the editor's image positional encoding for every tile, and bridge the two latent spaces with a pair of lightweight input/output projections warm-started from the editor's own patchify and unpatchify layers, so that at initialization a (static) video is embedded exactly as an image the model already understands. The whole system is then fine-tuned on the public Ditto-1M editing triplets, and a few denoising steps of Wan~2.2 serve as an optional temporal enhancer. We motivate the design with a chain of zero-training observations: the stock image editor already edits a video presented as a contact sheet; it is indifferent to whether the sheet's tokens come from one joint encode or from per-frame encodes stitched in latent space; and it even edits genuine video latents zero-shot to a clearly recognizable degree, leaving fine-tuning only a fidelity gap to close. Our results suggest that, despite the large investment in training video latent spaces, per-frame video latents remain close enough to the image domain that mature image editing priors transfer with minimal adaptation. Project Page: https://yunpeng1998.github.io/Qwen-Video-Edit-Page; Code: https://github.com/yunpeng1998/Qwen-Video-Edit; Model: https://huggingface.co/yunpeng1998/Qwen-Video-Edit.

</details>


### [29] [Zero-Shot Adaptation of Medical Vision Foundation Models for High-Frequency Micro-Ultrasound Prostate Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14796)
*Ayusha Abbas,Saram Abbas,Kabita Adhikari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本论文提出了一种无需训练数据的零样本前列腺超声分割方案MedSAM，并在微超声数据集上显著提升了分割准确性和一致性，有望解决当前手工分割不一致、传统算法泛化差等难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前列腺癌早筛和治疗依赖高精度前列腺边界分割，但常规及微超声技术均存在边界模糊或噪声干扰导致分割不准确的问题。现有有监督分割方法成本高、泛化性差，迫切需要一种无需标注或训练数据、能直接推广的分割方法。

Method: 本研究提出零样本分割流程：基于预训练的MedSAM模型定位无标注超声图像后，结合CLAHE增强、二值膨胀和傅里叶平滑等后处理，多策略空间提示（框/点）输入下评估分割效果。

Result: 在75例、2621幅微超声前列腺图像上，20例测试集上Dice从0.749提升至0.865，HD95从217.2降至120.1像素，平均重叠率与3组非专家一致显著（p>0.19）且分割一致性提升38-52%。点-点击提示方式因噪声等失败，仅需近似框即可临床部署。

Conclusion: MedSAM零样本分割方案显著提升了微超声下前列腺自动分割效果，无需额外数据和标注，适合不同设备、场景，方便临床落地，有助于前列腺癌筛查和治疗。

Abstract: Prostate cancer claims a life every 80 seconds. Early detection is needed to prevent disease progression, and both PSA density calculation and biopsy decisions rely on knowing the exact boundary of the gland. Conventional ultrasound at 6-12 MHz blurs this boundary, missing one in three high-risk cancers. Micro-ultrasound (29 MHz) improves resolution threefold but introduces dense acoustic speckle that obscures the outer wall; given the same image, two clinicians draw outlines differing by over 10% in area. Supervised methods are costly and generalise poorly across scanners. Can a foundation model segment the prostate with no training data?
  We present the first zero-shot pipeline for this modality: MedSAM, pre-trained on over 1.5 million medical images, localises the prostate; we then apply CLAHE to sharpen the outer wall, binary dilation to recover missed pixels, and Fourier smoothing (4 modes, s=1.05) to refine the boundary. MedSAM requires a spatial prompt, so we evaluate bounding-box and point-click strategies across 75 patients of the Micro-Ultrasound Prostate Segmentation dataset (2,621 slices).
  On the 20-patient held-out test set, the pipeline reduces mean boundary-distance error by 45% (Dice 0.749+/-0.043 to 0.865+/-0.029; HD95 217.2+/-36.9 to 120.1+/-26.1 px), reaching Dice 0.859 across the cohort. Its mean overlap shows no significant difference from the three non-expert rater groups (p>0.19), while segmenting 38-52% more consistently (lower inter-patient standard deviation). Point-click prompts fail regardless of placement (best Dice=0.350), because speckle gives no stable local contrast. Only an approximate bounding box is required, so any clinic can deploy it without data collection, annotation, or retraining.

</details>


### [30] [Where the Cost Falls: A Deployment-Aware Adoption Order for Stability Enhancements to Cycle-Consistent Adversarial Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14811)
*Rowan Hussein,Mohamed Ouf*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文分析了循环一致对抗网络（如CycleGAN）在无配对图像翻译中的常见失败，并系统性地比较了四种主流改进方法的影响和适用场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CycleGAN等无配对图像翻译方法面临训练不稳定（震荡或崩溃）、细节丢失、判别器无法发现局部伪影等问题。业界虽有多种增强方法，但往往仅从输出质量对比，缺乏对资源消耗（如计算和延迟）带来的实际部署对比，导致不知道在有算力或内存限制下应优先采用哪种改进。

Method: 分析和整合四种主流增强方法：带梯度惩罚的Wasserstein损失、循环重构中的VGG19感知损失、多尺度判别器和自注意力模块。逐项引入并与轻调基线（马-斑马数据集）对比，给出各自作用、针对的失败模式及集成方式。输出FIDs、KIDs等指标并用可视化样本展示改进成效。讨论了每种方法对部署后资源占用的影响，尤其是自注意力的显著内存增长。

Result: 三种增强（Wasserstein损失、VGG19感知损失、多尺度判别器）仅影响训练过程，不影响最终部署模型；而自注意力模块在生成器上线后显著增加内存开销，因此在资源受限时应被优先砍掉。集成所有增强后，FID/KID等指标获得最优，失败模式（如崩溃或重构伪影）被有效缓解。

Conclusion: 各增强方法不仅影响质量，也决定了训练和部署的资源消耗。实际应用时，团队应根据算力和部署需求优先选择影响训练过程而不是上线生成器的增强手段。自注意力虽有效但代价高，资源有限时可延后采用。

Abstract: Teams that adopt cycle-consistent adversarial networks for unpaired image-to-image translation meet the same obstacles: adversarial training oscillates or collapses, cycle consistency preserves coarse layout while finer texture drifts, and a single discriminator judging global realism misses local artifacts. Four enhancements address these failures, and they are usually compared on output quality alone. We show that they also divide sharply by where their cost falls, and that this division, which follows from the architecture and not from any particular run, yields an adoption order for teams under a compute or latency budget. A Wasserstein objective with gradient penalty, a VGG19 perceptual loss on the cycle reconstruction, and multi-scale discriminators change training only, so a team can adopt or drop them without altering what ships. Self-attention alone persists into the deployed generator, with memory growing as the square of the feature-map size, which makes it the one component a resource-constrained team should defer. We integrate all four onto a lightly tuned baseline for horse-to-zebra translation, introduced one at a time on a fixed control and then combined, and for each we give the failure mode it targets and how it integrates. We document the collapse and reconstruction-artifact modes the baseline produced, report what visual inspection of saved samples showed for each variant, and report Fréchet Inception Distance and Kernel Inception Distance for the combined model. We specify the protocol still needed, covering the individual variants, perceptual similarity, and downstream segmentation, to rank these enhancements on measured evidence.

</details>


### [31] [OvDSGG: End-to-End Open-Vocabulary Dynamic Scene Graph Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14835)
*John Helsby,Yi Yang,Bodo Rosenhahn,Michael Ying Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了OvDSGG，这是首个端到端、开放词汇动态场景图生成（DSGG）框架，显著提升了开放词汇场景图生成的能力，对罕见概念具备更鲁棒的识别能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的DSGG方法仅限于固定训练词表（闭集），难以处理实际中出现的新、罕见概念，且继承大语言模型的开放词汇方法训练和推理成本高昂。

Method: OvDSGG结合了开放词汇空间和时序主干网络设计，提出三元组特征提取模块用于串联二者；引入视觉-语言对齐模块，在联合特征空间自适应学习决策边界，无需昂贵的知识蒸馏过程。

Result: OvDSGG在新建立的开放词汇DSGG基准（基于Action Genome）上，所有指标均大幅领先现有开放词汇方法，零样本召回率提升10-20个百分点，在闭集任务上表现亦与SOTA相当。

Conclusion: OvDSGG有效解决了开放词汇下DSGG的难题，兼顾效率和性能，为视频理解任务的实际应用带来了更高的灵活性和适应性。

Abstract: Dynamic scene graphs (DSGs) capture spatio-temporal interactions across videos as $\langle$subject, predicate, object$\rangle$ triplets, and underpin downstream tasks such as video captioning, video question answering, and action analysis. However, end-to-end dynamic scene graph generation (DSGG) methods are closed-set: they recognize only objects and predicates from a fixed training vocabulary and struggle with the long-tailed distribution of rare concepts, severely limiting their real-world applicability. Existing open-vocabulary models typically inherit pretrained large language models, resulting in multi-stage training and inference with substantial cost. We introduce OvDSGG, the first end-to-end framework for open-vocabulary DSGG. OvDSGG builds on top of an open-vocabulary Spatial Backbone and a Temporal Backbone; we further propose a Triplet Feature Extraction Module that bridges them, and a Visual-Language Alignment Module that preserves open-vocabulary recognition by learning an adaptive decision boundary in the joint visual-language feature space, without expensive knowledge distillation in existing methods. We further introduce a rigorous open-vocabulary DSGG benchmark adapted from Action Genome, with disjoint Base/Novel splits for both objects and predicates. OvDSGG significantly outperforms open-vocabulary baselines across all metrics, with zero-shot Recall@$K$ scores 10.0--20.4 percentage point higher than the next-best baseline, while on closed-set DSGG remaining competitive with state-of-the-art models. Code and benchmark are publicly available at https://github.com/jhelsby/OvDSGG/.

</details>


### [32] [Zero-MELO: Test-Time Evidence Calibration with Multimodal LLMs for Zero-Shot Micro-Gesture Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14854)
*Chengyan Wang,Hanliang Xie,Yueyi Yang,Haoyu Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的测试时证据校准框架，显著提升了多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在微手势识别（MGR）中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态大语言模型虽然在一般视频理解任务上表现优异，但在细粒度和以动态为中心的微手势识别任务上效果有限；而微手势对隐含情感分析十分关键，这种不足亟需解决。

Method: 作者提出一种基于树搜索的机制，逐步获取局部、细致的视觉证据，引入测试时校准模块以缓解分数偏差，并利用多线索融合模块综合多路证据信息增强最终预测，旨在提升MLLMs在MGR上的表现。

Result: 在iMiGUE和MA-52数据集上，所提框架分别获得26.84%和22.10%的均类准确率，显著优于Qwen2.5-VL基线（分别为16.15%和10.20%）。

Conclusion: 经过证据校准和多线索融合等方法，多模态大语言模型在微手势识别任务上获得了显著提升，验证了方法的有效性。

Abstract: While Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) excel in general video understanding, their capability in fine-grained and motion-centric tasks remains limited. This limitation is particularly critical in micro-gesture recognition (MGR), where micro-gestures (MGs) - subtle, short-duration, and spatially localized human movements - serve as key discriminative signals for implicit affective analysis, yet are easily neglected following common prompting practices. Although MGR has been intensively studied by many discriminative approaches, the use of MLLMs for MGR is underexplored, with notably poor performance. We hypothesize that the motion-sensitive representation ability of MLLMs is constrained by their inherent single-pass forward inference, which can be substantially enhanced through carefully designed test-time guidance. Motivated by this, building on our prior findings regarding temporal insensitivity in Video LLMs, we diagnose zero-shot MGR errors in the Negative Log-Likelihood (NLL) space. We observe that MLLMs suffer from two bottlenecks: 1) insufficient localized evidence and 2) severe score biases driven by language and motion-agnostic appearances. Thus, we propose a novel test-time evidence calibration framework that improves both reasoning details and prediction reliability. Specifically, we introduce a tree search mechanism to progressively acquire localized, fine-grained visual evidence, coupled with a test-time calibration module to mitigate score biases. The multi-cue fusion module then integrates evidence from multiple cues without relying on a single cue for final prediction. Our framework achieves mean-class accuracies of 26.84\% on iMiGUE and 22.10\% on MA-52, significantly outperforming the Qwen2.5-VL baseline, which produces 16.15\% and 10.20\%, respectively. The code will be available at https://zero-melo.github.io/Zero-MELO.

</details>


### [33] [Beam-Wise Statistical Background Subtraction for Static Roadside LiDAR: A Cross-Sensor Benchmark Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14868)
*Alexander Baumann,Marcel Vosshans,Thao Dang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文聚焦于使用基于光束的统计方法进行道路边LiDAR的背景消除，并提供了系统性的对比评测与公开数据集，验证了其高效性与可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管背景消除对于LiDAR感知至关重要，目前针对静态路边LiDAR的系统性多传感器评测和可复现性研究仍缺乏，对实际部署存在障碍。

Method: 将背景建模视为逐光束的时序建模问题，采用多种统计策略捕捉主导及多模态背景结构，并结合角度和三维空间域的空间滤波。同时，提出了新的HighwayScene多LiDAR数据集，并对CoopScenes数据集进行了丰富注释。

Result: 在多场景和多种LiDAR技术下，基于光束的统计建模展现出很强的鲁棒性和可迁移性；与空间一致性滤波联合使用可以大幅提升精度，同时保持高召回率和实时性能。

Conclusion: 光束级统计建模结合空间滤波，为静态LiDAR背景消除提供了高效且可移植的解决方案；公开的数据和实现能够促进后续系统性研究与应用。

Abstract: Background subtraction is a key preprocessing step for infrastructure-based LiDAR perception, enabling efficient isolation of dynamic traffic participants without semantic annotations. However, systematic cross-sensor evaluations and reproducible studies for static roadside LiDAR are missing. This paper presents a comparative benchmark of beam-wise statistical background subtraction for statically mounted LiDAR sensors. We formulate background estimation as a per-beam temporal modeling problem and investigate complementary statistical strategies that capture dominant as well as multi-modal background structures, combined with spatial filtering in the angular and 3D domain. To enable reproducible evaluation, we introduce HighwayScene, a new multi-LiDAR dataset recorded in a static roadside setup, and extend the public CoopScenes dataset with static/dynamic point-wise annotations. Across multiple scenes and heterogeneous sensing technologies, we demonstrate that beam-wise statistical modeling provides a robust and transferable solution. Combining lightweight per-beam models with spatial consistency filtering substantially improves precision while maintaining high recall and real-time capability. All datasets, annotations, and implementations are publicly released.

</details>


### [34] [SpIn-ViT: Designing a Sparsity-Induced Vision Transformer That Is Mechanistically Interpretable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14922)
*Philip H. Lee,Parth Padalkar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SpIn-ViT框架，将ViT与改进的稀疏自编码器（SAE）端到端联合训练，大幅提升了分类准确率和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有将SAE用于ViT的可解释性研究大多为事后分析，即在ViT已冻结参数后训练SAE，导致学习到的特征未必与下游分类任务对齐，因此解释性与模型表现之间存在瓶颈。

Method: 提出SpIn-ViT，通过端到端地联合训练预训练的ViT和改进版SAE，将稀疏补丁级表示与图像分类任务直接对齐，并学习具有语义一致性的神经元激活特征，能够定位图像中的重要区域。对比分析包含量化解释性指标、AI和人工评估等多维度。

Result: 在九个图像分类基准测试中，SpIn-ViT比最优事后SAE方法取得8.84%的平均分类准确率提升，AI解释性得分提升近4倍，人工评价得分提升2倍以上。此外，用SAE神经元还可提取出易于解释的符号规则，较之现有方法规则数减少58.8%，准确率高5.97%。

Conclusion: SpIn-ViT显著提升了ViT的可解释性和分类性能，为神经网络表征的可解释分解及更高效的神经符号模型建立奠定了基础。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability has recently expanded to Vision Transformers (ViTs), with Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) increasingly used as post-hoc tools to decompose internal representations into sparse and more interpretable features. However, because post-hoc SAEs are trained on frozen representations after the ViT has already been optimized, their latent features are not directly aligned with the downstream classification objective. We introduce SpIn-ViT, a framework that jointly trains a pretrained ViT and a modified SAE end-to-end, directly aligning sparse patch-level representations with image classification. SpIn-ViT learns semantically coherent neuron activations that localize meaningful image regions while maintaining competitive predictive performance. We evaluate SpIn-ViT across nine image-classification benchmarks using classification accuracy, quantitative interpretability metrics, AI-based and Human evaluations. Compared with the previous state-of-the-art post-hoc SAE method, SpIn-ViT achieves 8.84% higher average classification accuracy, an AI-based interpretability score nearly four times as high, and a human-evaluation score more than twice as high. We further extract interpretable rule-sets using the SAE neurons to create neurosymbolic models which achieve 5.97% higher average classification accuracy while requiring a 58.8\% smaller rule-set than the neurosymbolic models created from the SOTA post-hoc SAE method.

</details>


### [35] [PaSTel: Anchoring Histology in Spatial Transcriptomics via Multi-Scale Hierarchical Bio-Prior Contrastive Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14924)
*Azim Dehghani Amirabad,Junchao Zhu,Pushpak Pati,Walid Abdelmoula,Tommaso Mansi,Rui Liao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PaSTel利用多尺度生物先验知识对空间转录组学数据进行多模态预训练，在多个下游任务中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间转录组学需要结合组织形态和基因表达信息，但现有多模态方法易被管家基因主导，且忽略空间依赖性，导致特征表达能力受限。

Method: 提出PaSTel框架：1）用TF-IDF筛选空间敏感基因，2）通过KEGG通路编码全局语义，3）用空间聚类模拟中尺度组织结构，三级集成生物先验提升表示力。

Result: PaSTel在多项空间转录组学下游任务中，表现优于目前主流的视觉和视觉-组学编码器。

Conclusion: 融合多尺度生物先验的方法能有效增强空间转录组学的多模态表示质量，具备更强泛化与迁移能力。

Abstract: Spatial transcriptomics (ST) links tissue morphology with molecular programs, motivating multimodal pretraining methods that align histology images with gene expression. However, existing approaches suffer from two key limitations: spatially informative gene selection is often dominated by ubiquitous housekeeping genes, leading to weakly discriminative representations, and independent spot-patch alignment fails to capture spatial dependencies that are critical for tissue organization. To address these challenges, we introduce PaSTel, a hierarchical multimodal pretraining framework that integrates biological priors at three levels. At the spot level, TF-IDF reweighting is used to identify spatially informative genes; at the functional level, curated KEGG pathways serve as anchors for encoding global biological semantics; and at the regional level, spatial clustering aggregates neighboring spots to model meso-scale tissue structure. Across multiple downstream tasks, PaSTel consistently outperforms existing vision and vision-omics encoders, demonstrating that incorporating multiscale biological priors yields more informative and transferable representations for spatial transcriptomics.

</details>


### [36] [Looks Can be Deceiving: Annotator and Reviewer Performance Across Imagery Sources in Crowd-Sourced Aerial Damage Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14942)
*Thomas Manzini,Priyankari Perali,Raisa Karnik,Stephen Johnson,Robin R. Murphy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次对多源遥感影像（无人机、有人机、卫星）中人工标注和审核流程进行了实证分析，揭示了不同影像源下标注一致性的问题，并提出了多源数据集优化建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前绝大多数航空遥感数据集仅使用单一影像源，导致尚无有效实践用于大规模多源数据集的人力分配，尤其是如何高效保证标注质量尚未可知。

Method: 作者基于9次灾后建筑受损评估数据，分析了近2万栋无人机、2万余栋有人机与3万多栋卫星影像中由187名标注员标注的建筑物标签，经历单人审核和委员会共识两轮质控，并统计各轮修改比例。

Result: 发现从高分辨率（有人机）到低分辨率（卫星）影像，最终委员会对初始标注的修正率显著升高（如卫星高达36.95%），单人复核虽可降低修正率但效果有限，分辨率越低残留分歧越大。

Conclusion: 作者认为单一、均匀的复核分配无法解决低分辨率影像一致性差的问题，建议未来多源数据集应采用自适应任务分配与预算感知质控等更精细策略以提升数据一致性。

Abstract: This paper presents the first known empirical investigation of annotator and reviewer performance across multi-source remotely sensed imagery, evaluating human labeling across drone, crewed aviation, and satellite views. Because existing aerial imagery datasets rely predominantly on single-source imagery, there is no currently established state of practice for efficiently allocating human labor to curate large-scale, multi-source aerial datasets. This work addresses this limitation by analyzing annotator and reviewer performance within a post-disaster building damage assessment dataset of 9 disasters, where 20041 buildings in drone, 20695 buildings in crewed aviation, and 33392 buildings in satellite imagery were labeled. These labels, provided by 187 annotators, were then refined through two successive quality-control stages: a single-reviewer pass followed by a consensus-committee review. Our analysis reveals two findings that raise questions for standard crowd-sourcing practices. First, initial annotations were revised by the final committee at rates that rise steeply from higher- to lower-resolution sources (25.27% for crewed aviation and 36.95% for satellite), with the same ordering at every observed workflow stage. Second, a single individual review reduced but did not resolve this disagreement: after review, the committee still revised 6.85% of drone, 14.05% of crewed, and 20.86% of satellite labels. These observations suggest that, in workflows like this one, uniform review allocation leaves the most residual disagreement in lower-resolution imagery.
