PixRestore performs flow matching directly on patchified pixels, preserving fine-grained details while keeping the token sequence tractable. To adapt to different degradations, PixRestore learns to predict the reliability of layer features using LQ--HQ DINO feature similarity. Features from more reliable layers are fused as dense conditioning, while less reliable layers receive stronger HQ-feature supervision to encourage degradation removal. We train PixRestore on a large-scale corpus of diverse scenes and degradations, and further finetune it into a one-step generator using DINO-based adversarial objectives for efficient inference. Experiments on public benchmarks and real-world test sets show that, with only about 50M parameters and single-step inference, PixRestore achieves the best overall fidelity, perceptual quality, and robustness to degradations among competing UIR models while being far more efficient. Larger PixRestore variants can further boost performance, demonstrating the scalability of our pixel-space design. Code and the curated benchmark can be found at https://github.com/csslc/PixRestore.

</details>


### [213] [Diagnosing Dense Same-Class Attribute Misbinding in Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16805)
*Yuanzhi Xu,Qian Gao,Jun Fan,Guohui Ding,Zhenyu Yang,Yuteng Xiao,Sixue Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了多实体同类属性混绑（Dense Same-Class Attribute Misbinding, DSCAM）的问题，并提出了专门的评测基准InstaBind-Lite，以细致检测大规模视觉-语言模型在物体/属性识别中的错误归属现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大模型在拥挤场景下虽能识别出多个同类物体及其属性，但常常将属性错误地分配给了相邻同类个体。现有VQA指标和hallucination指标无法精确展现这种错误类型，因此需要专门的方法和数据集加以识别和度量。

Method: 作者设计了InstaBind-Lite基准，包括524张图、529组3-6个同类目标，总1773个框定实例，设置分级问题，共9580个可判定问题，并给出实例归属和属性混绑的专门标注及评价指标。此外，通过比较七个主流大模型的表现，分析不同模型的混绑率和错误类别分布。

Result: 开源模型平均混绑率为19.84%，API商用模型为7.55%。其中超80%的错误源于相邻个体的转移。特定的本体定位和实例优先干预对某些模型有效，但无法通用根治。常规的总体准确率遗漏了此类错误。

Conclusion: InstaBind-Lite揭示了主流大模型在同类多目标场景下对于属性归属的关键盲点，为以往难以察觉的错误提供了可溯源的评测分类，是常规基准所不具备的可靠性测试维度。推动后续模型关注“归属性”，而不只是“可见性”。

Abstract: Large vision-language models can recognize the objects and attributes in a crowded scene yet assign an attribute to the wrong same-class instance. Generic visual-question-answering accuracy marks the response as wrong, while object-hallucination metrics may regard both the object and attribute as image-supported; neither reveals the transfer. This study formalizes this blind spot as Dense Same-Class Attribute Misbinding (DSCAM) and presents InstaBind-Lite, a controlled benchmark that makes it directly measurable. Its 524 images contain 529 curated groups of 3-6 same-class entities, 1773 boxed instances, ordered neighbors, distinguishable color-like attributes, and four complementary question levels, yielding 9580 deterministically evaluated questions. Unlike existing protocols, source-instance annotations separate unsupported generation and recognition failure from an attribute copied from another visible entity. Binding-specific metrics further quantify transfer frequency, adjacency, ordinal distance, and intervention effects. Across five open-source and two commercial/API models, the open-source systems average 19.84% Misbinding Rate and the API systems 7.55%; these errors are hidden by aggregate accuracy. Among identifiable transfers, 80.70% and 81.51%, respectively, originate from adjacent instances. Localization and instance-first interventions help selected models but are not universal remedies. InstaBind-Lite therefore turns previously undifferentiated wrong answers into source-identifiable failure categories and tests a reliability dimension that conventional benchmarks cannot determine: whether a model knows not only what is visible, but which instance owns each attribute.

</details>


### [214] [Unsupervised Learning of Cell Instances with Generative Routing Pyramids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16810)
*Ziwen Liu,Martin Weigert*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需人工标注、基于无监督学习的细胞实例分割与表型分类方法，利用粗到细的路由金字塔架构实现像素与稀疏潜变量关联，兼顾分割和表征性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 显微图像分析中的细胞分割常需人工标注、分割与后续表征分开处理，既费时又难以充分利用数据。因此，作者希望通过无监督方法统一分割和表征，并减少标注需求。

Method: 作者提出一种无监督、粗到细的路由金字塔方法，将像素与稀疏空间潜变量关联，既可输出实例分割掩码，也能用潜变量对细胞形态特征建模，并适用于表征和产生不同扰动下的细胞表型。

Result: 该方法在多种细胞形态和显微成像方式下的实例分割上达到有竞争力的结果，且能实现细胞表型的生成建模。代码和模型已开源。

Conclusion: 此工作为无标签显微图像细胞分割和表征提供了新思路，提升了处理自动化和泛化能力，对表型研究具有应用前景。

Abstract: Identifying and representing object instances such as cells or nuclei is a common task in microscopy image analysis. Established machine learning workflows typically use supervised detection or segmentation followed by feature extraction or classification, which requires manual annotations and treats instance segmentation and cell representation as separate stages. We describe a new unsupervised method for cell instance segmentation and phenotypic classification from unlabeled microscopy images. Our method is based on reconstructing each image using a coarse-to-fine routing pyramid that associates pixels with spatially sparse latent sources. The resulting pixel-to-latent associations yield instance masks, while the source latents encode cell morphology. We demonstrate competitive performance in instance segmentation across diverse cell morphologies and imaging modalities, as well as generative modeling of cellular phenotypes under perturbations. Source code and checkpoints are available at https://github.com/weigertlab/routing-pyramids.

</details>


### [215] [Unlocking the Potential of Image Editing via Concept Scaling and Dense Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16812)
*Long Cui,Xiaoqian Liu,Qi Qin,Yi Xin,Tao Lin,Jianguo Li,Linfeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本论文提出了新的图像编辑数据集与训练方式，显著提升了编辑细粒度与训练效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像编辑框架多沿用文本到图像扩散模型的训练范式，但在编辑任务上面临编辑概念粒度不足与稀疏监督信号导致的训练效率低下问题。

Method: 作者建立了包含1000多个细致编辑概念的分层分类体系，并通过改进的数据合成框架，构建了包含1200万高质量编辑对的大规模数据集ConceptEdit-12M。同时，提出了密集监督训练策略，通过在单个图片对中合成多个互不干扰的编辑概念，为模型提供更丰富的学习信号。

Result: 该方法有效缓解了生成数据分布坍塌并保证数据高度保真。实验结果显示，新策略下的模型在性能与训练效率上均显著优于先前方法。

Conclusion: 论文提出的库驱动、分层细粒度数据集和密集监督训练策略，可极大提升图像编辑扩散模型的能力，并通过ConceptEdit-Bench套件从多维度评估模型实际场景下的表现。

Abstract: Existing image editing frameworks predominantly follow the training paradigm of text-to-image diffusion models. However, extending this paradigm to image editing highlights two inherent discrepancies, specifically, the insufficient attention to edit concept granularity and the training inefficiency caused by sparse supervision signals. To address these issues, we establish a comprehensive hierarchical taxonomy featuring over 1,000 fine-grained edit concepts and build ConceptEdit-12M, a massive dataset of 12 million high-quality editing pairs via an improved synthesis framework. This library-driven approach effectively rectifies the distribution collapse of generated data while ensuring high data fidelity. Furthermore, we propose a dense supervision training strategy that synthesizes multiple non-interfering concepts into single image pairs. By providing richer learning signals, this strategy significantly enhances both training efficiency and overall model performance. Training results validate our strategy, significantly outperforming prior works. Finally, we present ConceptEdit-Bench, a granular evaluation suite designed to diagnose model capabilities across a vast array of real-world scenarios.

</details>


### [216] [Can Unsupervised Methods Outperform Supervised Deep Learning When Ground Truth Is Sparse? A Case Study of Bronchovascular Bundle Segmentation in Low-Dose CT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16855)
*Anna Mrukwa,Marek Socha,Aleksandra Suwalska,Agata Durawa,Malgorzata Jelitto,Katarzyna Dziadziuszko,Edyta Szurowska,Pawel Bozek,Michal Marczyk,Witold Rzyman,Rafal Dziadziuszko,Joanna Polanska*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种新方法RONALD，可有效分割肺部支气管血管束，提高低剂量CT中早期肺结节的检测率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肺癌由于早期诊断较难，死亡率居高不下。支气管血管束与肺结节常相连，难以区分，影响结节检测准确性。因此需开发新的分割方法协助早期发现。

Method: 基于广泛应用的低剂量CT数据集（如DLCS、Pomeranian项目），提出一条多阶段分割流程RONALD：先对肺、肺叶、纵隔做预处理分割，再分别分割血管与支气管树，并输出二值掩码。

Result: 在DLCS数据集上，结节保留率由最高93.98%提升至100%；在Pomeranian数据集由最高83.16%提升至99.92%。比其他方法表现更优。

Conclusion: 所提方法可显著提升超早期肺结节的检测能力，有助于提高肺癌筛查的诊断潜力。

Abstract: Background
  Lung cancer remains the deadliest cancer worldwide because it is often diagnosed too late. Effective treatment depends on detection at an early screening stage. However, the growing number of patients and the limited number of radiologists lead to prolonged diagnostic waiting times. In very early stage lung cancer, nodule visibility is further reduced by adjacent blood vessels and airway walls, because nodules are often connected to or supplied by these structures. Task-specific analysis of the bronchovascular bundle is therefore important for efficient nodule detection, and its removal can increase the diagnostic potential of lung cancer screening.
  Materials and Methods
  To assess the efficacy of the proposed method, we used series from widely utilized LDCT datasets, including the Duke Lung Cancer Screening (DLCS) dataset and the Pilot Pomeranian Lung Cancer Screening Program. The proposed bronchovascular bundle segmentation pipeline, RONALD, operates on computed tomography images and returns binary masks of vessels and bronchi located in the lung parenchyma. The method includes a preprocessing stage with lung, lobe, and mediastinum segmentation, followed by separate vessel and bronchial tree segmentation.
  Results
  The proposed pipeline segmented the bronchovascular bundle in low-dose computed tomography scans while improving nodule retention compared with other segmentation methods: from 93.98% and 90.36% to 100% in DLCS, and from 83.16% and 62.36% to 99.92% in the Pomeranian dataset.
  Conclusion
  The resulting segmentations can improve lung nodule detection in the very early stages of lung cancer.

</details>


### [217] [HarnessEval-W: Agentifying the Evaluation of Visual Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16859)
*Weiliang Chen,Haowen Sun,Jun Gao,Jiawei Chi,Hanyang Wang,Qiyu Dai,Yihao Li,Hao Li,Jingnan Gao,Yi-Hsin Hung,Xingzhuo Guo,Shangchen Miao,Zhiyuan Shi,Xiang Li,Fengrui Tian,Weihua Du,Ziqi Huang,Shenyuan Gao,Siqiao Huang,Mingyu Liu,Yifei Li,Shizun Wang,Xi Wang,Tianqi Zhang,Xue Luo,Xiyin Ren,Jinshan Ren,Xiaoyang Shen,Xiaobo Hu,Zhiyang Dou,Mingyu Ding,Yichao Yan,Xinchao Wang,Yizhou Wang,Shilong Liu,Wenzhao Zheng,Yueqi Duan,Yuan Gong,Ziwei Liu,Ming-Yu Liu,Jialong Wu,Jiangran Lyu,Fangfu Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新型的世界模型评测基准HarnessEval-W，它不只给出分数，而是将每次评测的推理链条透明化，以证据树形式展现，从而增加评测可信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型基准仅以分数评价，缺乏评测过程的透明推理链，难以发现和解释物理、因果等方面的错误。

Method: 借鉴LLM生态中的harness范式，设计了分层代理系统：主代理分解评测任务，生成具备专业工具的子代理分别负责子问题，各自推理后由主代理整合证据链，生成最终判断。

Result: 在18个代表性世界模型的330个评测案例中，HarnessEval-W的评判高度贴合人类偏好，且能细致诊断每次生成的有效性。

Conclusion: HarnessEval-W让世界模型评测具备可查证、可解释的推理证据链，提高了评测透明度和公信力，且已开源供社区拓展。

Abstract: A benchmark should deliver more than a scalar score: what makes an evaluation trustworthy is the reasoning that justifies the score. This is especially critical for world models, where judging a rollout requires understanding whether physics, causality, and world state evolve correctly. Humans spot such violations naturally, yet no existing benchmark automates this capability: metrics are computed brute-force, leaving no reasoning chain that can be examined or verified. We introduce HarnessEval-W, an agentified evaluation pipeline that brings the harness paradigm from the LLM ecosystem to world model benchmarking. Rather than applying a fixed rubric, HarnessEval-W interprets the context of each evaluation case, decomposes the evaluation question into measurable subproblems, and spawns specialized sub-agents, each equipped with tailored context and diagnostic tools to reason over its own subproblem. The parent agent then validates the gathered evidence and summarizes it into the final verdict. This hierarchical workflow turns every evaluation into a transparent evidence tree whose complete reasoning chain justifies the result. We apply HarnessEval-W to 18 representative world models over 330 evaluation cases. Its judgments closely align with human preferences while providing verifiable, fine-grained diagnoses of every generated rollout. We open-source the full pipeline as a live benchmark and invite the broad community to contribute to grow new skills and evaluation cases as world models evolve.

</details>


### [218] [SplatGuide: Geometric Priors from 3D Gaussians for Pose-Free Novel View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16863)
*Yejun Zhang,Zihan Wang,Xu Ji,Yihao Wang,Yuxin Hou,Junyuan Fang,Juho-Matti Kilpeläinen,Arno Solin,Hamed Rezazadegan Tavakoli,Esa Rahtu,Juho Kannala*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SplatGuide方法，将3DGS重建与多视图扩散相结合，实现了无需姿态监督的高质量新视角生成，并在多个数据集上取得了最新最优结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往仅从3D重建提取单一信号（像素或特征），未充分利用可见性与其他重建信息，造成信息割裂，影响无姿态新视角合成效果。

Method: SplatGuide创新性地将单一3DGS场景重建结果复用到三方面：1）利用重建渲染图像作像素级几何约束，2）通过渲染每个高斯体素的源视角索引生成投票图，实现遮挡感知的参考视角筛选，3）重建特征通过交叉注意力机制为生成网络提供引导，三者均来自同一次前向过程。

Result: 在RealEstate10K、DL3DV、Tanks-and-Temples、Mip-NeRF 360等数据集上，SplatGuide在无姿态输入下的新视角合成精度优于现有方法。尤其是在RealEstate10K中，输入视角数量适中时，性能超过依赖真实姿态的基线方法。

Conclusion: SplatGuide有效整合3D重建的多种信号，克服了以往方法的信息割裂，实现了无需姿态的高质量新视角渲染，为无监督视角生成提供了新范式。

Abstract: Generating photorealistic novel views from unposed images requires both 3D geometric understanding and the ability to synthesize unseen content. A natural strategy combines feed-forward 3DGS reconstruction with multi-view diffusion. Yet prior pipelines extract at most one signal from the reconstruction, either pixel rendering or learned features, while none exploits per-Gaussian visibility for occlusion-aware reference selection. This *information disconnect* leaves renderable geometry, visibility cues, and learned features unused. SplatGuide closes this disconnect by reusing a single 3DGS scene across three complementary roles. Rendered images provide pixel-aligned geometric conditioning. Per-Gaussian source-view indices are rendered into a target-view voting map for occlusion-aware reference selection. Reconstruction tokens supply feature-level guidance via cross-attention. All three signals derive from the same reconstruction forward pass. Across RealEstate10K, DL3DV, Tanks-and-Temples, and Mip-NeRF 360, SplatGuide achieves state-of-the-art pose-free novel view synthesis. On RealEstate10K, with a moderate number of input views, it surpasses the ground-truth-pose baseline.

</details>


### [219] [An Empirical Study of Training Pixel-Space Text-to-Image Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16887)
*Dengyang Jiang,Ruoyi Du,Zhennan Chen,Dongyang Liu,Zanyi Wang,Mingzhe Zheng,Xiangpeng Yang,Huanqia Cai,Aiming Hao,Yuming Jiang,Peng Gao,Harry Yang,Steven Hoi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过实证研究提出了一种有效的像素空间扩散模型训练方法，使其性能达到甚至超越潜空间模型，并显著提升推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然像素空间扩散模型受到越来越多关注，但大多数工作只集中在小规模或有类别条件的设置。缺乏能在大规模情况下媲美或超越潜空间模型的像素空间训练范式。因此，作者希望找到一种切实可行的像素空间模型训练方案。

Method: 作者首先对比了像素空间与潜空间的大规模直接预训练，发现像素空间收敛更慢。由此提出'潜空间到像素空间'策略：先在潜空间快速高效预训练生成先验，然后在后期转向像素空间。接着系统性地分析了转变过程中的关键设计选择（包括权重初始化、数据成分、预测目标、解码器结构和噪声安排等），最终归纳出可行实践范式。

Result: 所提出的方法使像素空间模型在生成质量方面达到了或超过潜空间模型，并且在端到端推理速度上提升了3.18到4.75倍。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了像素空间生成模型实证洞见和实用训练指南，为未来像素空间扩散模型的研究与应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: This paper investigates an increasingly important topic in generative modeling: pixel-space diffusion models. Although numerous studies have explored this topic, most focus on small-scale or class-conditional settings. Consequently, a practical recipe for training pixel-space models that rival or exceed well-established latent-space counterparts remains elusive. Through a comprehensive empirical study, we first observe that direct large-scale pre-training in pixel space converges substantially more slowly than in latent space. This observation motivates a latent-to-pixel strategy that acquires generative priors efficiently in latent space and transitions to pixel space during post-training. We then systematically investigate the key design choices governing this transition, including weight initialization, data composition, prediction target, decoder architecture, and noise schedule, and identify a practical recipe that makes the resulting pixel-space models match or outperform their latent-space counterparts while delivering 3.18 to 4.75 times end-to-end inference speedups. We hope that our findings provide useful empirical insights and practical guidelines for future research on pixel-space generation.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [220] [Auxiliary uncertainty signals for LLM-assisted systematic review screening: a benchmark across eight Cohen drug-class reviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14551)
*Arya Rahgozar,Pouria Mortezaagha*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出用BERT+GCN辅助LLM进行系统综述的文献筛选，提升筛选效果，并探索了不同的提示词传递策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在系统综述文献筛选时，决策结果的可信度和不确定性没有很好地被量化和利用，影响实际应用效果。作者希望通过引入结构化的不确定性信息，提升LLM筛选的效率和可靠性。

Method: 设计一个辅助的BERT+GCN分类器，为LLM提供结构化不确定性信号，并通过不同的信息传递方式（如信息丰富度、选择性、时机）与LLM结合。在八个药物数据集上，通过交叉验证和多种分组策略系统测试，并与GPT-4.1-mini做跨模型实验。

Result: （1）全面信息传递能显著提升F1和WSS@95分数，代价为令牌消耗增加。（2）仅对“不确定（MAYBE）”类进行处理性价比最佳，显著提高召回率且增量成本低。（3）两步筛选设计显式证明当前LLM无法自我校正。跨模型实验显示提升效果可迁移。算法实验证明核心判据可简化为简单的logit-gap。

Conclusion: 引入辅助模型的不确定性信号、结合优化的信息传递策略，能有效提升LLM在系统综述中的筛选效率和性能，并揭示了当前LLM在自我校正方面的局限性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for title-abstract screening in systematic reviews, but their decisions lack calibrated uncertainty. We show that an auxiliary BERT+GCN classifier supplies a structured uncertainty signal that improves LLM screening efficiency, and we identify the prompt-delivery strategy that maximises the benefit-to-cost ratio.
  We evaluate five LLM prompt-delivery conditions on eight drug-class datasets from the Cohen (2006) benchmark using 3 seeds x 5-fold stratified cross-validation (600 fold-level results). A BERT+GCN model trained per fold classifies each test paper as INCLUDE, EXCLUDE, or MAYBE via two spectral tests (algebraic radical and categorical paradox). Conditions vary information content (none / label / full scores), selectivity (all papers vs. MAYBE only), and timing (proactive vs. reactive two-pass). A cross-model pilot against gpt-4.1-mini on three datasets tests cross-generation transfer.
  Three findings: (i) Full-context delivery yields significant gains in F1 (+0.011, paired Wilcoxon p=0.008) and WSS@95 (+0.050, p=0.039) at a 1.28x token-cost premium, while preserving recall. (ii) MAYBE-only routing is Pareto-optimal: highest mean recall (0.92) and AUC-ROC (0.54) at only 1.05x baseline cost -- one sixth of full-context overhead. (iii) The two-pass design escalates 22.2% +/- 8.8% of records yet never revises its decision (0% flip rate across all datasets and folds), giving decisive evidence that current instruction-tuned LLMs cannot self-triage. The cross-model pilot shows an identical +0.8% recall uplift for both LLM generations. A per-paper ablation across 20,796 observations shows the dual paradox test reduces empirically to a one-line logit-gap criterion. We release the full pipeline; the 600-run experiment replays in under one hour from cached LLM responses.

</details>


### [221] [HarmProfile: Characterizing Harmful Distributions in Frontier LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14577)
*Zhouyuan Ma,Yutao Wu,Hanxun Huang,Xiang Zheng,Xiao Liu,Yixin Cao,Zuxuan Wu,Xingjun Ma,Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了HarmProfile数据集，系统性地收集并分析了前沿大模型在安全失效时产生的有害内容，为模型安全评估提供了丰富的数据资源和新的分析视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往的安全评估主要关注模型是否会输出有害内容，却较少研究这些有害内容的具体分布和特征。由于缺乏大规模、高质量的模型失效案例集，限制了安全分析的深入。因此，作者希望填补这一空白，揭示前沿LLM在“失控”情况下真实的有害输出全貌。

Method: 作者构建了HarmProfile基准数据集，从23种属于13个不同家族的前沿大模型中，收集和验证了超过8万个模型安全失效样本，覆盖15大类、57小类有害内容，并据此定义各模型的风险分布——即风险画像。通过对模型输出的内容、严重性以及多样性进行系统化整理与分析，深入剖析大模型的安全弱点。

Result: 分析表明，即使是最先进的大模型，在大规模测试中依然会稳定地产生有害内容，并且不同模型展现出不同的风险画像。随着模型能力的提升，输出有害内容的严重程度和多样性反而上升，显示出表面对齐的模型隐藏着更深层次的危险知识。

Conclusion: HarmProfile数据集为深入理解和量化大模型的安全风险提供了宝贵资源，也揭示了模型能力越强，风险特征越复杂，对安全监管提出更高挑战。未来的大模型安全工作须着重关注模型失效内容本身，而非仅仅停留在是否输出层面。

Abstract: Frontier large language models (LLMs) safety evaluation has largely treated harmful generation as an attack outcome rather than as an object of analysis. Consequently, little is known about the harmful outputs produced during model misbehavior, partly because large-scale, high-quality collections of frontier-LLM misbehavior are difficult to obtain. To address this gap, we introduce HarmProfile, a content-centric benchmark dataset that collects model misbehavior across diverse harm categories and model families, and defines the resulting harmful-output distribution as a model-level risk profile. The premise is that, just as linguistic behavior can be characterized from an utterance corpus, model risk can be characterized from the content, severity, and variation of its safety failures. HarmProfile contains over 80,000 validated artifacts from 23 frontier LLMs across 13 model families, organized into 15 harm categories and 57 subcategories. Using this corpus, we find that frontier LLMs reliably produce harmful content at scale, yet exhibit distinct risk profiles; both harmfulness and diversity grow with model capability, suggesting that frontier LLMs may appear safe yet harbor increasingly dangerous knowledge beneath the alignment surface. Our source code is available at https://github.com/fresh-ma/HarmProfile .

</details>


### [222] [Multi-Modal Generative Fuzzy System: Fuzzy Inference Guided Large Model Interactive Question Answering Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14584)
*Hailong Yang,Jianqi Wang,Guanjin Wang,Zhaohong Deng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种融合模糊推理的新型多模态生成架构MMGFS，用于提升多模态问答系统在准确性和可解释性等方面的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态问答系统需融合文本、图像、语音等多种模态信息，但现有方法存在模态偏倚、跨领域知识不确定性大及推理深度不足和可解释性弱的问题。为解决这些挑战，作者引入模糊系统的思想。

Method: 提出了一种多模态生成模糊系统（MMGFS），主要包括两大创新：（1）通过多模态协作反刍机制缓解模态偏倚问题；（2）利用模糊规则与多步推理机制实现跨领域知识融合和分层推理，以增强不确定性建模能力和深度语义理解。

Result: 在开放域（如MultimodalQA、WebQA）和特定领域（如BioMol-VQA、EHRxQA）数据集上，MMGFS在准确性、一致性和泛化能力等指标上均优于现有方法，有效缓解了模态偏倚并提升了模型对问题不确定性的处理能力。

Conclusion: MMGFS能够通过模糊推理与多模态协同机制，实现更深层次的语义推理和更高水平的答案生成，为多模态问答系统提供了新的高效解决方案。

Abstract: In Multimodal Question Answering (MQA), models are required to jointly encode and integrate heterogeneous information from multiple modalities, including text, images, and speech, to perform complex semantic reasoning and decision making. Despite recent advances, existing approaches, including traditional deep learning models and Large Models (LMs) or prompt-based frameworks, continue to face several critical challenges. First, modality bias arises from discrepancies in feature distributions across different modalities, which limits effective cross modal collaborative understanding. Second, many questions require knowledge drawn from multiple domains, introducing significant uncertainty. Third, current methods often rely on shallow semantic matching, resulting in limited reasoning depth an reduced interpretability. To address these issues, inspired by the traditional fuzzy system (FS) framework, we propose a fuzzy-inference-guided multimodal generative architecture termed the Multi-Modal Generative Fuzzy System (MMGFS). The main contributions of MMGFS are two folds. First, it alleviates modality bias through a multimodal collaborative rumination mechanism. Second, it introduces fuzzy rules and a multi-hop inference mechanism to support cross-domain knowledge fusion and hierarchical reasoning, thereby strengthening uncertainty modelling and deepening semantic understanding. We conduct comprehensive evaluations on open-domain question answering datasets, including MultimodalQA and WebQA, as well as domain-specific benchmarks, including BioMol-VQA and EHRxQA. Experimental results demonstrate that MMGFS consistently outperforms existing methods across multiple datasets. It effectively mitigates modality bias and question uncertainty while achieving superior performance in answer accuracy, consistency, and generalization.

</details>


### [223] [Wiola 13M, a Gated Spiral Attention Architecture for Parameter Efficient Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14604)
*Aryuemaan Kumar Chowdhury,Praveen Oosa,Vineesha Reddy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Wiola是一种专为小规模语言模型（千万至亿参数级）设计的解码器模型，包含三项创新结构，以提升效率与性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前大多数小语言模型直接沿用标准Transformer结构，未针对小规模场景进行优化，导致推理、本地部署和科研探索时表现有限。

Method: Wiola每层包含三大创新：1）螺旋旋转位置编码，通过上述频率扰动提升长距离区分能力但不增加参数；2）门控螺旋注意力，为每个注意头加入基于历史上下文的自适应门控；3）蝴蝶前馈块，利用乘积交互与旁路加深梯度流通，改善浅层模型表现。文中对每部分参数和计算量均有严格推导，并证明门控注意力机制在训练与推理阶段完全一致。

Result: 三项新结构在参数量不变的前提下，提升了梯度流动、长距离依赖建模和高效注意力机制。训练和评估流程严格可复现，在标准故事小语料库上完成评测。

Conclusion: Wiola为小型语言模型带来结构创新，兼顾性能与部署效率，开源实现、支持复现与发布，适合学术和实践应用。

Abstract: Small language models in the ten to one hundred million parameter range are attractive for on device inference, rapid experimentation, and controlled scientific study, yet most of them reuse the standard transformer block without adaptation to the small scale regime. We present Wiola, a decoder only language model whose novelty is concentrated in three drop in components of every layer. First, Spiral Rotary Positional Encoding perturbs the standard rotary frequencies by a slowly growing per dimension factor so that phase trajectories fan outward, improving long range discrimination while adding no parameters. Second, Gated Spiral Attention introduces a per head, content adaptive scalar gate derived from a causal cumulative statistic of the query stream, providing an implicit and differentiable form of soft head selection at negligible cost. Third, the Butterfly feed forward block replaces the conventional expansion layer with a multiplicative interaction and an intra block bypass path, matching the parameter count of a four times gated linear unit block while improving gradient flow in shallow stacks. We formalize each component, derive exact parameter and computation budgets, and prove that the gated attention admits an exact and numerically verified equivalence between full sequence training and cached autoregressive decoding, so that no approximation is introduced at inference time. We also describe a fully reproducible training and evaluation protocol on a standard tiny story corpus. The reference implementation is released as an open source package with weights ready publishing support.

</details>


### [224] [AutoMem: A Text-Gradient Recursive Self-Improvement Framework for Automated Memory Architectures Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14621)
*Lin Du,Jie Zhou,Yuxuan Cai,Kai Chen,Qin Chen,Xin Li,Bo Zhang,Wei Li,Liang He*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自适应内存架构自动搜索方法AutoMem，可针对不同任务自动组合编码、存储、检索和管理模块，以提升LLM代理在多个基准任务下的性能与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代理中的长期记忆设计高度耦合，不同任务和模型需求不一，单一内存架构难以适应，人工设计存在性能瓶颈，因此亟需一种能动态适应不同任务的内存架构优化方法。

Method: 1) 构建包含5种编码器、5种存储、6种检索器、4种管理器的离散搜索空间。
2) 提出AutoMem框架，包括基于经验的架构搜索与基于失败的模块诊断，通过递归自我改进发现任务自适应内存架构。
3) 结合历史搜索轨迹和失败文本反馈，提升搜索效率，精确定位并优化瓶颈模块。

Result: 在GAIA、WebWalkerQA和xBench-DeepSearch等任务、两种LLM基础模型上，AutoMem自动选择的内存架构平均提升准确率2.8个百分点；在Qwen3.5-122B-A10B下，相比最佳人工基线减少Token成本14.3%，且搜索效率明显优于大规模随机搜索。

Conclusion: 针对LLM任务多样性，单一内存架构难满足需求。AutoMem实现了内存架构的高效自动搜索，并在准确率及成本上超越人工设计，对未来自适应智能体具有重要价值。

Abstract: Long-term memory is increasingly central to LLM agents, yet memory design remains a highly coupled architecture problem: what to encode, how to store it, how to retrieve it, and how to manage it can vary substantially across tasks and backbone models. We construct a discrete search space with 5 encoders, 5 stores, 6 retrievers, and 4 managers, and show that no single memory architecture consistently dominates: different tasks favor different module combinations, leading to substantial performance gaps. Motivated by this, we propose \textsc{AutoMem}, a text-gradient recursive self-improvement framework for task-adaptive memory architecture search. \textsc{AutoMem} optimizes over the factored space through two components: Experience-Guided Architecture Search, which proposes candidate architectures from historical search trajectories and accumulated reflections, and Failure-Guided Module Diagnosis, which localizes memory-related failures to specific modules and converts them into targeted textual feedback. Experiments on GAIA, WebWalkerQA, and xBench-DeepSearch across two LLM backbones show that \textsc{AutoMem} consistently discovers task-adaptive memory architectures that outperform the strongest human-designed memory baselines, improving accuracy by $2.8$ points on average across six benchmark-backbone settings. Further analysis shows that \textsc{AutoMem} achieves a favorable accuracy-efficiency trade-off, reducing token cost by $14.3\%$ over the strongest accuracy baselines under Qwen3.5-122B-A10B, while also finding stronger architectures than substantially larger random searches within only a few guided iterations.

</details>


### [225] [LLM Safety Alignment in Low-Resource Languages: A Systematic Literature Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14626)
*Valdini Douglace Lemofouet,Blessing Ngozi Uzor,Paula Chikaodinaka Anyanwu,Danielle Blanche Kapsa,Sukairaj Hafiz Imam,P Sam Sahil,Abigail Oppong,Tassallah Abdullahi,Clemencia Siro,Idris Abdulmumin,Seid Muhie Yimam,Shamsuddeen Hassan Muhammad*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本综述系统梳理了大语言模型（LLMs）在低资源语言下的安全性对齐研究，分析存在的问题并指出未来改进方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLMs在高资源语言下的安全对齐取得进展，但在低资源及多语言环境下的安全保障明显较弱，存在较大差距。为解决这一问题，作者希望全面梳理相关领域进展和挑战，并提出改进建议。

Method: 采用PRISMA 2020方法，对来自Semantic Scholar、arXiv和OpenAlex的大约1500篇论文进行筛选，最终选取50篇相关研究，围绕安全对齐方法、多语言安全风险、评测基准、跨语言可迁移性四大主题进行分析，并构建了基于数据适配、目标优化、机制对齐三类适应机制的对齐方法分类体系。

Result: 发现现有多语言模型在跨语言攻击、夹杂语言攻击和低资源语言下的安全表现较弱，英文基准测试难以反映多元文化相关风险，非均衡的多语言预训练、缺乏本地化数据和文化敏感评测等因素导致多语种安全性持续不足，非洲等地区的低资源语言安全基准尤其匮乏。

Conclusion: 多语言安全性仍存在长期差距，未来需开发具文化内涵的基准、注重参与式数据采集、均衡多语预训练以及可扩展的多语对齐方法以提升低资源语言下LLM的安全性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have achieved substantial progress in safety alignment, yet their safety guarantees remain significantly weaker in low-resource and multilingual settings than in high-resource languages. In this paper, we conduct a Systematic Literature Review (SLR) of LLM safety alignment in low-resource languages by adopting the PRISMA 2020 methodology. Out of roughly 1,500 papers identified from Semantic Scholar, arXiv, and OpenAlex, 50 relevant studies have been selected and analyzed. Our review is organized around four themes: safety alignment methods, multilingual safety risks, evaluation benchmarks, and cross-lingual transferability. We further propose a taxonomy of safety alignment approaches based on three adaptation mechanisms: data adaptation, objective optimization, and mechanistic alignment. Across literature, translated English benchmarks fail to sufficiently represent culturally rooted harms, and multilingual models are more vulnerable to cross-lingual jailbreaks, code-switching attacks, and safety degradation in underrepresented languages. These failures are driven by several key factors, including uneven multilingual pre-training coverage, insufficient native-language preference data, poor transfer of safety representations, and a lack of culturally aware evaluation frameworks. The review also notes that many low-resource languages, especially African languages, have fewer safety benchmarks available than other multilingual regions. Overall, the results reveal a persistent multilingual safety gap, and suggest that future progress will require culturally grounded benchmarks, participatory data collection, balanced multilingual pre-training, and scalable multilingual alignment methods.

</details>


### [226] [Inference-Time Mitigation of Adversarial Political Bias in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14629)
*Tejaswi V. Panchagnula,Bruce Coburn,Bryce J. Dietrich,Robert X. Browning,Edward J. Delp,Fengqing Zhu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出用链式思维（CoT）提示和直接偏好优化（DPO）等方法，结合递归自纠正机制，在生成摘要时提高大模型在对抗性提示下的政治中立性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型被广泛用于信息检索与摘要，但它们易受到仇视性或政治偏见的对抗性提示影响，现有模型对齐（如RLHF）并不能专门抑制政治偏见。提升AI安全与公信力需要更有效的中立性保障方法。

Method: 作者基于公开立法视频数据集，生成摘要并通过对抗性提示注入偏见，采用链式思维（CoT）和直接偏好优化（DPO）等多种提示方法，并设计四维度评分系统评估模型摘要的政治中立性。提出递归自纠正方法进行推理阶段的偏见缓解。

Result: 采用递归自纠正等策略，使模型在政治中立性Likert量表得分从2.14提升到4.56，平均跨模型有效提升了政治偏见抵抗能力。

Conclusion: 递归自纠正方法与新提示策略能显著提升大语言模型响应对抗提示时的政治中立表现，为LLM安全性和可信度提升提供了新思路。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) become the mainstay for information retrieval and summarization tasks, ensuring that they are always non-partisan and invulnerable to political bias is a critical step towards safer and more trustworthy Artificial Intelligence (AI). Current model alignment paradigms, such as reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF), make LLMs follow overarching safety instructions. However, this instruction tuning can be exploited via adversarial prompt injection and be used to generate unsafe content. In particular, political bias has not been specifically targeted by modern alignment techniques as harmful and biased content. To address this vulnerability of LLMs, we propose mitigation strategies using Chain of Thought (CoT) prompting and Direct Preference Optimization (DPO). Using a public dataset of legislative videos, we generate summaries using LLMs, inject bias via adversarial prompting and evaluate their performance on a four axis scale designed for political summarization. In this paper, we present different methods to shield LLMs against the injection of political bias. Our results demonstrate that the proposed Recursive Self-Correction approach raises model performance from a Political Neutrality Likert scale baseline of 2.14 to 4.56, averaged across all models, demonstrating effective inference-time mitigation of political bias in LLM-generated summaries.

</details>


### [227] [Characterizing Rhetorical Misalignment in Decision-Making with Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14630)
*Zirui Cheng,Joey Chan,Simo Du,Chenhao Tan,Yue Guo,Hao Peng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出并验证了在高风险人机决策场景下，LLM（大型语言模型）的表述方式可能导致临床决策中的有害结果，即便模型事实正确也可能因措辞引发认知偏差，造成错误决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM越来越多地参与到关键的人类决策流程中，需要了解其输出是否会加剧或引发用户的认知偏差，甚至带来实际负面后果。特别是在医疗等高风险领域，合适的语言表达十分重要。

Method: 作者提出了'修辞失配'的决策理论框架，指LLM在不恰当的决策背景下使用了不合适的表达方式。以美国医师资格考试的数据为基础，设计了真实的临床场景人类受试者实验，并测量LLM生成的信息如何影响决策。此外，利用LLM模拟决策者，实现了可扩展的理论框架评估。

Result: 实验发现，受到LLM修辞影响，临床决策的错误翻转率平均达2.81%。通过受试者的答题理由，发现这些错误与LLM语言诱发的锚定、权威等一系列认知偏差有关。理论框架的可扩展仿真同样验证了这一现象。

Conclusion: LLM的语言表达即便在事实层面完全对齐，也可能因修辞方式的不当使用诱发用户认知偏差，导致有害决策。这一点在高风险应用领域对安全性提出了新的重要挑战。

Abstract: Human decision-making is often shaped by a range of well-documented cognitive biases. As large language models (LLMs) become increasingly integrated into high-stakes human-AI decision-making, it is important to understand whether their outputs can amplify potential biases, how this influences human decisions, and crucially, whether it can lead to harmful consequences. In this work, we develop a decision-theoretic framework to study rhetorical misalignment, a failure mode where an LLM uses rhetorically inappropriate forms of presentation for a given decision context, thereby inducing suboptimal human decisions. We empirically investigate this phenomenon through a human-subject experiment in realistic clinical decision-making using a dataset curated from the United States Medical Licensing Examination. By measuring how LLM-generated information affects decisions, we observe that LLMs induce an average 2.81% rate of harmful decision flips across different models, where clinician participants change from a correct to an incorrect answer. Rationales reported by participants provide evidence that these revisions are closely related to the language used by LLMs that may induce different types of cognitive biases, including anchoring, authority bias, and loss aversion. To enable scalable evaluation, we instantiate our theoretical framework using decision-makers simulated by LLMs to computationally measure rhetorical misalignment. Our findings reveal a safety concern previously unrecognized in high-stakes domains: a model can be factually aligned yet still induce harm through its rhetorical presentation.

</details>


### [228] [DeMTS: Denoising Trajectories as Multivariate Time Series for Hallucination Detection in Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14632)
*Xin Zhang,Yili Wang,Yue Tan,Xin He,Yanyu Qian,Yixin Liu,Yi Chang,Shirui Pan,Xin Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了DeMTS方法，通过多变量时间序列建模，有效提升了扩散大语言模型（D-LLMs）幻觉检测的性能，超过了现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: D-LLMs作为生成文本的新范式，和自回归大语言模型一样易发生“幻觉”——生成语法流畅但不真实的内容。现有的幻觉检测手段未能充分利用去噪过程中的二维token-step全信息，导致效果有限。提升检测的准确性具有重要意义。

Method: 提出DeMTS框架，将D-LLMs去噪轨迹建模为可学习的多变量时间序列，通过token-to-variable分配模块，将token信号映射为更稳定的潜在变量；再结合动态多变量时序建模，整合变量间依赖与时序编码，实现准确的幻觉预测。

Result: 在两类D-LLM骨干网络和三个基准数据上，DeMTS在幻觉检测准确率、稳健性、效率和跨任务可迁移性方面，均优于目前主流检测方法。

Conclusion: DeMTS框架可显著改善D-LLM幻觉检测，且具备良好的扩展性和适用性，对推动更可靠的文本生成具有实际价值。

Abstract: Diffusion large language models (D-LLMs) have emerged as a promising paradigm for text generation. However, similar to autoregressive LLMs, D-LLMs remain vulnerable to hallucinations, where fluent outputs may contain factually incorrect or unsupported content. Although existing hallucination detection methods for D-LLMs attempt to leverage uncertainty trajectories of the denoising process to better identify hallucination signals, they typically compress the trajectories along either the temporal or token dimension, overlooking the useful information encoded in the complete two-dimensional token-step structure. Consequently, they may fail to capture hallucination-relevant patterns, such as inconsistent convergence and cross-token fault propagation, leading to suboptimal detection performance. To bridge this gap, we propose a D-LLM hallucination detection framework that formulates the Denoising trajectories as Multivariate Time Series over learnable latent variables (DeMTS for short). DeMTS employs a trajectory-preserving token-to-variable assignment module to convert token signals into stable latent variables. Based on these variables, we propose dynamic multivariate temporal modeling to progressively integrate inter-variable dependency modeling with temporal encoding for hallucination prediction. Extensive experiments on two D-LLMs backbones and three benchmarks demonstrate that DeMTS outperforms existing hallucination detection methods while maintaining strong robustness, efficiency, and cross-task transferability.

</details>


### [229] [Automatic or Controlled? Repetition Priming Reveals Divergent Processing in Base LLMs, Instruct LLMs, and Humans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14681)
*Jinglei Ren,Yuyue Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究了大语言模型在处理重复词汇时，是激活已有的表征还是重新评估词语，并探讨了训练后模型行为的变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解语言模型在遇到重复词汇时的内部处理方式，对于改进模型的认知模拟和提升交互体验具有重要意义。目前尚不明确模型是如何应对重复信息的以及训练方式对其处理机制的影响。

Method: 作者借鉴了心理学中的重复启动实验（repetition priming），在15个模型（涵盖五个不同家族，参数量1.5B-14B）上，使用语义分类和完形填空任务进行测试，并与人类实验进行了对比。

Result: 发现基础模型表现出自动加工：对重复词汇有立即的促进效应，且效应随时间滞后保持稳定，对去除上下文也有一定的抵抗力，并与对先前词汇的关注相关联。而Instruct模型表现为受控加工：促进效应随时间滞后减弱，在缺乏预期上下文时消失，且在大规模下甚至表现为干扰。在Qwen 2.5系列中，这种差异随模型规模增加而增强。人类则表现为类似Instruct模型的时滞敏感促进，但没有干扰现象。

Conclusion: 训练后语言模型对重复信息的加工方式发生定性变化，基础模型和Instruct模型的行为逐渐分化，两者都不能完全拟合人类认知。该工作为语言模型加工重复信息的机制及其演变提供了新证据。

Abstract: Words recur constantly in natural language use, yet it remains unclear whether language models reactivate prior representations or re-evaluate repeated words afresh, and whether post-training changes this default behavior. We apply repetition priming (Shiffrin and Schneider, 1977) to 15 models across five model families (1.5B-14B parameters) in two tasks, semantic categorization and cloze completion, with matched human experiments using identical stimuli. We find that base models exhibit automatic processing: they show immediate facilitation that remains stable across lags, partially survives context removal, and correlates with attention to prior occurrences. Instruct models exhibit controlled processing: their facilitation decays with lag, collapses without expected context, and reverses to interference at larger scales. Within the Qwen 2.5 family, this dissociation increases monotonically with model scale, suggesting that post-training progressively alters repetition processing. Humans show a hybrid profile, with lag-sensitive facilitation resembling instruct models but without interference, suggesting that neither model type fully captures human cognition. Our findings reveal a qualitative shift in how language models process repeated information after post-training and provide mechanistic evidence for the divergence between model behaviors.

</details>


### [230] [Domain Agnostic Text Redaction from Natural Language Rules using Instruction Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14693)
*Aravindhan Arunagiri,Ayaan Khan,Udayaadithya Avadhanam,SaiBarath Sundar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种可解释、与领域无关的文本脱敏方法，通过用指令微调的大语言模型，基于自然语言规则识别并遮盖结构化和非结构化敏感信息，同时输出脱敏操作的透明理由。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着个人及企业沟通数字化的发展，传统脱敏方案多局限于结构化信息且缺乏可审计性，难以适应复杂多样的信息合规和隐私需求，需一种可解释、灵活、适用性广的新方法。

Method: 用户用自然语言定义敏感信息（无论结构化或非结构化）；通用LLM生成或补充脱敏规则，再用这些规则对小模型进行指令微调；微调后模型能逐步推理规则，对文档进行脱敏，同时给出每次操作的自然语言解释和触发规则。用重建机制度量脱敏强度。

Result: 该方法在各种结构化与非结构化场景下均表现出高重建错误率（即信息难以恢复）与高脱敏精度，证实其在法律、医疗、企业信息治理等关键场景的自动脱敏能力。

Conclusion: 该方案不仅支持多类型敏感信息的自定义脱敏，还在每一步脱敏过程中提供可审核的自然语言解释，能显著提升自动化文本脱敏的实用性与合规性，适合高要求的行业应用。

Abstract: With the increasing digitization of personal and corporate communication, the automatic sanitization of textual data has become a crucial component of data privacy and compliance frameworks. Traditional text sanitization solutions are majorly suitable for obscuring sensitive data with standard structure such as Personal Identifiable Information (PII). These solutions do not provide transparent justification for their redaction, which makes it difficult to audit them. This paper introduces an explainable, domain-agnostic text redaction solution that uses natural language rules of redaction, applied via an instruction-tuned language model, to identify and redact sensitive information in unstructured documents. Unlike traditional text sanitization, this method enables a user to conveniently define any sensitive information; which may be structured (e.g.\ PII) or unstructured (e.g.\ legal terms and conditions) in natural language. A general-purpose LLM generates or augments these natural language rules of redaction from the user's definition, which are then used to instruction-fine-tune a smaller language model that reasons the rules step-by-step over any given document to identify and redact the corresponding sensitive content, while providing transparent justifications for each redaction and highlighting the specific rule that triggered the decision. This explanation is generated in natural language to support human reviewers and auditors in understanding why specific content was redacted. A reconstruction-based metric is used to estimate the probability of recovering redacted information from the sanitized document, quantifying redaction coverage. The solution shows high reconstruction error and high redaction precision, making it suitable for automated text sanitization in critical applications such as legal discovery, medical documentation, and corporate information governance.

</details>


### [231] [Which Question Is Your Attention Metric Answering? Attention Rows as Compositional Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14712)
*Marios Papamichalis,Regina Ruane*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文分析Transformer注意力矩阵中的sink token影响，提出将sink和非sink内容分开评估，并证明这一处理影响实验结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 注意力矩阵多数概率集中于sink token（通常是第一个token），但主流的注意力分布度量方法（如余弦相似度、熵等）在处理sink token上存在歧义（保留还是移除并归一化），这个选择会导致不同甚至相反的实验结论。现有工作通常没有详述这一选择。

Method: 系统比较了10个预训练Transformer模型，检查两种处理sink token的方式对注意力分布比较结论的影响。采用Aitchison距离和熵分解，将sink影响和内容token影响正交拆分，理论和实验两方面评估其作用和区分能力。

Result: 发现约17-47%的头部相似性判断会因为sink处理方式不同而翻转，用常规流程聚类BERT注意力头部时显著依赖这种处理。大部分熵下降实际只是sink变大而非注意力分布变稀疏。错误处理pathway会导致困惑度暴涨。

Conclusion: 注意力分布分析需分离sink和内容影响，推荐采用正交分解的方法更精准度量，并明确给出各度量适用场景。随文发布全部代码和实验数据。

Abstract: Each row of a transformer's attention matrix is a probability distribution over tokens, and in trained models most of that probability lands on a single \emph{sink} token, usually the first. Standard tools for comparing attention rows (cosine similarity, Jensen--Shannon divergence, Shannon entropy) therefore hinge on a choice papers rarely report: keep the sink, or drop it and renormalize. This choice can reverse conclusions. On ten pretrained models from five families, 17--47% of verdicts about which of two heads is more similar flip with the convention, and the most prominent structure in a standard BERT head-clustering pipeline is an artifact of it. The reason is that one-number summaries mix two questions: how much attention the sink takes, and how the rest is divided among the content tokens. Treating rows as compositional data separates them exactly: the Aitchison distance splits orthogonally into a sink term and a content term, entropy splits by an exact identity, and the content distance is characterized by invariances the transformer itself possesses. The separation matters in practice: most measured entropy collapse during training is the sink growing, not attention sharpening (30% of the drop at 70M parameters, 95% at 1B, 79% at 1.4B), and pruning heads with the wrong channel can inflate perplexity more than a hundredfold. We map where each convention is safe, test a frozen out-of-sample predictor (one confirmation, one abstention, one failure), and release code regenerating every number.

</details>


### [232] [Class Imbalance and Batch Effects in LLM-Based Screening for Systematic Reviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14737)
*Gilberto Sussumu Hida,Danilo Monteiro Ribeiro,Clayton Suguio Hida*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究考察了大型语言模型（LLM）在不平衡二元分类任务中的表现，特别是在系统综述中文献筛选的应用。通过实验证明了批处理方式对模型行为影响更大，而预置类别比例信息对模型提升作用有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统综述中的文献筛选通常面临正负样本高度不平衡的问题，研究者希望探索如何更有效地利用LLM进行准确筛选及评估不同元信息（如类别比例）与处理方式（批量/单独）对其性能的影响。

Method: 在五项系统综述中进行实验证明，将任务分别以单独处理和批量处理的方式喂给LLM，并设置有无类别比例（prevalence metadata）两种条件，系统比较、分析LLM的表现。

Result: 结果表明，类别比例信息对LLM分类性能影响有限，并未带来显著提升。而批量处理对LLM决策行为产生的影响更大，这种影响随着样本类别分布不同而变化。聚合层面的分析和逐条（item-level）的分析结果有时并不一致。

Conclusion: 在实际文献筛选任务中，批量处理的影响不仅体现在时间或成本，更会显著影响模型的决策行为和结果，因此应综合考虑其行为影响，而非仅关注效率或便捷性。

Abstract: This study analyses LLMs in imbalanced binary classification, using study screening in systematic reviews as the application domain. An experiment was conducted in five reviews, comparing individual and batch processing, with and without prevalence metadata. The results indicate a limited influence of the prevalence metadata, with no evidence that it improves performance. In contrast, batch processing produced larger behavioral changes that varied according to the prevalence of the class. The aggregate and item-level analyses did not always coincide. Therefore, batch processing should be evaluated not only in terms of cost, but also in relation to its effects on decision-making behavior.

</details>


### [233] [Prompting is not enough: supervised baselines and leakage control for measuring shared decision-making with LLMs in pediatric encounters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14792)
*Bernardo Modenesi,Jody Lin,Kimberly Kaphingst,Angela Zhu,Maya Wheeler,Peilu Zhang,Angela Fagerlin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究评估大语言模型（LLM）的零样本提示（zero-shot prompting）能否可靠检测真实临床会诊中的共享决策行为（SDM），并比较监督学习的增益。结果显示，仅靠零样本提示表现有限，监督学习有明显提升，但数据切分与标签泄露等环节需谨慎处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多医疗场景需要评估医患共享决策行为，但人工标注费时费力。希望用大语言模型实现自动化检测，探索零样本（无监督）与小样本监督学习在实际临床数据中的有效性。

Method: 分析21例外科会诊音频数据，由人工标注12类SDM行为。评估零样本本地大模型（Qwen 2.5 32B）、基于冻结嵌入的监督分类器与两者的堆叠模型（logistic stack），并采用患者分组的嵌套交叉验证，统计置信区间。

Result: 零样本大模型kappa=0.139，监督分类器kappa=0.227，堆叠模型kappa=0.242。监督方法明显优于零样本。实验还发现多种标签和数据泄露路径，影响模型评估准确性。

Conclusion: 零样本提示无法如小型监督学习那样可靠地检测SDM行为，且标签泄露和数据分组方式对评测有较大影响。需外部验证后才能推广到其它情境。

Abstract: Objectives: To determine whether zero-shot prompting of a large language model (LLM) is sufficient to detect shared decision-making (SDM) behaviors in real clinical encounters, and whether supervised learning adds value under patient-grouped, nested evaluation.
  Methods: We analyzed 21 audio-recorded outpatient surgical decision encounters (19 unique patients; 7,566 utterance segments; ~6.1 hours) between families of children with multiple long-term conditions and their surgical providers. Trained coders labeled segments for 12 SDM behaviors (human-human macro Cohen's kappa = 0.695). We compared a zero-shot local LLM (Qwen 2.5 32B), a supervised classifier over frozen sentence embeddings, and their logistic stack, under patient-grouped outer folds with inner cross-fitted thresholds and patient-resampled confidence intervals.
  Results: The zero-shot LLM reached macro kappa = 0.139 (95% CI 0.111-0.164). The supervised classifier reached kappa = 0.227 (0.186-0.262), a paired improvement of 0.088 (0.051-0.119). A logistic stack of the two reached kappa = 0.242 (0.198-0.284). We identified multiple corpus-specific leakage paths, including grouping sibling recordings separately and allowing labels from an outer held-out patient to enter few-shot exemplars used while fitting downstream models.
  Conclusion: Zero-shot prompting alone is not sufficient to measure SDM behavior as reliably as a small supervised model, and patient-level grouping alone does not prevent leakage when labeled prompt exemplars are precomputed outside the outer evaluation loop. Reported performance is sensitive to the unit of data splitting and to where labeled exemplars enter the pipeline. External validation is needed before these findings generalize beyond this population, model, prompt, and codebook.

</details>


### [234] [Beyond Tokens: A Survey on Decoding Methods for Large Language and Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14797)
*Haoran Wang,Xiongxiao Xu,Philip S. Yu,Kai Shu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文综述了用于大语言模型（LLMs）和大视觉-语言模型（LVLMs）的生成输出推理方法（解码方法）的最新进展，强调其在提升生成内容与用户意图一致性方面的重要性和应用前景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLMs和LVLMs具备强大的生成能力，但如何使模型输出更好地契合用户意图仍是难题。多数现有方案集中于训练阶段，然而推理时的解码方法因效率和可扩展性更受青睐，因此有必要对这些方法进行系统梳理和评价。

Method: 论文归纳了当前解码方法的三大新兴范式，系统性地回顾了这些方法的原理，包括如何通过引导单词选择、整体序列生成、或并行生成等方式，调控生成内容并提升生成速度。

Result: 通过文献回顾，论文总结了现有代表性的解码方法，评述了它们在效率和效果上的表现，梳理了尚存的技术挑战，并整理了相关论文与资源。

Conclusion: 解码方法因其能高效提升LLMs和LVLMs的生成质量与用户意图契合度，具有广泛应用潜力。未来研究应继续优化解码算法，应对现有挑战，进一步拓宽其应用场景。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) and large vision-language models (LVLMs) have demonstrated impressive generative capabilities, yet ensuring their outputs align with user intent is still challenging. While most existing approaches address this issue at the training stage, inference-time approaches like decoding methods offer a more efficient and scalable solution. Decoding methods control model generation by guiding token-level selection, performing sequence-level generation, or generating tokens in parallel to accelerate the process. In this survey, we identify three emerging paradigms from recent works on decoding methods for LLMs and LVLMs, provide a systematic review of these methods, highlight ongoing challenges, and discuss potential future research directions. Our goal is to underscore the efficiency and effectiveness of decoding methods and offer a practical view of their applications. Paper lists and more resources on decoding methods for LLMs and LVLMs can be found at https://github.com/wang2226/Awesome-LLM-Decoding.

</details>


### [235] [Beyond the pale: Assessing prevalence and contents of extremist speech in LLM training data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14813)
*Dmitry Nikolaev,Ashley A. Mattheis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文揭示开放训练语料Dolma中存在大量极端言论和仇恨言论，为大语言模型(LLMs)训练数据安全性和可信性提出警示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管AI安全与可信性备受关注，但LLMs的训练和微调阶段语料成分，尤其是否包含未筛查极端言论，少有人关注。作者希望探索LLMs暴露于极端言论的真实状况。

Method: 采用官方文件和现有研究定义极端言论，结合自动化文本处理及专家验证，分析开放语料Dolma中极端内容的分布和比例，进而评估其安全性风险。

Result: 在Dolma语料中发现数十万份包含多类型极端内容的文档，包括仇恨言论和直接暴力号召，证实极端信息广泛存在于主流训练集。

Conclusion: Dolma等开放训练语料的安全性需重新审视，极端内容普遍存在可能影响模型安全和输出可信性，未来数据筛选和预训练策略必须加强治理与监管。

Abstract: Despite a strong interest on the part of the research community in the topic of trustworthy and safe AI, the composition of the text corpora that large language models (LLMs) encounter in pre- and post-training has not yet drawn much attention. In this work, we address the question of whether LLMs are exposed to unfiltered, uncontextualised extremist speech. Using several definitions of extremist speech, stemming from official documents and research literature, and an extraction pipeline combining automated text processing with expert verification, we provide a lower bound on the prevalence of extremist documents in Dolma, an open training corpus underpinning the OLMo series of models. We show that Dolma is likely to include hundreds of thousands of documents containing extremist content and hate speech of several types, including direct calls for violence, and discuss the implications of this for data curation and model pre-training.

</details>


### [236] [Writing Style Similarity Reflects Academic Genealogy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14843)
*Cameron Manzo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 作者分析了学术论文作者归属系统在区分实际作者与其学术关系对象（如导师、同门学生）上的失误，发现这些系统常将风格相似的学术亲属归为同一人，可能导致误判。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着作者归属技术被用于鉴别代写或AI生成论文，对合法作者的误判风险增加，尤其是学术圈中学术风格继承和传递现象未被系统充分考虑。

Method: 作者基于Math Genealogy Project构建含5803名单独作者及2501组真实导师-学生对的arXiv数学论文数据集，并用微调后的嵌入模型测量作者间风格距离，对比导师与学生、同门兄弟、仅同机构/学科作者间的相似度。再在该语料上做作者归属任务，分析误判分布。

Result: 导师与学生间、学术兄弟间论文风格较普通同行更加接近（39.9%、30.4%距离提升），仅共享机构/领域者相似度微弱。作者归属系统错误更多发生于作者的导师和学术兄弟，与随机情形相比频率高出11倍。

Conclusion: 学术写作风格在学术系统内存在家族式传承和扩散，当前归属系统未能区分此类自然相似，导致误判风险。应改进算法以减少对合法作者尤其是学术亲属的错误指控。

Abstract: As authorship attribution systems are increasingly deployed to detect ghostwritten and AI-generated papers, their errors can support accusations against legitimate authors. These systems assume each author's style is their own. Researchers, however, study under advisors, and inherit their stylistic quirks. We build a corpus of arXiv authors with $\geq 2$ solo papers from the Mathematics Genealogy Project graph, giving $5{,}803$ total authors and $2{,}501$ ground-truth advisor-student pairings. Using embeddings from a fine-tuned model, advisors sit $39.9\%$ closer in cosine distance to their students than a random same-field author does. Two open encoders reproduce the effect at $12.6\%$ and $14.5\%$. \emph{Academic siblings}, two students of one advisor who may never have met, sit $30.4\%$ closer across $8{,}360$ pairs, even when they studied at different institutions. Pairs who share only an institution and a field show negligible similarity. Given a closed-set attribution task over the same corpus, the system's errors occur on the true author's advisors and academic siblings $11$ times more often than chance.

</details>


### [237] [What to Forget in Unlearning? Forget Set Curation for Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14855)
*Animesh Jha,Arpandeep Khatua,Youssef Allouah,Sanmi Koyejo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究语言模型在删除指定内容时面临的上游难题：如何根据请求定位需被遗忘的数据（forget set）。提出了CleanSlate基准，揭示忘记集选取方法虽可有效屏蔽目标内容，但易造成模型能力退化与意外遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器去遗忘大多假设需删除样本已知，而真实场景下请求者难以直接指出需删除哪些具体数据（如歌曲、书籍的片段），需先解决忘记集筛选问题。

Method: 作者提出了CleanSlate基准，专门针对模型逐字输出抑制任务，包含模型相关的内容提取，以及关于原文内容的问答与模型能力保持评估。通过比较直接词面/子串选取与评价驱动选取的忘记集策略分析其影响。

Result: 词面和子串法通常生成的忘记集难以实现有效抑制；而知晓评估指标后精挑细选的忘记集虽然能彻底压制请求内容，却往往带来严重副作用，如无关内容性能下降与模型能力削弱。

Conclusion: 实际的机器去遗忘任务不仅是给定忘记集后的优化问题，忘记集如何筛选决定了能被遗忘的内容及模型其他能力维持的程度。

Abstract: Machine unlearning aims to remove targeted data or behaviors from a trained model without retraining from scratch. Yet most evaluations assume that the examples to forget are already known. In realistic language-model deployments, a requester may ask a model to stop reproducing a song or book without knowing which spans, documents, quotations, or near-duplicates in a trillion-token corpus support that behavior. We study this missing upstream problem, forget set curation: mapping a suppression request to the data passed to an unlearning algorithm. We introduce CleanSlate, a benchmark for verbatim output suppression over songs and books, with model-specific extraction profiles, content-grounded QA, and capability-retention evaluations. CleanSlate exposes two failure modes. Natural lexical and exact-substring curators often yield forget sets that lead to weak suppression. An evaluation-aware curator suppresses requested continuations almost completely, but causes collateral regression on non-requested content and model-dependent capability loss. These results show that practical unlearning is not only an optimization problem once a forget set is given: the data chosen for forgetting determines both what can be unlearnt and what else is damaged.

</details>


### [238] [Where Does Retrieval Fail? Evaluating RAG Architectures for Agricultural Advisory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14886)
*Khan Raiyan Ibne Reza,Sanjana Aktar Maria,Sumaiya Tabassum Nimi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文分析了RAG系统在孟加拉语农业咨询中的检索质量，发现不同检索模型和语言条件下表现差异巨大，强调评估应细分报告而非使用单一分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统（检索增强生成）常用整体得分（如R@10）评估检索质量，这在处理含混、不同语言风格（如口语农民查询与正式科研文档）时掩盖了方法优劣。作者希望揭示不同模型、语言和查询类型下的实际检索表现，推动低资源场景下RAG评估方式的改进。

Method: 作者收集了1,000个孟加拉农民查询和2,882个知识节点（取自284份孟加拉官方农业文献），并构建测试集。分别测试5种检索架构和6种嵌入模型，考察原生孟加拉语、英语对孟加拉语的跨语言等三类语言条件下检索效果。

Result: BM25对原生孟加拉查询表现最好，R@10为0.506，但Hybrid RRF总体最高（R@10=0.539）。密集检索对口语查询表现极差（R@10=0.093）但对正式查询极佳（R@10=0.970）。跨语言时BM25表现剧降（R@10=0.004），密集检索影响较小（从0.464降至0.425）。嵌入配置与段落长度均可致R@10变化达7倍。

Conclusion: RAG系统评估不应简单用单一得分，必须细分语言与查询类型报告结果，特别在低资源语言场景下才更具参考价值。公开了相关数据集和评测脚本。

Abstract: Retrieval quality in RAG systems is commonly reported as a single aggregate score, which can hide large differences across query types and language conditions. We study this problem in Bengali agricultural advisory, where farmer queries are often colloquial while official advisory documents use formal scientific terminology. We construct a test collection of 1,000 queries and 2,882 knowledge nodes extracted from 284 official Bangladeshi agricultural publications, and use it to evaluate five retrieval architectures and six embedding models under three controlled language conditions.
  The results show that no single retrieval method is consistently best. For native Bengali queries, BM25 is the strongest single retriever (R@10 = 0.506) while Hybrid RRF reaches the highest overall R@10 of 0.539. However, dense retrieval performance varies sharply by query type: R@10 is 0.093 on colloquial farmer queries and 0.970 on formal safety queries. Across language conditions, BM25 R@10 drops from 0.506 on Bengali queries to 0.004 when English queries are matched against the Bengali corpus, while dense retrieval falls only from 0.464 to 0.425. We also find that embedding task configuration and passage length can each change reported R@10 by a factor of seven, independent of architecture. These results show why low-resource RAG evaluation should report performance by language condition and query type rather than relying on aggregate scores alone. The dataset and evaluation scripts are available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/RaiyanKhaan/AgriTrust-RAG.

</details>


### [239] [Interpretable Cross-Lingual Alignment in Small Language Models: Probing Cultural and Pragmatic Reasoning in Japanese-English Bilingual LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14896)
*Florian Braun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了J-PragEval-v0基准，专门评估大型语言模型在日语语用现象上的能力，并分析模型内部不同层对这些现象的表示与处理方式。作者还提出一种无需调整参数的推理时改写技术，实现语用风格的操控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然大型语言模型（LLMs）在英语上的表现优异，但在与英语类型差异显著的日语等语言上的能力被低估且理解不足。现有的评测方法通常无法细分模型在日语语用（例如敬语、内外团体指称、情境礼貌、零照应等）上的具体表现，因此需要更精准的测评工具来揭示模型的优缺点。

Method: 作者开发了J-PragEval-v0基准，针对四类日语语用现象设计了最小对测试样本，使语用能力的评估与表层流畅性区分开来。结合线性探针和教师强制log-概率评估，分析TinySwallow-1.5B模型不同层中的语用对比信息。此外，提出了一种“语用表示引导”方法，通过沿探针识别出的类均值差异方向调整残差流激活，实现无需参数调整的推理时操控。

Result: 1）敬语层面在模型的第15层残差流中表现清晰，平衡准确率达0.96。2）零照应与内团体指称在最终token处线性不可解（准确率0.48和0.38），但生成过程中能获得较高区分（翻转率0.77和0.79）。3）间接拒绝合成了礼貌与续写长度，导致探针准确率高（0.95）但翻转率低（0.43）。4）“引导”法的对比激活添加能在存在线性信号时，几乎恢复探针的准确率。

Conclusion: 新基准能揭示LLM在日语语用细粒度现象上的真实表现，不同类型语用在模型中的可解释性和分布有差别。“语用表示引导”作为方法具备可行性，但尚需在更大模型及更充分样本上验证。该工作为提升多语言语用理解和控制提供了新工具和分析思路。

Abstract: Large language models work well on English and behave in poorly understood ways on languages typologically far from it. Japanese is a clean example, where evaluation still leans on translation quality and JGLUE-style benchmarks, which roll lexical, syntactic and pragmatic competence into a single score. The phenomena on which general-purpose models fail Japanese users are pragmatic: honorifics, in-group and out-group reference, context-sensitive politeness, zero anaphora.
  I introduce J-PragEval-v0, a minimal-pair benchmark isolating four such phenomena from surface fluency, and combine it with linear probes and teacher-forced log-probability evaluation to ask where inside TinySwallow-1.5B (28 layers, hidden size 1536) the corresponding contrasts live. The four features split three ways. Honorific register sits cleanly in the residual stream: 0.96 balanced accuracy at layer 15, and the model flips its preferred continuation with the scenario on 93 percent of items. Implicit subject and in-group reference are not linearly decodable at the final prompt token (0.48 and 0.38), yet flip rates are 0.77 and 0.79, so the contrast is worked out during generation rather than stored at the prompt. Indirect refusal is the negative case: 0.95 probe accuracy collapsing to a 0.43 flip rate under length-normalised teacher forcing, because the current minimal pairs conflate politeness with continuation length.
  I also specify Pragmatic Representation Steering, a parameter-free inference-time method that edits residual-stream activations along the class-mean-difference directions probing identifies. Feasibility is argued indirectly rather than demonstrated: the contrastive activation addition baseline, the same geometry the method would inject, recovers probe accuracy within one to two points of logistic regression wherever a linear signal exists. Scaling to Llama-3.1-Swallow-8B is the next step.

</details>


### [240] [How Do Agents Fail on AutoResearch: End-to-End Diagnostic Evaluation on 100 Real-World Frontier Research Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14905)
*Yanlin Fei,Nazhou Liu,Xinmiao Yu,Shaolong Chen,Lei Li,Rahul Thapa,Madalina Ciobanu,Qingqing Mao,Ritankar Das*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套针对自动化科研代理（AutoResearch）的全面评估框架AutoResearchEval，并归纳总结了该领域常见的失败模式和根本性限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管AI已广泛助力科学研究，近期LLM和agentic scaffold的发展使得自动化科研从假设生成到论文发表成为可能，即“AutoResearch”范式。但现有评价体系只注重结果，忽视过程分析，无法系统揭示代理的不足。

Method: 作者建立了AutoResearchEval评测体系，覆盖七大学科完整研究周期的100个真实科研任务，通过8种自动科研代理模型组合，系统跟踪并注释800条完整代理路径，细致分析过程和中间产物。以此为基础，构建了包含45种失败类型的AutoResearch Failure Taxonomy (ARFT)，并用人工调校的“代理法官”机制回溯判别。

Result: 8种模型组合均出现高度一致的失败模式，集中表现在缺乏元认知能力：无法反思产出与依据、修正推理过程、或质疑自身路径。这些缺陷不依赖于具体的框架或调度方案，而体现为底层模型普遍性不足。

Conclusion: 现有AutoResearch代理虽能完成流程，但受限于缺乏元认知循环。AutoResearchEval和ARFT工具的公布将促进后续针对自动科研的深层次改进和理论创新。

Abstract: AI has long assisted scientific research, but the rapid advance of LLMs and agentic scaffolds is reshaping the landscape; a single system can now carry whole-stage research from an initial hypothesis all the way to final published paper, which is a paradigm now referred to as AutoResearch. Existing evaluations reveal little about how these agents operate or where they break down. Tasks are narrowly-scoped, evaluation measures performance but not process, and failure diagnoses lack systematic coverage or artifact-level visibility. To address this gap, we introduce AutoResearchEval, featuring 100 tasks grounded in published frontier science across 7 scientific domains and the full research lifecycle, including ideation, retrieval, execution, analysis, writing, and review. Evaluating 8 harness-model combinations yields 800 autoresearch agent trajectories, with process-level annotation. We organize these insights into AutoResearch Failure Taxonomy or ARFT, a framework of 45 empirically-grounded failure patterns. To enable scalable fine-grained attribution, we leverage a human-calibrated agent-as-a-judge pipeline to inspect complete trajectories and intermediate artifacts. Failure patterns converge on a single overarching limitation, namely that current agents lack a metacognitive loop, which entails the ability to check what they produced against what they found, revise when it does not hold up, and question whether the path they took was sound. The same patterns recur across all 8 harness-model combinations, including the strongest models tested, locating the deficit at the model level rather than in any particular scaffold; whether orchestration-level interventions can close it is an open question this work does not test. We publicly release AutoResearchEval and ARFT to facilitate continued research and development in autonomous scientific discovery.

</details>


### [241] [Training Leaves Traces: Centered Residual Signatures for Language Model Lineage Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14929)
*Aman Singh Thakur,Rayan Khoury*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需数据、仅通过权重分析来判别两个开源语言模型是否存在继承关系（lineage）的白盒方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前开源大模型常常经历finetune、量化、修剪、合并等操作，且其具体来源与修改历史不明。传统验证方法依赖数据或行为分析，难以判断权重量独立的继承关系。论文动机是发展一种仅依靠模型权重本身的血缘验证方法，解决模型安全与来源溯源难题。

Method: 研究发现残差训练会在权重中引入共享的恒等分量，不足以单独判别继承关系。作者去除这一分量后，比较不同残差块的特定结构，提出一种对称的传统量分数(lineage score)，并用独立模型进行了校准。该方法速度快，可分离fine-tuned、LoRA-merged、pruned、quantized与独立/蒸馏模型。

Result: 在residual-MLP和GPT-2等基准上，该分数可完全区分不同来源模型（AUROC=1.0）；在伪装相同功能的laundering实验中，传统基线易受损失，本文方法分数保持不变且在GPT-2上运行速度提升76倍。此外，该方法在六种主流语言模型家族中表现稳定，并在LLaMA-2公开模型上成功判别亲缘关系。

Conclusion: 文中提出的无数据、白盒模型权重血缘判别方法通用有效，为开源大模型的溯源与安全检验提供了新工具，有助于模型合规与知识产权保护。

Abstract: Open-weight language models are fine-tuned, quantized, pruned, and merged, yet their provenance is often undocumented. We study data-free white-box lineage verification: can weights alone reveal whether two compatible model checkpoints share ancestry?
  Residual training produces a shared identity-aligned component in branch products, so this structure alone cannot establish ancestry. We remove it and compare checkpoint-specific structure across residual blocks, yielding a symmetric lineage score calibrated against independent checkpoints. On residual-MLP and GPT-2 benchmarks, the score separates fine-tuned, LoRA-merged, pruned, and quantized descendants from independent and distilled models (AUROC=1.0), distinguishing weight ancestry from behavioral similarity. Under function-preserving checkpoint laundering experiments, weight-space baselines lose margin or fail; our score remains unchanged and runs 76x faster than the nearest robust baseline on GPT-2. The projection-pairing signal appears across six language-model families and beyond, and a case study correctly identifies 3 related and 7 unrelated LLaMA-2 public checkpoints. Collectively, these results establish a passive, data-free provenance signal for compatible open-weight language-model checkpoints

</details>


### [242] [DA-RAC: Distance-Aware Calibration of LLM Judges for Trustworthy AI Auditing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14950)
*Cheng Wu,Vishal Anand,Jaya Krishna Mandivarapu,Xiya Liu,Rui Zhuang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种新的LLM判定方法DA-RAC，通过引入距离感知的参考锚点，有效提升大模型自动评测的可信度和稳健性，减少不良输出被误判合格的风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式AI系统的评测主要依赖上下文无关的LLM自动评分，但参考示例的无关性或不当选取会导致模型评分失真，漏判、错判高风险输出，影响自动评测的安全与质量。

Method: 提出DA-RAC方法，根据待判断实例检索语义和结构相似的标注‘锚点’，并根据其距离赋权，通过锚点邻域的难度对评测校准和分层，有别于传统的零样本、思维链和静态锚点等基线方法。

Result: 在多轮LLM自动评测基准测试中，DA-RAC方法校准效果更佳，‘误通过’风险显著降低，表现优于主流静态锚点等比较方法。机制分析发现，评分结果随锚点距离系统性变化，静态参考风险高。

Conclusion: LLM评测不仅需更强模型，也应配以可溯源、可质询、相关性强的参考识别，DA-RAC证明了距离感知参考能显著改善大模型自动评测的可用性与安全性。

Abstract: Generative AI systems are increasingly producing real-world artifacts, however their efficacy and validity are often evaluated via context-free LLM-scoring. These judges can be miscalibrated by irrelevant in-context reference examples, creating false confidence and allowing low-quality or harmful outputs to pass evaluation. We study this failure mode as context-induced miscalibration and introduce DA-RAC, a distance-aware reference-anchored calibration method for LLM judges. DA-RAC retrieves semantically and structurally similar labeled anchors for each judgement scenario, weights them by distance, and exposes neighborhood difficulty as a calibration and triage signal. On multi-run LLM-judge evaluation benchmarks, it improves calibration and reduces false-pass risk relative to zero-shot, chain-of-thought evaluation, and static-anchor baselines. Mechanistic analysis shows that judge scores vary systematically with anchor distance, while static references can induce misleading decision boundaries. Thus LLM-judgement requires not only better models, but also calibrated, auditable reference selection, especially when automated evaluation is used to support high-impact AI generated artifacts. Judgments should be grounded in relevant, inspectable, and contestable interpretive artifacts.

</details>


### [243] [RamseyGadgets: A Graph Construction Dataset for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14999)
*Zohair Raza Hassan,Deepak Pandita*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个新的数据集RamseyGadgets，专门用于测试生成式大模型在特定类型组合图（Ramsey-good图）构造任务中的能力，并对主流开源大模型进行了准确率评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的组合图构造常由人类手动和经验推动，且已有问题难以甄别模型真正推理能力，故提出需要全新且未被充分探索的图构造任务来评价大模型的推理与生成能力。

Method: 作者设计了RamseyGadgets数据集，包含70个新颖且具代表性的组合图构造任务，均为小规模（不超过10个顶点），可通过SAT求解器自动验证。随后，作者利用该数据集系统性评估了5种开源大模型对这些任务的解答能力，并分析了不同提示对方法效果的帮助。

Result: 在难度较高的任务上，模型平均准确率仅为37.70%，其中表现最好的模型是Gemma-4-31B。此外，数据集便于扩展，也支持探讨如何通过提示提升大模型的表现。

Conclusion: 当前开源大模型在未见过的图构造推理任务上表现有限，新数据集为进一步研究模型的数学推理能力和提示设计提供了有力工具。

Abstract: Constructing special graphs is an important task within graph theory and computer science. Many popular graph constructions are the result of a comprehensive exploration of relevant graphs and human ingenuity. Given the rise of generative AI usage in mathematics, it is natural to test whether LLMs are able to construct graphs with specified properties using their reasoning capabilities. Unfortunately, many natural graph construction problems, such as finding extremal Ramsey-good graphs (i.e., avoiding specific monochromatic subgraphs), have been explored extensively in the literature, making it difficult to ascertain whether a construction is the product of an LLM's reasoning capabilities or its recollection from training data. In this work, we introduce \textbf{RamseyGadgets}, a novel dataset of 70 underexplored graph construction problems that require finding Ramsey-good graphs with special properties (e.g., containing an edge with a fixed color). These problems have reasonably sized solutions (at most 10 vertices) that can be verified by SAT solvers, making them suitable for automatic evaluation. Our dataset is easily expandable, as one can simply change the monochromatic subgraphs being avoided to obtain a new set of problems. We evaluate the performance of five open-source LLMs on our dataset and report the results. Our findings show that LLMs achieve only 37.70% accuracy on the hard-tier problems in our dataset, with Gemma-4-31B achieving the highest performance out of the five. We also showcase how our dataset allows us to ascertain what kind of hints help LLMs perform better at this task.

</details>


### [244] [Harness the Memory: A Holistic Evaluation of Memory Substrates in Memory Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15008)
*Wei-Chieh Huang,Weizhi Zhang,Yuchen Wu,Yankai Chen,Eric Hanchen Jiang,Wooseong Yang,Yiwei Yang,Henry Peng Zou,Hanrong Zhang,Ying Nian Wu,Haolun Wu,Kai-Wei Chang,Philip S. Yu,Xue Liu,Aylin Caliskan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统评测了不同记忆基底在长时序大模型智能体中的表现，并提出没有万能的方案，设计需根据具体应用场景与性能权衡选择合适结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 记忆系统对大模型智能体的长期推理和任务完成至关重要，但现有评测缺乏对不同记忆基底选型的实证指导，影响智能体在不同任务下的效果优化。

Method: 作者设计了统一的评测平台，涵盖稠密/稀疏索引、文本存储、结构化与分层存储、渐进型记忆、参数更新和激活兼容上下文机制，针对三个主流骨干模型与四套基准任务体系，用26项性能与效率指标仔细对比分析。

Result: 不同记忆基底在特定任务和环境下表现各异：广泛检索有利于长上下文问答，但过度检索会影响动作决策；可扩展性也导致部分基底在长历史场景下表现变差或资源消耗过高。没有单一基底在所有场景下占优。

Conclusion: 设计长时序智能体内存时，需具备基底路由机制以适配不同任务需求。本研究为高效、可靠、场景感知的智能体持久记忆系统设计提供了实证参考。

Abstract: Memory is becoming core infrastructure for long-horizon LLM agents, yet existing evaluations offer limited guidance on which memory substrate, namely the underlying medium in which memory is represented and stored, should be used under different operating regimes. We present a controlled harness evaluation of memory substrates for memory-augmented agents, covering dense and sparse indices, text records, structural stores, hierarchical stores, refinement-based memories, parametric updates, and activation-compatible context mechanisms. Across three backbone models and four benchmark suites spanning user-centric question answering and agent-centric decision-making, we instrument 26 performance and efficiency metrics under a unified harness. Our results show that no single substrate consistently dominates: broad retrieval benefits long-context factual QA, while excessive retrieval can harm sequential decision-making by shifting attention away from action-critical context. Scalability introduces a further routing axis, as substrates that perform well at moderate history lengths can become costly or brittle at longer horizons. These findings motivate substrate routing as a necessary component of adaptive agent memory systems and provide empirical guidance for designing efficient, reliable, and regime-aware long-term memory for LLM agents. Code will be made available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [245] [Handoff-H1: An Orchestrated Vision-Agent System for Material Quantity Takeoff from Construction Blueprints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15032)
*Bruno Chicelli,Henrique Alves,Rodrigo Anselmo,Joshua Weinberg,Felipe Lemos,Jan Baryla*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Handoff-H1系统，它能从建筑蓝图自动提取物料清单，在现有方法与人工水平之间取得了显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从建筑蓝图提取完整的物料清单非常依赖于人工专业知识和复杂的视觉、推理能力，而自动化工具尚无法达到专家的准确性和覆盖度，因此需要新的解决方案提升自动化水平以提高行业效率。

Method: Handoff-H1系统分为三层：（1）专用的计算机视觉模块用于提取基础元素；（2）具备图像操作与视觉任务工具（如计数、检测与图纸分解等）的智能代理；（3）基于结构化的行业知识库的分层信息存储。系统在真实住宅蓝图集上进行了实证测试。

Result: 在10套真实蓝图上测试，Handoff-H1对主要建材实现了81.6%的加权综合分（86.1%覆盖率，78.8%的数量精度@25%），显著高于前沿自动系统（最高61分）和人工估算（77.6%）。

Conclusion: Handoff-H1系统在从建筑蓝图自动提取物料清单的准确性和覆盖度方面，首次实现了超过人工专家的综合表现，可辅助行业大幅提升工作效率。

Abstract: Converting a set of architectural blueprints into a complete material quantity takeoff requires visual perception across drawing sheets, dimensional and multi-hop reasoning, and grounding in construction conventions that the drawings never state. We present Handoff-H1, a takeoff system built from three layers: purpose-built computer-vision models that extract primitives; tool-using agents equipped with image operations and in-house visual-task tools, including CV-model-backed counting, detection and plan decomposition; and a persistent, hierarchically structured project foundation, grounded in a curated construction knowledge base. We evaluate on the Construction Blueprint Takeoff Benchmark: 10 real residential blueprint sets paired with consensus-validated expert takeoffs - 2,009 verified line items, restricted for scoring to the 1,348 primary-tier materials that drive an estimate - scored per trade by an LLM judge on material coverage and quantity Precision@25% (P@.25) and combined into a weighted composite. Under identical scoring from the raw PDF, seven frontier and open-weight models span composites of 35-61, and independent professional estimators - scored against the same reconciled gold standard - post 77.6% (65.5% coverage, 87.9% P@.25). Handoff-H1, working end-to-end from the raw PDF, reaches 81.6% (86.1% coverage, 78.8% P@.25): roughly 20 points above the strongest frontier agent, and above the independent estimators by pairing near-human quantity precision with coverage they do not reach. The evaluation harness is public for the open harbor framework; the blueprint sets and ground truth are available upon request for research use.

</details>


### [246] [RecurrentGPT: Expressive Depth through Recurrent Modulation in Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15062)
*Amr Hegazy,Amr Alanwar,Mostafa Elhoushi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出RecurrentGPT，通过在变换器结构中引入循环共享核心，实现参数高效与表达能力的权衡，显著减少参数和显存消耗，在保持或提升模型性能的前提下实现推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩大Transformer语言模型规模时，唯一化每层参数有利于功能专业化却内存消耗大；而深度共享虽然节省内存但降低表现力。因此需要一种既能高效利用内存又不损失模型表达力的结构。

Method: RecurrentGPT结构由固定的前奏和尾声块夹住一个可多次迭代的共享核心层，借鉴门控循环神经网络的思想，通过投影和条件更新门，根据隐藏状态、前奏输出和重新采样的噪声自适应地调节每次循环的更新，使整个模型可用少数层实现多样化表达。

Result: 在等FLOPS条件下，3层RecurrentGPT达到与12层GPT-2 Small接近的精度，在各规模和预算分区均优于其他深度共享方案；在等参数量下，循环结构带来更优验证损失；大规模下参数、内存节省显著，只小幅增加推理延迟。

Conclusion: 自适应深度复用可在不牺牲模型性能的情况下大大减少参数和显存开销，是提升大规模Transformer模型效率的一种可行方法。

Abstract: Scaling transformer language models creates an inherent tension between expressivity and memory efficiency. While unique weights across layers preserve functional specialization---from input-grounding to abstract refinement---they incur a substantial memory footprint. Conversely, standard depth-sharing enforces uniform transformations that collapse representational diversity and degrade modeling quality. We introduce RecurrentGPT, a recurrent depth transformer where fixed-depth prelude and coda blocks bracket a single shared core iterated R times. Inspired by gated recurrent neural networks, we employ a lightweight projection and an elementwise update gate---conditioned on the hidden state, the fixed prelude output, and noise resampled at every step---to modulate the recurrent update. This allows the model to specialize the input to the same few layers across recurrences, rather than requiring many unique layers to achieve functional diversity. Under an isoFLOPS constraint, a 3-layer RecurrentGPT matches the accuracy of a 12-layer GPT-2 Small baseline with similar training and inference FLOPs, and leads MoR and heavy-tail depth sampling in all nine scale-by-budget cells; at medium and large scale it approaches dense quality at the standard token budget and overtakes it at medium scale once that budget is doubled. Under an isoPARAMS constraint, deeper recurrence achieves a 2.76 validation loss versus 2.84 for a non-recurrent counterpart at matched parameter and data budget. Our results demonstrate that adaptive depth reuse is a principled strategy for trading parameters for quality: at large scale, 63% fewer parameters and 59% less peak decoding memory for a 10% increase in compiled generation latency.

</details>


### [247] [A Pilot Study of Autocompleting Tokenizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15080)
*Samuel Wexler,Mark Hopkins*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于轻量级自回归字节语言模型的输入压缩方法，通过删除能够被上下文预测的字节，减少输入序列长度，在不降低机器翻译质量的前提下提升变换器（Transformer）模型效率。实验证明，在多个语言对上，该方法能在保持甚至提升翻译质量的同时，实现显著的输入压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 字节级分词虽具有语言无关性，但导致输入序列较长，增加了计算成本并可能影响模型性能。现有自动补全技术受益于复原性上下文启发，促使作者探索类似思路在Transformer输入压缩中的可行性。

Method: 作者提出先用轻量级自回归字节语言模型，对源语言序列进行预测，去除容易被上下文预测的字节，压缩后再送入标准的编码-解码Transformer体系中进行机器翻译。该方法无语言依赖性，适用于不同语言类型。

Result: 在英法、芬英、俄英、中英等机器翻译任务上，方案能在不降低或提升翻译质量的前提下，将输入序列长度压缩至原始的47%到67%之间，英法任务中序列平均缩短近三分之一。

Conclusion: 许多输入字节在上下文中具高度可预测性，可通过压缩机制隐式表示，从而有效降低字节级模型的输入长度负担，为提升翻译系统效率提供了简易实用的手段。
