### [320] [Modeling and Control of an Eel-Inspired Soft Robot for Design Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14860)
*Zhangjingyi Jiang,Mark Campbell*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种仿鳗鱼软体机器人运动的仿真模型，通过有限元方法（FEM）结合流体动力学，实现了柔性机器人水中运动的高效模拟，并验证了不同结构与材料设计对性能的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鳗形运动是一种高效的水下游动方式，随着新型软体材料的涌现，有必要开发能有效模拟与优化类似运动方式的软体机器人设计工具。

Method: 采用弹性杆有限元方法（FEM）对软体机器人的材料及其随时间变化的特性进行建模，并将材料模型与水动力学模型耦合，模拟软体仿生机器鱼在水中的真实行为。

Result: 通过仿真分析验证了所提出的控制策略达成目标游动行为的有效性，揭示了略带不对称的设计能提升机器人的灵活性，同时保持高水平的游动速度。

Conclusion: 所构建的仿真模型为软体仿生机器人设计优化和控制提供了强有力的工具，并为今后的特定任务性能优化和材料退化、失效对性能的影响分析提供依据。

Abstract: Anguilliform locomotion is a highly efficient swimming mode; the advent of new materials for soft robots enables the development of an eel-inspired soft robot. This paper presents a simulation model of an eel-inspired soft robot designed for anguilliform swimming. This model can aid in design optimization and the development of model-based estimation, reasoning, and control systems. A Finite Element Method (FEM) model of an elastic rod is used to capture the soft materials of the robotic fish, which makes it particularly amenable to variation over time as the material properties change. The material model is coupled with a hydrodynamic force model to simulate the behavior of a soft, elongated robot in water. The model is used to demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed control approaches in achieving desired swimming behaviors. It also provides insights into design decisions, including the robustness of different system configurations and the impact of material degradation and failure. The results show that slightly asymmetric designs are advantageous, offering comparable swimming velocities but greater maneuverability. This model can be used to guide future robotic design decisions aimed at optimizing performance for specific tasks.

</details>


### [321] [Real-time Estimator of Actuator Control and Health (REACH) on an Eel-Inspired Soft Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14865)
*Zhangjingyi Jiang,Myungsun Park,Michael T. Tolley,Mark Campbell*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于软体游动机器人执行器健康估算的算法REACH，能够实时准确评估因材料老化或损坏导致的执行器性能变化，提升水下机器人任务安全性和返航能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下软体机器人常处于恶劣环境，执行器及材料易发生退化、老化甚至失效，因此必须实时监控评估其健康状况以保障任务顺利完成和及时返航。

Method: 提出REACH框架，结合软体机器人动力学模型、西格玛点滤波器和统计假设检验，针对非线性与时变特性设计健康估算流程。评估了三种传感器类型（GPS、IMU、弯曲传感器），并分析不同传感器数量与布置对算法性能的影响。在多种典型游动姿态下，实验验证算法对各传感器数据的健康预测能力。

Result: 结果显示，IMU和弯曲传感器均能提供足够准确的健康估算，IMU需2个传感器，弯曲传感器需3个，REACH能稳定评估三种游动方式下的执行器健康状况。滤波验证表明算法在判别故障和性能退化时具有统计一致性。对于实际噪声和制造差异，REACH依然可准确估算执行器状态。

Conclusion: REACH算法为软体水下机器人执行器健康监测提供了有效方案，同时具备实时性和鲁棒性，可广泛应用于实际软体游动机器人，提高其安全性与自主作业能力。

Abstract: An actuator health estimation algorithm for a soft swimming robot that can perform anguilliform swimming is developed. Due to harsh operational environments of underwater robots, and the common degradation of soft robot materials and actuators, accurate estimation of actuator functionality is necessary for robots to perform their missions as well as return to base in the event of actuator degradation and failure. Termed REACH (Real-time Estimator of Actuator Control and Health), the architecture employs a soft robot model, sigma point filter, and a formal statistical hypothesis test to adequately capture the nonlinearities and changes over time. The performance of REACH using three sensor types (GPS, IMU, and Bend Sensor) with one sensor on each actuator is compared, demonstrating that both bend sensor and IMU are adequate choices. Sensor quantity and placement are evaluated for IMU and bend sensor, showing two sensors are sufficient for IMU, whereas three sensors are needed for bend sensor. Three swimming gaits (linear swimming, wide turning, tight turning) are compared, demonstrating that REACH can successfully predict actuator health for all three gaits, with minimal differences in performance. A filter validation method shows the fault estimation algorithm is statistically consistent in finding the correct degradation. The approach is experimentally evaluated using bend sensor data collected from a fish robot, demonstrating that REACH can successfully estimate actuator health with noisy data and variations in manufacturing.

</details>


### [322] [Geometry-Aware Online Mapping for 3D Gaussian Splatting SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14902)
*Thai Luu,Quan Tran,Hieu Phan,Tuan Dang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文改进了3D高斯分布绘制（3DGS）用于SLAM系统中的初始化和密度控制，提出三种几何感知方法来提升在线建图的效率和质量，在保持低开销的同时显著提升渲染效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3DGS-SLAM技术大多直接沿用离线重建中的初始化和密度控制策略，但在需实时增量式地图构建和优化资源有限的SLAM环境中，这些启发式方法经常表现不佳，导致地图质量和渲染效果受限。

Method: 作者提出了三项新的方法，分别为：一、保持透射率的稠密化（transmittance-preserving densification）；二、基于相机深度和内参的尺度初始化（camera-aware scale initialization）；三、基于误差引导的稠密化（error-guided densification），主要针对高残差区域优化点的稠密化。这些方法均在SLAM的建图线程中独立实现。

Result: 实验表明，作者的方法在几乎不增加计算负担的前提下，能够持续提升渲染质量，并揭示了光度残差与位姿不确定性在SLAM中的耦合关系。

Conclusion: 改进的初始化和密度控制策略适应了在线SLAM的实际需求，可用于提升3DGS-SLAM系统的建图效果和鲁棒性。相关代码将开源以促进社区发展与方法复现。

Abstract: Recent 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has enabled efficient photorealistic view synthesis and is rapidly being adopted in simultaneous localization and mapping (SLAM) systems for online mapping. In these systems, a Gaussian map must be expanded and refined incrementally while tracking runs in real time, so initialization and density control directly determine where limited computation and iterations are spent. This contrasts with offline 3DGS reconstruction, where such heuristics can be amortized over long optimization schedules. However, most 3DGS-SLAM pipelines inherit initialization and density-control heuristics from offline reconstruction, which can become brittle under the strict per-keyframe optimization budgets and incremental map growth of online SLAM. In this work, we revisit these heuristics in a decoupled 3DGS-SLAM setting and propose three geometry-aware methods that operate in the mapping thread: transmittance-preserving densification, camera-aware scale initialization from depth and intrinsics, and error-guided densification that focuses new primitives on high-residual regions. Our results show consistent improvements in rendering quality with negligible overhead, highlighting the coupling between photometric residuals and pose uncertainty in online SLAM. We will open-source our code to the community to foster growth and validate reproducibility.

</details>


### [323] [From Continuous Design to Delay-Aware Discrete Synthesis: Guaranteed High-Bandwidth Joint Control for PMSM Drives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14937)
*Edmundo Pozo Fortunić,Mehmet C. Yildirim,Sami Haddadin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种考虑延迟的离散时间关节模型，面向现代机器人的高动态需求，直接设计具备带宽和延迟保证的PI电流控制器，并在仿真和实验中显著优化了采样频率和母线电压要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PMSM电流控制器多以连续时间设计再离散化，忽略了实际通信与计算带来的离散效应，导致带宽和性能受限，无法满足现代机器人高性能需求，因此需要新的控制框架。

Method: 提出了基于任务需求、扩展延迟的离散时间关节模型，显式计入通信和计算延迟，直接合成具备指定带宽和延迟保证的PI电流控制器，并推导所需采样频率、控制参数及母线电压的解析计算方法。通过广泛的仿真和定制机器人关节实物实验验证方法有效性。

Result: 新方法能显著降低控制所需的采样频率和母线电压，同时满足设定的带宽和延迟性能指标，在各类动态需求下均表现优异。

Conclusion: 所提离散延迟控制框架能有效解决实际实现中的控制带宽与资源消耗矛盾，为高动态机器人关节电流控制器设计提供了理论和工程依据。

Abstract: The increasing dynamic demands of modern robotic joints require current controllers to achieve high bandwidth over wide operating ranges of speed, acceleration, and torque, where communication, computation, and discrete-time effects can no longer be neglected. Conventional PMSM current controllers are typically designed in continuous time and subsequently discretized, leaving the sampling frequency and the impact of implementation delays largely to heuristic selection and iterative validation. This paper introduces a task-aware, delay-extended discrete-time joint model that explicitly accounts for physical communication and computation delays and enables direct synthesis of a discrete PI current controller with prescribed bandwidth and delay guarantees throughout the operating envelope. The framework analytically determines the minimum required sampling frequency, controller gains, and DC-link voltage needed to satisfy the specified motor and joint performance. Simulations across a range of dynamic requirements validate the methodology and demonstrate substantially reduced sampling-frequency and DC-link-voltage requirements compared with conventional continuous-time-based design. Experiments on a newly developed custom robotic joint further validate the proposed framework under real embedded implementation conditions.

</details>


### [324] [SkillComposer: Learning Reusable Skills for Natural-Language Robot Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14944)
*John Woods,Hasti Seifi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SkillComposer是一种结合大语言模型与可复用程序抽象的自然语言机器人编程系统，通过不断学习和复用先前的程序片段，显著提升了机器人完成复杂任务的能力和自然语言编程的易用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自然语言机器人编程系统对复杂、多步骤任务的处理能力有限，尤其在自动分解高层指令和复用历史方案方面存在不足。论文希望打破现有系统的局限，提升复杂任务编程效率和用户体验。

Method: 提出SkillComposer系统，采用大语言模型的“生成-测试”架构：LLM生成和修订机器人程序，执行成功后利用在线学习算法将重复出现的函数序列压缩为可复用宏技能，不断扩充技能库。通过消融实验和12人用户研究评估系统性能。

Result: 实验显示，SkillComposer所引入的评估者引导生成策略及学习到的程序抽象明显提升了任务成功率和系统可用性，降低了用户的编程负担。

Conclusion: SkillComposer通过持续学习宏技能，改善了自然语言机器人编程的复杂任务处理能力，为基于自然语言的人机协作和机器人应用推广带来了积极影响。

Abstract: Natural-language interfaces can lower the barrier to programming robots, but existing systems struggle when users request complex tasks. While large language models (LLMs) perform well with simple commands, they often struggle to generate code for multi-step tasks, decompose high-level instructions, or reuse prior solutions. We present SkillComposer, an interactive natural-language robot programming system for simulation environments that continually learns reusable program abstractions. SkillComposer uses a generate-test architecture in which an LLM iteratively generates and revises robot programs before execution. Successful programs are stored and processed by an online library-learning algorithm that compresses recurring function sequences into reusable macro skills for future tasks. We evaluate SkillComposer through ablation experiments and a user study with 12 participants to determine its effectiveness on manipulation and robot caregiving tasks. The results show that evaluator-guided generation and learned abstractions improve success rates and usability while reducing user effort in natural-language robot programming.

</details>


### [325] [Evidence of Absence: Cross-Modal Abductive Risk Perception to Sustain World Models When Vision Fails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14952)
*Cong Xu,Ravi Sankar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在视觉感知受阻时利用声音信号进行环境建模的推理方法，通过麦克风阵列检测交通场景中引擎和轮胎噪音，在缺乏视觉佐证时进行声学溯因推断，有效提升了在遮挡路口预警路上隐藏危险物体（如车辆或行人）的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶和高级驾驶辅助系统高度依赖视觉感知。然而在视觉被遮挡或受损时，关键的环境信息可能丢失，带来安全风险。为了在视觉不可用时补充环境理解，本文探索如何用声学信息弥补视觉缺失。

Method: 本文提出了一种模态无关的溯因框架，并以声学为例进行具体实现。通过麦克风阵列估计车辆引擎和轮胎声源方位，并提取接近速率（利用多普勒效应或宽带音频信号），遇到“声学信号存在但缺少视觉佐证”时，触发对隐藏交通参与者的溯因推断，发出风险提示。同时分析了隐藏状态的可恢复性和风险提示的检出率/误报率问题，并在真实遮挡场景下测试性能。

Result: 实验证明，该方法在遮挡路口可在目标入视野前平均提前1.7秒预警，检测率与已有声学基线方法持平、误报减少42%，方位定位中位数误差仅3.4度，风险提示校准良好并能在严重视觉退化时维持高风险感知（>0.87），远超纯视觉方案。方法在新场景下具有良好泛化性，但声音特征分类器迁移较差，且自车噪声是最主要的部署限制。

Conclusion: 结合声学溯因推理可显著提升自动驾驶系统在视觉受损下的安全隐患感知能力。声学校准具有良好泛化潜力，但实际部署仍需关注自车噪声对性能的影响。

Abstract: A structured world-state (entities, relations, context, and predictive cues) is designed to preserve prediction-critical content when perception degrades, but it presumes observations to populate it; when the primary visual modality is occluded or degraded, those observations may be missing. We address how to sustain the world model from a complementary modality by treating the absence of expected co-evidence as evidence of a hidden cause. The abductive framework is modality-agnostic; this article instantiates it acoustically. A microphone-array front-end estimates the bearing of engine and tire sources and extracts approach-rate evidence (Doppler when a stable tone exists, a broadband looming readout otherwise); the event "signature present, visual co-evidence absent" then triggers abductive inference of a hidden road user, emitting a calibrated risk advisory rather than a control command. Recoverability of the hidden state is analyzed as an identifiability question separating shared from modality-unique information, and cueing is cast as Neyman-Pearson detection under an explicit false-alarm budget. On real occluded-approach recordings at blind junctions, the method warns a mean 1.7 seconds before line-of-sight entry, matches the sustained-window variant of the published acoustic baseline's detection rate with 42% fewer false alarms, localizes to 3.4 degrees median once in view, is well calibrated (expected calibration error 0.034), and keeps hazard awareness above 0.87 under staged vision degradation that collapses a vision-only channel to 0.03. We also measure the method's limits: calibration transfers to an unseen junction almost losslessly, the signature classifier does not, and moving-ego noise is the binding deployment constraint.

</details>


### [326] [GaussMemory: Task-Driven 3D Gaussian Scene Memory for Long-Horizon Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14986)
*Zhiqiang Hu,Shouren Huang,Masatoshi Ishikawa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种主动、任务驱动的空间记忆机制GaussMemory，用于提升机器人长期操作任务中的表现，突破以往被动、固定规则存储手法，实现根据任务自动学习与更新记忆。实验表明其显著优于现有主流方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D记忆系统仅做被动、平等观察记录，无法区分重要性，造成信息冗余和关键元素忽略，无法适应复杂长期机器人操作任务需求。作者希望让机器人记忆具备主动性和任务相关性，提高效率与灵活性。

Method: 提出主动、端到端学习的空间记忆机制GaussMemory，将3D高斯投影（Gaussian Splatting）作为记忆体。记忆的更新和读取紧密结合，允许任务需求动态指导记忆内容的保留、更新或丢弃，无需人工固定规则。

Result: 在LIBERO数据集上，GaussMemory比MemoryVLA在目标和Long-10任务上表现更佳；在VLABench数据集上，相较π_0-FAST分别在Track 1和Track 6提升5.2%和6.0%。

Conclusion: 本文提出主动型空间记忆范式能更好适应机器人长时操作任务，显著优于传统被动型系统，为机器人自适应场景与任务感知、记忆管理提供新思路。

Abstract: Long-horizon robotic manipulation fundamentally relies on persistent spatial memory. However, existing 3D memory systems function merely as passive recorders: they store observations using fixed, hand-crafted rules, treating every scene element--whether a critical grasp target or an irrelevant background wall--with equal importance. In this paper, we propose a paradigm shift from passive storage to active, task-driven spatial memory. We argue that a robot's memory should not simply record what it sees, but actively learn how to remember--discovering which objects to track precisely, how aggressively to update them, and what to discard, all learned end-to-end without hand-designed rules. Crucially, this active paradigm is realized by unifying memory update and readout as two sides of the same cognitive process, enabling bidirectional flow where task needs shape update strategies and vice versa. To instantiate this vision, we introduce GaussMemory, which leverages 3D Gaussian Splatting as a persistent geometric substrate. On LIBERO, GaussMemory outperforms MemoryVLA on Goal and Long-10; on VLABench, it surpasses $π_0$-FAST by +5.2% (Track 1) and +6.0% (Track 6).

</details>


### [327] [HP2-SLAM: Adaptive Hybrid ICP for Robust and Efficient LiDAR SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14996)
*Nam Tran,Thu Tran,Hieu Phan,Thai Luu,Toan Nguyen,William J. Beksi,Tuan Dang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为HP2-SLAM的新型LiDAR SLAM系统，使用自适应的几何方法显著提升了在多种场景下鲁棒性、精度与效率，且无需学习模块或大量调优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的方法虽性能突出，但需大量数据与算力且泛化性不足；传统几何法虽然高效，但在平面或重复结构环境下表现恶化。因此亟需一种兼具鲁棒性、准确性和高效性的SLAM方案。

Method: HP2-SLAM框架核心为邻域规模自适应的混合ICP配准方法，通过引入考虑局部几何结构与密度的自适应阈值，合理融合点到面与点到点残差，无需手工特征或训练。整个系统集成有子地图管理、回环检测与位姿图优化。

Result: HP2-SLAM在多个公开数据集上超越了主流几何基准，且可在普通计算平台实现实时运算，展现出强泛化与高鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 精心设计的几何自适应策略能凭借简洁效率，在无需复杂学习或特定调参的前提下，实现SLAM系统的高性能与强泛化。

Abstract: Achieving robustness, accuracy, and efficiency simultaneously remains a central challenge in light detection and ranging (LiDAR) simultaneous localization and mapping (SLAM). While learning-based approaches deliver strong benchmark performance, they often require extensive training, substantial computational resources, and struggle to generalize to unseen or degenerate environments. Geometry-based methods are efficient and interpretable, yet their performance degrades in planar or repetitive scenes due to limitations of standard iterative closest point (ICP) formulations. We present HP2-SLAM, a minimalist yet robust LiDAR SLAM framework built around a neighborhood-size adaptive hybrid ICP. Our key insight is a planarity-aware adaptive threshold that dynamically classifies correspondences based on local geometric structure and density, thereby enabling a principled balance between point-to-plane and point-to-point residuals. This formulation stabilizes alignment in both structured and degenerate environments without feature engineering, learning modules, or dataset-specific tuning. Integrated into a complete SLAM pipeline with submap management, loop closure detection, and pose graph optimization, HP2-SLAM consistently outperforms strong geometry-based baselines across publicly available datasets while maintaining real-time performance on commodity hardware. Our results demonstrate that carefully designed geometric adaptation can achieve strong generalization and robustness without sacrificing simplicity or efficiency.

</details>


### [328] [NPU Offloading of a Frozen Visual Encoder for Robot Policy Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15002)
*Hyojun Yun,Seungjae Won,Hyungpil Moon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了在训练机器人策略时，将冻结的视觉编码器前向运算迁移到低功耗AI加速器（如NPU）能否降低整体能耗。结果显示能耗下降，但训练时间变长，策略性能略有下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于冻结视觉编码器可以节省训练计算资源，但其前向运算仍需要在每一步执行，因此依然消耗GPU算力。作者探讨是否将这部分计算迁移到NPU能够有效降低能耗，同时评估对训练效率和策略性能的影响。

Method: 作者搭建了异步训练流水线，将冻结的视觉编码器在NPU上用A8W8 INT8精度运行，策略生成则在GPU上以FP32训练。实验设计包含GPU-only基线与四组NPU offload条件（L1-L4），逐步增加NPU处理的Transformer编码层数量。每组训练3个随机种子，各3万步，并评估能耗、训练时长、显存占用和策略表现。

Result: 实验发现，NPU接管越多编码层，单位样本能耗下降越大（最大降至27.9%），但训练时间相应提升（最长增加37.7%），显存占用下降约20%。策略在各环境下评估发现，转移到NPU后成功率有所下降（最多1.89个百分点）。

Conclusion: 将冻结视觉编码器的前向计算迁移到NPU确实可以显著降低训练能耗，同时减少GPU显存压力，但会拉长训练时间，并使机器人策略表现略有下降。整体权衡需根据实际需求进行选择。

Abstract: When a robot policy is trained for a new task or dataset, its visual encoder can be frozen and only its action generation module trained, reducing training cost. Freezing removes the encoder's backward pass, but its forward pass must still run at every training step because the input images change, so it keeps consuming GPU compute. We therefore ask whether moving this computation to a low power AI accelerator such as an NPU can reduce total energy despite the added data transfer and longer training time, and how it affects policy performance. We built an asynchronous training pipeline that uses both a GPU and an NPU for the AR-Actor specialist. The frozen visual encoder runs in A8W8 INT8 on a Mobilint Aries2 NPU, while the FP32 action expert is trained on an NVIDIA GeForce RTX 5060 Ti GPU. We compared a GPU-only baseline with four conditions, L1 to L4, which gradually extend NPU offloading from one to four Transformer encoder layers. Each condition was trained for 30,000 steps with three random seeds. We measured GPU board power for the GPU-only condition and combined GPU and NPU board power for the NPU conditions. Energy per sample decreased by 17.1% in L1, which offloaded ResNet18 and the first encoder layer, and by 27.9% in L4, which offloaded ResNet18 and all four encoder layers. In contrast, training time per sample increased by 15.2% in L1 and 37.7% in L4, and peak allocated GPU memory decreased by 19.8 to 20.7%. The 15 resulting policies were each evaluated with the same 300 environment seeds, for a total of 4,500 simulator rollouts. The combined success rate was 93.33% for GPU-only and 91.44 to 92.89% for the NPU conditions. These results show that NPU offloading of a frozen visual encoder can reduce training energy, but it increases training time and lowers policy success rate by 0.44 to 1.89 percentage points compared with GPU-only training.

</details>


### [329] [ForceU-VLA: A Force-Aware Vision-Language-Action Model for Embodied Ultrasound Scanning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15009)
*Xingzheng Wu,Cheng Zhang,Guihao Yan,Xifeng Hu,Zhi Liu,Qing Cai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为ForceU-VLA的力觉感知视觉-语言-动作模型，实现了自主超声扫描的新突破，提高了成像质量及扫描稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的超声自主扫描方法在力和超声图像数据的结合上耦合不够紧密，同时缺乏扫描过程阶段感知，导致对探头与组织间动态交互的捕捉能力不足，影响了自动化扫描的准确性和可靠性。

Method: 提出了ForceU-VLA模型，结合力觉信号与超声图像反馈。设计了两个核心模块：Force-Ultrasound Synergistic Fusion Module（FUSFM）用于高效融合多模态信息，Stage-Adaptive Modulation Mechanism（SAMM）针对不同扫描阶段自适应调整特征。并发布了包含力量、视觉和动作信号的大规模真实数据集ForceU-VLA-Data，涵盖两个器官五个临床视图约十万帧数据。

Result: 实验显示，ForceU-VLA模型在自主超声扫描任务上显著提升了探头接触的稳定性以及压力调节能力，任务执行和系统整体可靠性均有明显增强。

Conclusion: ForceU-VLA创新性地实现了力觉感知的多模态自主超声扫描，不仅提升了超声扫描的标准化和自动化水平，也为后续智能医疗设备的研发提供了重要方向和开放数据资源。

Abstract: Embodied intelligent ultrasound scanning enables the automation and standardization of the ultrasound examination process by integrating perception, decision-making, and execution capabilities. However, existing methods suffer from loosely coupled modeling between force and ultrasound modalities and lack awareness of scanning stages, which limits their ability to capture dynamic probe-tissue interactions. To address these issues, we propose ForceU-VLA, a force-aware Vision-Language-Action model for autonomous embodied ultrasound scanning, which leverages force signals and ultrasound image feedback throughout the scanning process to enable accurate and high-quality ultrasound acquisition. Firstly, we propose a Force-Ultrasound Synergistic Fusion Module (FUSFM) that synergistically fuses ultrasound visual and force-feedback information to provide stable, reliable guidance for probe motion. Secondly, a Stage-Adaptive Modulation Mechanism (SAMM) is proposed to accommodate the task requirements across different scanning stages by adaptively modulating multimodal features to enhance their representation quality. Additionally, we introduce ForceU-VLA-Data, a real-world, force-aware embodied ultrasound dataset that integrates visual, force, and action signals, including data from two organs across five representative clinical scanning views, and comprising 450 expert-collected trajectories with approximately 100,000 synchronized multimodal frames. Extensive experimental results demonstrate that ForceU-VLA significantly improves contact stability and probe pressure regulation in embodied ultrasound scanning, thereby effectively enhancing task execution quality and overall system reliability. The source code is available at https://github.com/VMVLab/ForceU-VLA.

</details>


### [330] [MotionGS-SLAM: Event-Modulated Gaussian Splatting for Motion-Blur Robust SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15024)
*Zhiqiang Hu,Shouren Huang,Masatoshi Ishikawa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MotionGS-SLAM提出了一种新方法，在出现运动模糊的情况下，通过物理建模而非去模糊恢复，有效提升了SLAM系统在极端运动模糊下的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉SLAM系统在遇到运动模糊时表现极差，因为它们要逆向“推断”出清晰图像，这本质上是一个病态问题。因此亟需一种根本不同的方式来提升鲁棒性，尤其是在高运动环境下。

Method: 作者引入了MotionGS-SLAM方法，将运动模糊作为一个正向物理成像过程建模而非逆向去除。他们结合事件相机的高时间分辨率特性，设计了基于运动信息自适应的高斯核以及双重调制机制：一是将2D高斯从各向同性点变为与运动方向对齐的各向异性椭圆刷痕（空间调制）；二是根据局部速度改变曝光采样密度（时间调制）。系统通过光度和事件约束联合优化相机轨迹与三维场景。

Result: 在大量高运动模糊场景下，MotionGS-SLAM在轨迹精度和地图质量方面显著优于现有主流方法。

Conclusion: 采用面向物理建模的运动模糊机制并充分利用事件相机信息，能够极大提升SLAM系统在极端运动模糊下的鲁棒性和准确性。

Abstract: Current Vision-based SLAM systems fail catastrophically when motion blur corrupts the visual input, as they attempt the ill-posed inverse problem of recovering sharp content from degraded observations. We present MotionGS-SLAM, which fundamentally reimagines motion blur handling through a paradigm shift: rather than removing blur artifacts, we reformulate the challenge as a well-constrained forward problem that generatively models blur formation within the rendering pipeline. By leveraging event cameras' microsecond temporal resolution and immunity to motion blur, we introduce a novel event-modulated Gaussian kernel that dynamically adapts each Gaussian's rasterization based on precise motion cues. Our dual-modulation mechanism transforms 2D Gaussian projections from isotropic dots into anisotropic, motion-aligned elliptical brush strokes (spatial modulation) while adaptively varying exposure integral sampling density based on local velocity (temporal modulation). This physics-based approach enables joint optimization of intra-exposure camera trajectories and 3D scene geometry through blur-aware photometric and event-based constraints. Extensive experiments demonstrate significant improvements over state-of-the-art methods in trajectory accuracy and map quality under severe high-motion conditions.

</details>


### [331] [PACE: Phase-Progress-Aware Credit for Long-Horizon Embodied Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15026)
*Chengye Song,Jiawei Zhang,Rui Song,Shengqi Wang,Xiangrong Zhang,Ziyi Wang,Huanbin Zhou,Hongzhou Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PACE是一种用于长时序操作任务的视觉-语言-动作(VLA)模型后训练的信用分配框架，通过细粒度的步骤级反馈信号提升策略表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时序操作任务的结局常常只有在整个任务完成后才可知，且任务过程极长，无法简单根据成功与否对中间步骤进行有效指导，急需能够细粒度信用分配的方法提升学习效率和效果。

Method: PACE框架包含两大模块：1. GLC-Critic会在局部时间窗口内聚合视觉和运动特征，推断当前步骤所处阶段及阶段内进程，并用残差校正的方式对剩余成本做分布化离散修正，实现有效的步骤级信用分配；2. PPD通过设定任务门限，将信用信号转变为正、负两类条件，然后首先利用高信用正样本保护预训练策略，接着用所有样本细化区分质量边界，最终推理时用正负条件输出之差放大高信用动作。

Result: 在大量仿真实验和多样化真实机器人臂实验中，PACE相较于现有最强的基线方法均持续取得显著性能提升。

Conclusion: PACE能实现更有效的步骤级信用分配，显著提升长时序VLA模型在复杂操作任务中的策略学习与泛化能力。

Abstract: Post-training of vision-language-action (VLA) models typically relies on expert demonstrations and policy interaction trajectories. However, in long-horizon manipulation, a single episode often spans hundreds of control steps and multiple phases, while success or failure is only revealed at episode termination. Policy improvement therefore requires step-level credit signals to distinguish behaviors that advance the task from those that stall or regress. We present PACE, a credit-assignment framework for post-training on long-horizon manipulation, centered on a phase-progress-aware critic. PACE consists of two key modules: (1) the Global-Local Cooperative Value-Correction Critic (GLC-Critic) aggregates visual and motion-difference features within local temporal windows to infer the phase and intra-phase progress of each step, and applies residual correction to a discretized remaining-cost distribution accordingly, enabling step-level credit assignment; (2) Progressive Policy Distillation (PPD) converts credit into positive and negative conditions via task-wise thresholds and trains a credit-conditioned action generation policy: it first protects the pretrained policy with high-credit positive samples, then incorporates all positive and negative credits to learn the quality boundary, and at inference amplifies high-credit behaviors through the difference between conditional outputs. Extensive simulation experiments and diverse real-world robotic-arm experiments demonstrate that PACE consistently achieves significant improvements over the strongest baseline.

</details>


### [332] [Max-Q Selective Imitation for Human-in-the-Loop Online Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15088)
*Zihang Wang,Yishan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对真实机器人的人类在环 (HIL) 在线强化学习训练方法，通过MC Q-chunk评论器和max-Q选择性模仿机制，大幅提升了吸收人类干预和自我提升的效率。在实物和仿真任务上，该方法明显优于现有方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实机器人场景下，人工干预数据稀缺且采集成本高，人机交互强化学习需快速有效地吸收有限人类经验，并能自行超越人类演示水平。现有方法在干预和自学间平衡、分布漂移等方面存在局限，且学习速度缓慢。

Method: 1）提出MC Q-chunk评论器，将chunk级动作价值直接回归到蒙特卡洛回报，实现基于经验回放的人为干预高效信用分配；2）max-Q选择性模仿：在每个状态下只模仿Q值更高（当前策略或回放样本）的动作，按硬性赢家通吃规则切换人类演示与自学，自动适应策略强度，并用评论器集合均值降噪，无引入其他松弛或阈值。

Result: 在真实USB插拔任务，20次人类演示后，ACT QChunk-MCBC方法30分钟内成功率达99%，大幅优于HIL-SERL需5小时收敛。在Peg Insertion等仿真任务上，ACT/Flow Q-chunk变种同样在半小时实现≥96%成功率，整体优于HIL-SERL、EXPO、E2HiL等基线。

Conclusion: 所提方法在有限人类数据和真实在线环境下，能高效快速实现师生切换与自我优化，比主流HIL RL方法收敛快、成功率高，对机器人实际部署有很大应用潜力。

Abstract: Human-in-the-loop (HIL) online reinforcement learning for real robots must absorb human interventions quickly while continuing to improve beyond the human prior. We present a training method for this setting based on two components. First, an \emph{MC Q-chunk} critic regresses chunk-level action values onto Monte Carlo returns from the replay buffer, performing sample-average (behavior) policy evaluation so that intervention trajectories are credited directly rather than diluted by current-policy TD backups. Second, \emph{max-Q selective imitation} updates the actor by imitating, at each state, the higher-$Q$ action between the current policy action and a buffer sample under a hard winner-take-all rule. This rule automatically switches between learning from interventions and on-policy self-improvement: when the autonomous policy is stronger, targets align with the policy distribution, reducing the policy--target-sample gap that otherwise induces execution-time distribution shift. In practice we score candidates with a standard critic ensemble mean to reduce comparison noise, without softening targets or introducing score-gap thresholds. On a real USB pick-and-insertion task with 20 demonstrations, ACT QChunk-MCBC attains 99\% success within 30 minutes of HIL training, whereas HIL-SERL requires about 5 hours to converge. In simulation on Peg Insertion and Square, ACT/Flow Q-chunk variants similarly reach $\ge$96\% success within roughly half an hour of effective training, outperforming HIL-SERL, EXPO, and E2HiL on the success--time frontier.

</details>


### [333] [StructRL: Structured Action-Space Exploration for Flow-Based VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15139)
*Jiarui Yang,Bin Zhu,Jingjing Chen,Na Zou,Yanwei Fu,Jianggang Zhu,Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了StructRL方法，通过在动作空间中直接注入结构化噪声，提升基于流的视觉-语言-动作模型在强化学习任务中的探索效率和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于流的VLA模型使用在线强化学习适配新任务时，常用各向同性或时间独立噪声推进探索，但这不适合机器人需要的结构化、时序平滑且按动作组缩放的噪声，因此迫切需要新的探索方式。

Method: 作者发现：仅在中间链路中注入结构化噪声会被去噪步骤削弱（结构化噪声稀释）。为此，StructRL做出三项创新：① 采用确定性ODE解码器；② 直接在动作空间中注入结构化噪声；③ 最后一步回放，只在最终动作上计算策略梯度，避免对中间状态赋予概率。

Result: 在三个主流VLA模型、多项仿真实验和两个真实机器人任务上，StructRL相比传统方法显著提升了探索效率和OOD（分布外）表现。

Conclusion: 结构化动作空间探索法能有效提升基于流的VLA模型强化学习适应能力，验证了StructRL的有效性。

Abstract: Flow-based Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are now widely used for continuous robotic manipulation, and online reinforcement learning (RL) is emerging as a key technique for adapting them to new tasks. Existing RL methods typically inject stochasticity inside the denoising chain, often through isotropic or temporally independent noise. However, effective robot exploration calls for structured noise: temporally smooth and scaled differently across action groups. We show that simply switching the in-chain noise to a structured form does not suffice: noise added at an intermediate flow time can be weakened by the remaining denoising steps before execution, a phenomenon we call \emph{Structured Noise Dilution}. We propose \textbf{StructRL}, which avoids dilution by relocating policy stochasticity to the action space via three coupled choices: (i) a deterministic ODE decoder, (ii) structured noise injected directly in the action space, and (iii) last-step replay, where policy-gradient updates avoid assigning likelihoods to intermediate denoising states. This keeps structured exploration tied to the executed action while providing a tractable training signal for the flow decoder. Across three flow-based VLA models on multiple simulated manipulation benchmarks and two real-world tasks, StructRL improves exploration efficiency and OOD performance over prior in-chain baselines, demonstrating the effectiveness of structured action-space exploration for adapting flow-based VLA with RL. \textbf{Project page:} https://flyfaerss.github.io/structrl/

</details>


### [334] [Low-Rank Dynamics-Effective Latent Carriers for Counterfactual Rollout in Learned World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15156)
*Yang Liu,Yuming Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文探讨了世界模型隐藏状态的可控干预能力，特别是在预测未来轨迹时能否通过小规模且明确可操作的隐藏状态修改实现目标反事实轨迹。通过对可控碰撞环境下的RNN世界模型实验，发现只需低秩（秩为4）的补丁干预即可有效改变模型的自主预测路径，验证了干预的动态效力与稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前世界模型能够预测未来，但隐藏状态的哪些部分具体驱动预测仍不明确。本研究希望明确隐藏状态的具体干预方式，验证通过有限的修改是否能实现对未来预测轨迹的有效掌控，从而推动模型可解释性与可控性的提升。

Method: 作者构建了一个带192维隐藏状态的循环神经网络世界模型，在二维、两个物体的可控碰撞环境中进行实验。首先验证模型原生支持局部速度值编辑的反事实推广能力。随后，通过训练阶段获得的事实与反事实隐藏状态差值，构建低秩补丁载体，并学习从事实状态和目标编辑到补丁系数的映射。实验测试不同秩的效果，分析干预点和干预时刻的通用性与鲁棒性。

Result: 实验发现，用秩为4的低秩补丁即可在无需未来观测、强制教师信号或多次修正的情况下，有效引导模型连续12步的自主预测，且跨不同模型训练检查点具有稳健性。对照实验（错误对象、错误时刻、随机等）无类似效果。对位置编辑的压力测试显示，只有速度类补丁才具备特定、持续的动态效力。

Conclusion: 论文表明，仅仅能编辑模型预测结果并不足够，关键是要实现持续且目标特异的动力学影响。秩-4补丁为速度编辑类任务提供了紧凑、可控的干预接口，但并不意味着找到了一组封闭的隐藏状态或固有状态维度。

Abstract: World models may predict the future without making clear which parts of their hidden state actually drive those predictions. We ask whether a small, directly addressable hidden-state change can place a learned world model on the intended counterfactual trajectory and then let the model continue that future on its own. We study a recurrent world model with a 192-dimensional hidden state in a controlled two-object, two-dimensional collision environment. For a bounded family of local velocity edits, we first verify that the model can natively represent and roll out the edited future. We then construct candidate low-rank carriers from training-only factual-to-counterfactual hidden differences and learn a map from the factual state and requested edit to carrier coefficients. On the registered rank grid, rank 4 is the smallest tested rank that satisfies the full development-panel criteria. A single rank-4 patch at the anchor is sufficient to redirect a 12-step autonomous rollout, with no future observations, teacher forcing, or repeated correction. The frozen procedure satisfies the preregistered replication rule across independently trained checkpoints and remains usable across nearby intervention times. Random equal-norm, wrong-object, and wrong-time controls do not explain the effect. A position-edit stress test provides a negative contrast: the intended position patch can pass the raw rollout criteria, but no-patch and random controls can pass the same criteria, and wrong-object specificity is not established. Thus, successful editing alone is not enough. We use dynamics-effective to describe an intervention that changes the model's future computation in a sustained and target-specific way under autonomous rollout. The rank-4 result identifies a compact intervention interface for the tested velocity-edit family, not a closed four-dimensional state or an intrinsic state dimension.

</details>


### [335] [LAPF: LLM-Agent-Based Path Finder Using the UAVScenes Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15175)
*Yousef Emami,Mohammadhossein Homaei,Hao Zhou,Miguel Gutiérrez Gaitán,Atefeh Hajijamali Arani,Rui Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于大语言模型代理（LLM-Agent-Based Path Finder, LAPF）的无人机自主导航新框架，有效提升了复杂户外环境中导航的智能性和适应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无人机自主导航方法（如传统优化、ML和RL）依赖预先定义的模型或特定任务训练，导致其在不确定性环境中的泛化与自适应能力有限。近期LLM赋能的导航方法虽具备推理优势，但因代理功能不足（记忆、规划与工具交互等）影响实际应用。因此，亟需一种具备更完善模块、更高复杂场景适应性的架构。

Method: 提出LAPF框架，将感知、记忆、规划和行动模块整合入闭环认知结构，并利用LLM的Chain-of-Thought（CoT）推理能力赋能无人机：通过先前经验与环境反馈持续动态优化航点决策，并将检测到的每个风险与明确的纠正措施绑定，实现自主、闭环的导航决策。

Result: 在城镇尺度户外环境下，LAPF在三次独立试验中，路径长度分别为512.83m和506.37m，接近理想直线路径497.33m，并分别比CoT方法路径缩短17.2%和15.6%，路径效率分别达97.1%、98.1%。LAPF唯一能将每一处风险与纠正动作绑定且无失控事件，表现优于其他方案。

Conclusion: LAPF不仅大幅提升了无人机在复杂环境中的自主适应性和导航效率，还实现了高安全性与稳定性，为大规模无人机自治导航提供了新范式。

Abstract: Uncrewed aerial vehicles (UAVs) are increasingly deployed for autonomous navigation in complex outdoor environments, where dynamic conditions and mission requirements require intelligent adaptive decision-making. Existing optimization-based, Machine Learning (ML), and Reinforcement Learning (RL) approaches often rely on predefined models or task-specific training, limiting their generalization and adaptability in uncertain scenarios. Recent Large Language Model (LLM)-assisted approaches offer promising reasoning capabilities but remain constrained by limited agentic functionality, including insufficient memory, planning, and tool interaction mechanisms.This paper proposes an LLM-Agent-Based Path Finder (LAPF) framework for autonomous UAV navigation in town-scale outdoor environments. LAPF extends LLM-assisted navigation by integrating perception, memory, planning, and action modules into a closed-loop cognitive architecture. The proposed agent leverages prior navigation experiences, performs Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning, couples each detected hazard to a bounded corrective action, and dynamically refines waypoint decisions based on environmental feedback.The three independent trials per method demonstrate that LAPF achieves mean path lengths of 512.83 m and 506.37 m, compared to the straight-line optimum of 497.33 m, corresponding to path length reductions of 17.2% and 15.6% relative to CoT prompting and absolute path efficiencies of 97.1% and 98.1% in open-field and obstacle-injected scenarios, respectively. Furthermore, LAPF is the only evaluated approach that couples every detected hazard to a bounded, metric-neutral corrective action while maintaining near-goal stability, with zero clamp events in both scenarios, whereas CoT prompting increases from 9.7 to 14.0 events.

</details>


### [336] [Remember Smarter: Visual History Compressor and Hyperbolic Experience Space for Robotic Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15269)
*Dai Zhou,Jiexi Yan,Tong Li,Yuxuan Wang,Cheng Deng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了Remember Smarter (RS)模块，通过高效的视觉历史压缩和超球经验记忆，实现机器人长期任务中的精简记忆与经验复用，大幅提升机器人任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人在处理长时序任务时，需要访问大量近期观测数据和历史经验，但受限于视觉-语言-动作(VLA)上下文容量，导致表现受限。为实现更好的记忆压缩与经验利用，提出新结构以提升长期任务性能。

Method: RS模块包含两个分支：视觉历史分支通过空间Mamba和时序Mamba对多视角视觉数据压缩，再通过残差交互注意力与动作模块衔接；经验分支将成功的视觉-语言模型(VLM)状态存储于双曲(VAE)空间中，层级化管理，并在无需阻塞推理的情况下异步转化为地质化提示(token)以供使用。

Result: RS适配pi0后，在LIBERO-Plus任务总成功率由53.6%提升至70.6%，在真实机器人实验中表现出显著的记忆保持和经验利用效果提升。

Conclusion: RS模块有效解决了长时序任务中上下文容量瓶颈，增强了机器人对视觉历史和经验的利用能力，显著提升了机器人任务执行的成功率与经验复用效率。

Abstract: Long-horizon robot policies require compact access to recent observations and
  reusable experience without expanding the vision-language-action (VLA)
  context. We introduce Remember Smarter (RS), a plug-and-play module with
  complementary visual-history and hyperbolic experience-memory branches. Its
  visual branch compresses multi-view patch histories using bidirectional
  spatial Mamba and causal temporal Mamba, then exposes the resulting memory to
  action-facing hidden states through residual cross-attention while leaving the
  VLM visual-token stream unchanged. Its experience branch stores successful
  final-layer VLM states in a Poincare VAE space, organizes them hierarchically,
  and asynchronously converts retrieved experience into geodesic prompt tokens
  without blocking action inference. When adapted to pi0, RS increases total
  success on LIBERO-Plus from 53.6% to 70.6% and
  achieves substantial
  performance gains in real-robot experiments designed to evaluate memory
  retention and experience utilization.

</details>


### [337] [VTInstructor: Visual Trajectory Prompting for Navigation Instruction Generation in Continuous Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15284)
*Haolin Yang,Yuxing Long,Zihan Yang,Hao Dong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个面向连续环境的视觉-语言导航(VLN)指令生成框架VTInstructor，有效提升了无导航图和场景重建下的机器指令生成和下游导航表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在连续环境下从自我视角RGB视频生成导航指令对于人机交互和自动化数据集构建非常重要，但当前方法依赖离散视点图或全景观察，难以适用连续环境。

Method: 引入VTInstructor框架，核心是通过EDTC将长RGB轨迹压缩为关键帧，VTP在这些关键帧上显式叠加路径、转弯、目标提示，VTMod将这些提示注入视觉编码器，VT-GRPO训练阶段空间校准，无需导航图、预建地图或场景重建。

Result: 在R2R-CE和RxR-CE Val Unseen测试集上，各项NLG指标创下新纪录，相比最强基线CIDEr分别提升+0.357和+0.109。同时，自动生成的指令使‘冻结跟随者’的成功率提升到63.3%，比次优方法高+14.7百分点，并可带来下游导航任务SR提升+3。

Conclusion: VTInstructor有效解决了连续环境下导航指令生成的关键挑战，在自动评测和人/机器人执行层面都显著优于以往方案。

Abstract: Navigation instruction generation from ego-centric RGB video in continuous environments is an important yet challenging task for human-robot interaction and scalable dataset construction. Prior instruction generators assume discrete viewpoint graphs with panoramic observations, where trajectory structure is explicit; in continuous environments, however, the agent receives only a dense RGB stream, making trajectory cues difficult to recover. We propose VTInstructor, the first VLN instruction generation framework for continuous environments. Our key idea is to convert implicit trajectory geometry into explicit visual trajectory prompts: EDTC condenses long RGB trajectories into navigation-critical keyframes, VTP overlays path, turn, and goal cues onto these anchors, VTMod injects the resulting trajectory signals into the visual encoder, and VT-GRPO further calibrates this spatial injection during training, all without requiring a navigation graph, pre-built map, or scene reconstruction. On the challenging R2R-CE and RxR-CE Val Unseen benchmarks, VTInstructor sets a new state of the art across all standard NLG metrics, surpassing the strongest baseline by +0.357 CIDEr and +0.109 CIDEr, respectively. Beyond automatic metrics, VTInstructor-generated instructions raise a frozen follower's success rate to 63.3%, a +14.7 percentage-point gain over the best competing instruction source, and provide consistent data augmentation gains of +3 SR points on downstream navigation tasks.

</details>


### [338] [PhaseLoRA: Control-Regime-Conditioned Low-Rank Adaptation for Continuous-Action Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15285)
*Yufei Guo,Yinan Wu,Haoran Duan,Guiguang Ding,Jungong Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的高效微调方法PhaseLoRA，通过引入动作阶段相关的调节机制，提升了视觉-语言-动作策略在连续控制任务中的适应能力，实验上比现有方法表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的参数高效微调（PEFT）方法在对VLA策略进行适应时，普遍采用时序上一致的参数更新，而忽略了实际操作中不同阶段（如接近、抓取、搬运等）对策略适应性的不同需求。本文旨在解决这一阶段依赖适应的问题。

Method: 提出PhaseLoRA，将轻量化的LoRA参数化与阶段性上下文信息结合，在每个动作预测步骤，利用两个弱监督描述符（精细操作倾向和事件/边界强度）调制LoRA因子，实现每个阶段不同方向的低秩参数更新，同时保持主干网络参数基本冻结。

Result: 在LIBERO基准测试中，PhaseLoRA比相同参数量的高秩LoRA基线平均提升成功率12.2个百分点，并优于更强的LoRA变体。消融实验显示，随机调制或标量门控不能复制完整模型的性能；参数更新方向的分析揭示了与控制描述符关联的结构化时序变化。

Conclusion: PhaseLoRA通过结合阶段条件调制，为连续动作VLA策略提供了一种高效有效的参数微调途径，证明了轨迹内部条件调制在PEFT中的潜力。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) is a natural way to adapt pretrained vision-language-action (VLA) policies, but most adapter designs apply temporally static updates throughout a control rollout, overlooking the phase-dependent nature of continuous-action manipulation. Such policies traverse distinct regimes, including approach, contact transition, grasping, transport, and placement, each requiring different adaptation behaviors. We propose \textbf{PhaseLoRA}, a lightweight LoRA parameterization that conditions adaptation at each action-chunk prediction step using two weakly supervised descriptors: fine-control tendency and event/boundary intensity. PhaseLoRA modulates the LoRA left factor in the action expert, allowing the effective low-rank update direction to vary over time while keeping the backbone largely frozen. On LIBERO, PhaseLoRA improves average success rate by 12.2 points over a matched-parameter high-rank LoRA baseline and outperforms stronger LoRA variants. Ablations show that random temporal modulation and scalar gating do not reproduce the performance of the full model, while update-direction analyses reveal structured temporal variation associated with the predicted control descriptors. These results establish within-trajectory conditioning as an effective lightweight PEFT axis for continuous-action VLA policies.

</details>


### [339] [SCORE: Shape-Conforming Regions for Flight in Enclosed, Degraded Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15289)
*Eric Minwoo Kim,Jong-Kook Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种改进自主无人机在复杂空间（如洞穴、坍塌环境）中安全导航的方法，通过对keep-out区域的形状和适应性进行优化，在保证安全性的同时，提升了可用空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的keep-out区域多用凸形包围体，但这些方法在通道狭窄或感知能力下降时，会导致空间利用率低，阻碍无人机通过。希望在安全与空间利用之间取得更优平衡。

Method: 将nonconformity score应用到带符号距离场（SDF），生成紧贴障碍物几何的非凸keep-out区。同时结合传感器观测的体素级联合，补全单一传感器遗漏区域，并根据实际可见性自适应调整障碍物边缘的安全裕度。

Result: 在实际地下环境数据上表明，该方法比传统凸型baseline保留了更多可用空间，同时在相同安全认证下实现了更安全的闭环飞行。

Conclusion: 提出的非凸、自适应、自形变keep-out区，对自主无人机在感知受限复杂环境下的安全与高效飞行具有实际价值。

Abstract: Autonomous UAVs enter enclosed environments such as caves and collapsed structures that confine the vehicle and degrade perception. Conformal prediction provides a distribution-free guarantee by calibrating how far an obstacle keep-out must expand to absorb perception error at a target coverage level. However, existing keep-out regions use convex primitives whose bulges consume narrow passages and grow as perception degrades. Our main contribution defines the nonconformity score on a signed distance field (SDF). This produces a non-convex keep-out that tightly follows obstacle geometry and avoids the unnecessary bulging of equal-margin convex regions. Two supporting components keep this geometry usable as perception degrades. First, a voxelwise union of complementary sensor observations certifies voxels that any single sensor misses. Second, the margin around the obstacle adapts to measured visibility without weather labels or the online ground-truth feedback that single-pass flight cannot provide. Results on real subterranean data show that the resulting distribution-free, shape-conforming keep-out retains more usable free space than convex baselines at the same certified coverage, and produces safer closed-loop flight.

</details>


### [340] [MM-BEV: Enhancing Timeliness by Computing Where and When it Matters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15437)
*Liangkai Liu,Kang G. Shin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MM-BEV是一种高效的多模态鸟瞰图(BEV)感知系统，通过按需计算方式显著降低推理延迟，实现实时应用，同时在安全关键任务上几乎不损失性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态BEV感知系统结合了激光雷达和摄像头的优势，但实时性差，且现有方法没有充分利用输入数据的稀疏结构、模态时序对齐和任务优先级等信息，导致计算资源浪费。

Method: 提出MM-BEV系统，将感知任务分为‘强制’（紧急）和‘可选’（非紧急）两类，根据安全临界性优先处理对安全至关重要的区域，包括：(1)基于历史检测并运动外推的临界性区域选择；(2)稀疏、区域感知的特征提取，灵活采样图像及激光雷达信息；(3)依据场景动态自适应设置激光、图像分辨率和帧率的延时感知协调器；(4)异步调度器，实现感知与推理解耦，跳过过时帧。

Result: 在nuScenes公开数据集上，MM-BEV将推理延迟降低1.96倍、端到端延迟降低2.93倍，几何/安全关键召回率几乎无损。在真实无人车平台验证，平均延迟降至2.11倍，充分展示其实用性。

Conclusion: 通过分区优先级感知计算，MM-BEV能极大提升多模态BEV系统在实际场景下的实时性和效率，为自动驾驶感知系统的落地部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Multimodal bird's-eye-view (BEV) perception combines LiDAR depth accuracy with dense camera semantics, but its high computational cost and imperfect sensing conditions make real-time deployment challenging. Existing methods largely compress individual detectors and overlook three opportunities: structured sparsity within camera and LiDAR inputs, timing misalignment between modalities, and the fact that many detected objects do not affect the planner's immediate action. We present MM-BEV, a real-time multimodal BEV system guided by a simple principle: compute where and when it matters. MM-BEV divides perception into mandatory work for safety-critical objects within braking distance of the ego vehicle and with short time-to-collision (TTC), and optional work for less urgent regions. It prioritizes mandatory work and reduces or sheds optional work under tight compute budgets. MM-BEV integrates four mechanisms: (1) a criticality-ranked temporal ROI selector based on motion-extrapolated detections from prior frames; (2) sparse, ROI-aware feature extraction using shared-shape camera crops at context-adaptive resolution and ROI-aware LiDAR voxelization; (3) a latency-aware coordinator that adapts LiDAR sweeps, image resolution, and keyframes according to scene dynamics and TTC; and (4) an asynchronous scheduler that decouples sensing from inference and skips stale frames. On nuScenes, MM-BEV reduces inference latency by 1.96x and end-to-end latency by 2.93x, with no loss in geometry-critical recall and only a 0.2 percentage-point drop in safety-critical recall. On a Clearpath Husky A300 equipped with an Ouster-128 LiDAR, BEV cameras, and a Jetson AGX Orin, MM-BEV further reduces mean latency by 2.11x, demonstrating its potential for real-world autonomous systems.

</details>


### [341] [Accelerating Mixed Discrete-Continuous Motion Planning via Neural Graphs of Convex Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15440)
*Ananya Trivedi,Sarvesh Prajapati,Mohamed Khalid M Jaffar,Zhexin Xu,David Rosen,Taskin Padir*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合图注意力网络（GAT）的学习方法，加速基于凸集图（GCS）框架的运动规划，提高在线重规划的效率并维持高成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GCS 框架尽管能有效统一离散决策与连续轨迹的联合优化，但在实际在线重规划时，由于涉及大量复杂子问题的凸松弛计算，面临极大的计算量瓶颈，难以实时应用。

Method: 作者提出以图注意力网络（GAT）替代GCS中的凸松弛步骤，网络预测图中高概率路径集合，再用轻量排序网络根据轨迹代价预估对候选路径排序，并按次序提前终止搜索，以高效获得近似最优解。

Result: 在多种机器人任务（如3D四旋翼、7自由度机械臂及平面推送接触规划）下，无论目标函数和约束是否为凸，该方法较标准GCS提速1-2个数量级，并保持100%成功率，仅在解的最优性上略有折中。

Conclusion: 结合图神经网络与GCS的方法显著提升了运动规划的在线效率，为复杂机器人决策提供了创新且实用的加速方案，有望促进基于GCS的智能体在实时场景中的广泛应用。

Abstract: Motion planning problems such as collision-free navigation and contact-rich manipulation can be naturally formulated as optimization problems that couple discrete decisions with continuous trajectories. The Graphs of Convex Sets (GCS) framework offers a practical solution to these problems. It represents discrete decisions as nodes of a graph and encodes continuous trajectories in the edges connecting them. However, the resulting optimization subproblems can become computationally prohibitive for online replanning.
  In this work, we propose a learning-based strategy to mitigate this limitation. Specifically, we replace the costly convex relaxation step required by nominal GCS with a single forward pass through a Graph Attention Network that predicts a set of highly probable candidate paths through the graph. A lightweight ranking network then orders these candidates by their estimated trajectory cost. Evaluating them in this order, we terminate our search early while still recovering a near-optimal motion plan. We validate the resulting pipeline across diverse robotic tasks, including collision-free motion planning for a 3D quadrotor and a 7-DoF manipulator, and planning through contact for planar pushing. Across both convex and non-convex cost and constraint settings, our approach yields up to two orders of magnitude speedup over nominal GCS while maintaining a 100% success rate, at the cost of some suboptimality in the recovered solutions. Code implementations and video demonstrations can be found at https://neural-gcs.github.io/.

</details>


### [342] [GUIDER: Evaluating Goal-Free Human Intent Inference for Teleoperated Manipulation on Real-Robot Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15446)
*Nicholas Kenny,Cesar Alan Contreras,Basile Ouedraogo,Rustam Stolkin,Manolis Chiou,Maria Kyrarini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一个无需预设目标、基于概率的人类意图推断框架（GUIDER），用于机器人操作任务，并在多种辅助场景下进行了评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的人类意图推断方法通常依赖于明确的目标设定，而实际应用中目标往往并不明确。为了提升机器人对复杂真实环境下操作者意图的推断能力，需要开发无需预设目标即可推断人类意图的框架。

Method: 本研究提出并部署了GUIDER系统，结合了在线概率更新、工作空间约束、支撑面过滤和优先抓取可行区域的抓取模式。在机器人臂操作任务的数据集上，对其在不同辅助场景（如泡茶、取药）下的人类意图推断性能进行了测试。所有功能均在保持原始时间顺序的数据上进行了实验。

Result: 在三个场景共20步操作任务中，GUIDER系统在所有情况下都准确地推断了正确的抓取候选集；实现了3.7秒内做出高置信预测，首次抓取前剩余时间平均为49.6秒，预测稳定性为96.4%，每次意图感知阶段的运行时间均值/中位数为4.857/4.474秒。

Conclusion: GUIDER系统无需预设明确目标，能够稳健、高效地推断复杂场景下的人类操作者意图，在实际机器人辅助任务中具有很好的应用前景。

Abstract: This paper presents an evaluation of a goal-free probabilistic framework for human intent inference during robotic manipulation. We deploy the Global User Intent Dual-phase Estimation for Robots (GUIDER) on data collected from a robotic arm to test the manipulation phase across various assistance scenarios, including making tea and fetching medicine. To support operation, we add online probability updates, workspace limits, support-plane filtering, and a grasping mode that prioritizes feasible grasp regions, all of which are tested on the recorded data while preserving its original temporal conditions. Across 20 manipulation steps in three scenarios, GUIDER estimated human intent within the correct grasp-candidate set in all cases and achieved a time to confident prediction of 3.7 s, a remaining time before first grasp of 49.6 s, a prediction stability of 96.4%, and a runtime of 4.857/4.474 s (mean/median) per perceptual phase of intent.

</details>


### [343] [Detachable Wire Drive : Reconfigurable Robot Architecture with Shared Actuators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15461)
*Takahiro Hattori,Kento Kawaharazuka,Kei Okada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种“可拆卸线驱动”重构机器人系统，通过共享昂贵且笨重的执行器，实现不同机器人形态的高效重构和多功能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前重构机器人通常采用集中式结构，每个模块都需独立执行器，导致系统重量、体积和成本显著增加。为解决该问题，本文探索执行器共享方案。

Method: 设计了一种“线缆拆卸单元”，可物理分离或重连线缆驱动路径，将多个执行器集中到一个基座单元中。开发了2自由度刚性臂、连续体机械臂和两种不同结构的夹爪，这些末端组件可互换且共享同一组执行器。

Result: 实验结果证明该系统在机械拆卸过程中的可靠性，控制框架能够无缝管理不同结构的切换和集成。

Conclusion: 该方案为实现更高效、多功能的重构机器人系统提供了可行路径，降低了整体重量、体积和成本。

Abstract: Reconfigurable robots offer significant potential for adapting to diverse tasks; however, conventional centralized architectures often require dedicated actuators for each module, leading to substantial increases in overall system weight, volume, and cost. To address these challenges, this paper presents the "Detachable Wire Drive," a reconfigurable robotic system that enables the sharing of heavy and expensive actuators across various morphologies. The core of this system is the "Wire Detach Unit," a mechanism designed to physically split and reconnect wire drive paths, allowing motors to be consolidated into a common base unit. We demonstrate the versatility of this approach by developing a 2-DOF rigid arm, a continuum arm, and two distinct grippers, all of which are interchangeably attached to, and driven by, a single shared actuator set. Experimental results validate the mechanical reliability of the detachment process and the control framework's ability to seamlessly manage transitions between configurations, highlighting a path toward more efficient and multi-functional robotic systems.

</details>


### [344] [Vision-Based Tactile Intelligence for Robotics: Sensing, Learning, and Embodied Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15490)
*Peng Zhou,Jun Hu,Sihan Chen,Zeqing Zhang,Haofei Ma,Zhenyu Lu,Sichao Liu,Xueqian Wang,Pai Zheng,Xiang Li,Shan Luo,Jia Pan,David Navarro-Alarcon,Chenguang Yang,Michael Yu Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该综述论文系统梳理了基于视觉的触觉传感器（VBTS）在机器人复杂接触任务中的应用，从硬件、学习方法、仿真和数据集等多维度综合分析，旨在推动触觉智能的发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统触觉传感器信号稀疏、低维，难以满足复杂机器人感知与交互需求。VBTS通过将接触形变转化为高分辨率图像，有望突破现有限制，成为高信息量触觉感知的重要方案。因此本综述对VBTS进行全面梳理。

Method: 论文从四个方面梳理VBTS系统：（1）将主流硬件按可变形弹性体设计、尺寸形状、光学设计进行分类；（2）层次化介绍AI学习方法，包括从低层信号理解到高层任务决策及基础模型；（3）分析仿真平台和触觉数据集，用于扩展训练和现实迁移；（4）讨论跨传感器适配、基准测试和实际部署。

Result: 梳理得到了一套硬件分类体系、分层学习方法框架，以及与仿真平台和数据集结合用于规模化训练与评测的概览，指出了系统性集成的优势与挑战，并提出了未来发展方向。

Conclusion: 本文提供了VBTS领域软硬件、AI算法、仿真平台和数据集的全景综述，有助于推动触觉智能技术在接触丰富机器人任务中的应用和发展。

Abstract: Tactile sensing is essential for robots in contact-rich tasks, yet many tactile sensors still provide sparse, low-dimensional signals that do not capture sufficient information for complex robotic perception and interaction. Vision-based tactile sensors (VBTSs) offer a powerful alternative by con-verting contact-induced deformation of a soft interface into im-ages. The image-based formulation gives VBTSs high-resolution, information-rich tactile observations that enable complex robotic tasks. This review surveys the full VBTS pipeline and treats sensing hardware, learning methods, simulation, and datasets as an integrated sensing-and-learning system. We 1) organize representative VBTSs into a hardware taxonomy structured by deformable elastomer design, sensor size and shape, and optical system design to guide future sensor development; 2) present a hierarchical view of learning-based tactile intelligence from low-level signal understanding to task-level policies and foundation models; and 3) examine simulation platforms and tactile datasets as a scaling layer, together with sim-to-real transfer and cross-sensor adaptation for training, benchmarking, and deployment. Finally, we identify open challenges and future directions for VBTSs in robotics. By providing a holistic view of how hardware, AI architectures, simulation, and datasets interact, this review aims to advance tactile intelligence for contact-rich robotic tasks.

</details>


### [345] [Temporal Logic Guided Universal Task Representations for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15509)
*Hao Zhang,Zhangli Zhou,Zhen Kan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 作者提出了一种基于时序逻辑（LTL）的通用任务表示框架 LOTUS，显著提升了强化学习（RL）智能体在多样任务中的表现、泛化能力和学习效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有任务表示方法通常针对特定场景，泛化性差，且依赖RL控制器的梯度信号，易导致表示质量下降和学习效率低。为解决这些问题，作者希望构建一种既能泛化于多种场景、又独立于特定RL算法的通用任务表示框架。

Method: 作者设计了一种可从LTL公式建模任务关系及语义的新型任务表示结构。创新性地将LTL编码器视为策略进行更新，并利用bisimulation度量，理论上保证了表示的行为等价性、最优性保真和轨迹鲁棒。LOTUS可无缝集成于各类RL算法。

Result: 实验证明，LOTUS在单任务上加速收敛超20%；在未见过的操纵任务上成功率高出15%-45%；在含有更复杂子目标或合取约束的多任务环境中，泛化性能提升超过25%。各项指标均优于主流方法。

Conclusion: LOTUS极大提升了RL智能体的任务表示泛化力、学习速度和表现，可广泛应用于多种复杂任务场景，有理论和实践的双重优势。

Abstract: Task guided agents demonstrate strong performance in a wide range of complex tasks. However, most existing task representation algorithms are tailored to specific contexts and struggle to generalize across diverse scenarios. Moreover, they typically depend on gradient signals from reinforcement learning controllers to update their weights, which can degrade both representation quality and learning efficiency. To overcome these limitations, we propose LOTUS, a temporal logic inspired universal task representation framework that can be seamlessly integrated into any RL algorithm to enhance agent performance across diverse task settings. Specifically, we design a novel task representation architecture capable of modeling relationships and extracting task semantics from LTL formulas. We further introduce a more effective update mechanism that treats the LTL encoder as a policy, thereby improving representation capacity. To enhance stability and robustness, LOTUS leverages the bisimulation metric, which provides theoretical guarantees for LTL representation, including behavioral equivalence, optimality fidelity, and trajectory robustness. Experimental results show that LOTUS outperforms most existing methods in learning efficiency, generalization capability, and representation quality. Specifically, LOTUS accelerates convergence over 20% in single-task scenarios, achieves a 15%-45% higher success rate in unseen manipulation tasks, and improves generalization performance over 25% in complex multi-task environments with increased sub-goal depth or conjunctions. The corresponding code, videos, and appendix are available at: https://lotus-website.github.io/.

</details>


### [346] [Degenerate in Whose Frame? An Equivariance Condition for Degeneracy Detection in LiDAR Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15532)
*Yujie Zhang,Chunlei Zhao,Yuzong Lin,Yuxuan Guo,Xiaohui Jia,Jinyue Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了用于LiDAR配准的退化检测器是否能够输出与场景无关的六自由度轴向标签，并指出主流做法存在框架相关和物理不变性问题，提出了等变度量下的新判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LiDAR配准退化检测依赖于每轴二进制标签，判定可观/不可观方向。然而，这些标签可能因坐标系变换（如主体坐标系变化）而改变，从而引发实际应用中的一致性与物理不变性疑问。作者希望明确退化标签是否为场景固有属性，更普适且物理一致的判据是什么。

Method: 论文从理论上分析点到平面信息矩阵随坐标变换的性质，指出其通过合同变换而非相似变换变化，原有判据不具不变性。引入等变度量下广义特征值问题，将标签判决转化为体素尺度无关、坐标系无关的广义本征分解。通过四个公开数据集的365帧配对实验，分析不同判据输出一致性和实际对配准修正的影响。

Result: 实验显示，基于现有做法的每轴标签在换坐标系或场景移动下频繁变动，而新提出的等变广义特征值标签在绝大多数帧对上保持稳定，门限跨序列也适用。但即使采用等变度量，按轴标签本身依然可能变化，判据根本问题在所报告的量。

Conclusion: 论文强调用于配准退化检测的标签本身不总是场景不变属性，即使改进了判据后依旧存在报告量随框架改变而改变的问题。推荐采用等变度量下的广义特征值判据以提升实用一致性，指出未来还需改革报告方式以反映真正的不变量。

Abstract: Degeneracy detectors for LiDAR registration commonly return six per-axis binary labels. We ask whether these labels are properties of the scene. Under a body-frame change, the point-to-plane information matrix transforms by congruence, H' = Ad(T)^T H Ad(T), not similarity. Congruence preserves nullity and, through the adjoint reparameterization, identifies the same physical twist subspace; the per-axis footprint and a thresholded spectrum need not be invariant. In a noise-free circular tunnel, shifting the origin by one metre changes which degrees of freedom are flagged. A generalized criterion Hv = lambda Mv is universally frame-independent over positive-semidefinite information forms if and only if its metric rule is equivariant. No fixed metric qualifies, while a rig-adapted one exists only at zero screw pitch, met in one of nineteen surveyed calibrations. The equivariant point-displacement metric M = sum_i J_i^T J_i yields dimensionless, scene-scale-invariant generalized eigenvalues. They are invariant to body frame, consistent changes of length unit and scene scales; the threshold also transfers empirically across sequences. Across 365 frame pairs from four public sequences, labels rarely change at practical extrinsic magnitudes, yet a remapping estimator's correction differs between body-frame choices on 44.5-69.5% of pairs, with a median of 0.7-4.0 mm and a maximum of 0.87 m. The per-axis footprint changes even under the equivariant metric, placing the fundamental issue in the reported quantity.

</details>


### [347] [Contact Modes Are Strata: What Geometric Structure Buys in Discrete-Continuous Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15541)
*Phone Thiha Kyaw,Jonathan Kelly*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新型的接触丰富操作规划方法，将接触模式视作配置空间中的分层（stratum），并通过在分层上的地质线段实现高效规划。实验验证了方法的有效性和通用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器人操作中，涉及多个接触点的问题（contact-rich manipulation）难点在于既要选择合适的接触（离散决策），又要规划在接触下的运动（连续决策），两者交织且增加了问题复杂度。传统方法对接触序列依赖大，通用性与效率有限。

Method: 作者提出将接触模式直接映射为配置空间中的分层(stratum)，然后通过分层间步行（walk over strata）及每个分层内的地质线运动（geodesics）进行规划，不需预先给定接触模式或序列。

Result: 该方法在两个仿真任务中进行了验证，包括带障碍物推T型块和灵巧手重定位方块，均能在几秒内求得有效方案，无需预设接触序列。

Conclusion: 将接触操作的离散与连续部分统一成一个规划框架，极大提升了操作效率与通用性，在复杂操作任务中表现突出。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation poses a discrete question and a continuous one at once, namely which contacts are active and how to move while they hold. The two are coupled by a change of dimension, since each contact that a robot maintains confines its motion to a lower-dimensional manifold. We make that coupling the explicit object of planning by observing that a contact mode is not merely analogous to a stratum of the configuration space; it is one. A plan is then a walk over strata whose within-stratum segments are geodesics. On two contact-rich manipulation tasks in simulation, pushing a T-shaped block around obstacles and reorienting a cube in a dexterous hand, our planner returns solutions within seconds with no mode, contact sequence, or stratum given in advance.

</details>


### [348] [MistyPilot: Enabling Social-Robot Control through Multi-Agent LLM Skill Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15549)
*Xiao Wang,Lu Dong,Ifeoma Nwogu,Srirangaraj Setlur,Venu Govindaraju*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了MistyPilot框架，实现了从自然语言高层指令控制社交机器人，自动分派任务给不同代理，显著降低机器人编程门槛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上，编程社交机器人需手动调用API、管理任务状态、绑定传感器事件，流程复杂且易出错，开发门槛高。现有接口难以同时处理物理反应和社交互动，限制了自然语言指令的高效转化和执行。

Method: 作者提出了MistyPilot：基于多智能体大模型对自然语言指令进行解释。一个任务路由器将任务分配给物理互动代理（负责传感器控制、技能调用）或社交互动代理（负责对话管理、多模态生成等）。社交代理还可重用历史生成结果提升效率。

Result: 在Misty实体机器人上，通过传感器-技能绑定、技能执行等5组实验，MistyPilot路由准确率高、任务解析等表现优异，对比单智能体基线模型效果更稳定。12名体验者也反馈交互体验好、易用性高。

Conclusion: MistyPilot有效提升了自然语言对社交机器人的可编程性和交互质量，降低技能开发复杂度，未来代码将开源，有望推动相关研究与应用。

Abstract: Programming small social robots from natural-language instructions requires more than invoking isolated APIs. Interactive tasks combine reactive physical behaviors with stateful social behaviors, while existing interfaces often require developers to manually compose APIs into skills, configure their parameters, bind sensor events to skills, and manage task states at runtime. We present MistyPilot, a multi-agent LLM framework that interprets high-level natural-language instructions and orchestrates the corresponding skills on the Misty social robot. A Task Router dispatches each instruction to one of two specialized agents: a Physically Interactive Agent for sensor-triggered robot control and direct skill invocation, and a Social Interaction Agent for dialogue-oriented task-state management and context-dependent multimodal response generation. To improve efficiency, the Social Interaction Agent reuses previously generated results when applicable and invokes full generation otherwise. We evaluate MistyPilot on five component-level suites, with sensor bindings and skill invocations executed on the physical Misty robot, and a preliminary user study with 12 participants. MistyPilot attains high accuracy on routing, sensor-skill binding, task-state parsing, result reuse, and skill extension up to 100 skills, and lower variance than an otherwise identical single-agent baseline, while participants report positive perceptions of usability and interaction quality. The code will be made publicly available via the project page.

</details>


### [349] [ReForce: Learning Force-aware Retargeting for Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15560)
*Yuhang Wu,Lingqi Zeng,Changwei Jing,Jianglong Ye,Xiaolong Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为ReForce的力感知重定向方法，可以将人类操作中的运动和力传递到机器人动作，实现更精确的接触控制。实验显示其在多指接触和力跟踪方面优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的人体演示到机器人操作的迁移大多仅处理位置/运动（运动学重定向），而实际操作效果主要由手与物体间的力（动力学）决定，导致现有方法难以还原复杂精细的人类操作。本文希望突破这一限制，使机器人能重建人类操纵的真实接触与力。

Method: 提出了ReForce方法，在已有人体运动重定向基础上，利用通用力跟踪器预测残差动作，用于修正机器人动作以实现目标力。力跟踪器在大规模仿真数据上训练，既支持实时遥操作，也支持离线数据转换。

Result: 在仿真和真实机器人平台上，ReForce在纸杯抓取、钳子操作等多接触任务中，实现了更低的力跟踪误差和更强的多指接触控制，优于现有纯运动学方法。

Conclusion: 结合力感知后的重定向方法能更准确地转译人类演示，使机器人更好地完成需要精细力控制的操纵任务，推动了人-机操作迁移的发展。

Abstract: Human demonstrations offer a scalable data source for dexterous manipulation, but transferring them to robot actions remains challenging due to the embodiment gap. Today's retargeting is mostly kinematic, yet manipulation is decided by force, which governs how the hand interacts with the object and how the object moves. In this paper, we present ReForce, a Force-aware Retargeting method that turns human motion and forces into robot actions that reproduce the intended contact. ReForce predicts a residual on the kinematically retargeted action to reach the desired force, using a general force tracker trained on large-scale simulation interactions. It supports both online force-aware teleoperation and offline data translation. In simulation and on real hardware, ReForce achieves lower force-tracking error and stronger multi-finger contact engagement on contact-rich tasks such as paper-cup grasping and tongs manipulation.

</details>


### [350] [Not All History Helps: Velocity-Aware Selective Memory for Long-Horizon End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15573)
*Yuchen Liu,Ziying Song,Shengkai Zhang,Jiannan Chen,Peiliang Wu,Lei Yang,Bin Sun,Yan Gong,Li Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了StableDrive，一种面向自动驾驶长时域规划的新方法，显著提升了在复杂交通环境下的规划可靠性和安全性，并在多个主流基准上刷新了SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期规划对自动驾驶至关重要，但现有方法依赖历史状态带来的先验信息可能因时滞或冲突而不可靠，急需能够动态权衡历史信息和当前阶段的有效机制，以提升连贯性和安全性。

Method: StableDrive核心包括两个创新：1）Selective Momentum Memory（SMM），用Mamba选择性状态空间算子，有选择地融入和控制前一周期自规划状态对当前周期的影响；2）Motion-Stage Training Scaffold（MSTS），结合运动阶段、长时域轨迹和纵向运动等多重监督，训练具备阶段感知的未来运动预测网络，训练后推理时去除MSTS。最终通过参数中点挑选，部署无需模型集成或额外推理计算。

Result: 在nuScenes数据集MomAD评测协议下，StableDrive在1至6秒所有规划指标上均达SOTA：平均碰撞率降低23.3%，TPC降低30.9%，L2降低11.8%；在LT-nuScenes数据集6秒碰撞率下降23.81%，TPC降10.9%，L2降6.37%；在NAVSIM v1/v2三项指标均达最佳，尤其是v2 navhard场景EPDMS提升5.7分。

Conclusion: StableDrive在单模型条件下显著提升了长时域规划的安全性和鲁棒性，不依赖集成和多模型推理，具备实际部署价值，可为复杂动态交通场景中的自动驾驶提供更稳定、可靠的决策基础。

Abstract: Reliable long-horizon planning remains a key challenge in end-to-end autonomous driving. By accounting for future motion evolution and potential consequences, it provides forward-looking guidance for safe and consistent driving in evolving traffic environments. Existing methods use historical planning states as temporal context. Self-generated history may become stale or conflict with the current motion stage, introducing unreliable priors. We propose StableDrive to address cross-cycle historical reliability and within-horizon motion-stage evolution. Selective Momentum Memory (SMM), implemented with a Mamba selective state-space operator, controls the influence of the preceding self-predicted planning state on the current cycle. Motion-Stage Training Scaffold (MSTS) uses motion-stage, long-horizon trajectory, and longitudinal-motion supervision to guide stage-aware future motion learning and is removed before inference. A fixed parameter midpoint between two architecture-aligned endpoints yields a single deployable SMM planner without model ensembling or extra inference-time computation. On nuScenes under the MomAD evaluation protocol, StableDrive achieves SOTA performance across all reported planning metrics from 1 to 6 s, reducing average collision rate by 23.3%, TPC by 30.9%, and L2 by 11.8% over the best previously reported value for each metric. On the curated Longitudinal-Transition nuScenes (LT-nuScenes), StableDrive reduces 6-s collision rate by 23.81%, TPC by 10.90%, and L2 by 6.37%. On NAVSIM v1 and v2, StableDrive achieves the highest PDMS/EPDMS in all three reported settings, including a 5.7-point EPDMS gain on v2 navhard over the previous best.

</details>


### [351] [Algorithm-Architecture Co-Design for Efficient VLA Inference via Speculative Inference and Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15636)
*Chunyu Qi,Zhuoran Song,Jian Weng,Haozhe Jiang,Xueyuan Liu,Naifeng Jing,Guanghui He,Xiaoyao Liang,Haibing Guan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SpecVLA通过状态感知的推理调度和硬件协同设计，实现了具有效率和可靠性的体感智能（Embodied AI）实时机器人操控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前VLA（视觉-语言-动作）模型在体感智能领域表现突出，但推理计算开销大且预测动作长度有限，难以实现实时操控。现有硬件如Dadu-Corki没有充分利用机器人与环境的交互模式，导致预测动作长度短，降低效率。作者希望发掘环境中的主动/非主动状态，实现更高效推理。

Method: 1. 发现机器人环境中存在主动（需精准动作）与非主动（动作影响小）状态切换。
2. 提出SpecVLA算法-系统协同方案：
   - 算法上，采用状态感知的推理调度，并用差分残差与分块混合精度量化构建小型验证模型sVLA。
   - 系统上，设计GPU+专用硬件的异构架构，VLA和sVLA并行执行，实现推理与验证解耦。

Result: 在OpenVLA和RDT上测试，利用LIBERO与ManiSkill基准，SpecVLA显著降低端到端延迟，同时保持任务成功率。

Conclusion: SpecVLA通过长动作预测与及时验证，实现了高效且可靠的实时机器人操作，推动体感智能应用落地。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in the field of embodied AI, but their high computational cost and limited predicted action length hinder real-time deployment. Although Dadu-Corki, a dedicated accelerator for efficient embodied AI, has been introduced, it does not exploit the inherent interaction patterns between the robot and its environment, which results in a relatively short predicted action length. We observe that robotic environments naturally alternate between active states-where precise actions are crucial-and inactive states-where actions have limited impact on task success. This insight enables a new scheduling opportunity: long-action-length speculative prediction in inactive states, paired with selective verification in active states.
  We propose SpecVLA, an algorithm-system co-design framework that adaptively balances action length, inference latency, and task reliability. On the algorithm side, SpecVLA introduces a state-aware VLA inference execution paradigm and a hardware-friendly construction of a smaller verification model (sVLA) using differential residuals and block-wise mixed-precision quantization. On the system side, we develop a heterogeneous architecture consisting of a GPU and a robotic-specific hardware module, along with a speculative dataflow that decouples VLA and sVLA through parallel execution. Comprehensive evaluations on OpenVLA and RDT across LIBERO and ManiSkill benchmarks show that SpecVLA reduces end-to-end latency significantly while preserving task success rate. By enabling long-action-length speculative prediction with timely verification, SpecVLA achieves real-time robotic manipulation with both high efficiency and reliability.

</details>


### [352] [Robo-Dopamine 2.0: History-Conditioned and OOD-Aware Process Reward Modeling for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15680)
*Yijie Xu,Haopeng Jin,Run Zhou,Shengbang Liu,Sixiang Chen,Hongyang Cheng,Sicheng Hu,Peterson Co,Jinwen Luo,Huajie Tan,Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Robo-Dopamine 2.0，一种历史感知且具备OOD鲁棒性的过程类奖励模型，通过配对预测和参考面板机制提升了机器人视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的鲁棒性和下游强化学习训练效率，在实际任务中取得了显著效果提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉-语言-动作模型在机器人操作中易受累积误差、场景变化和偏离轨迹的影响，强化学习虽可对预训练策略进行微调，但奖励稀疏、奖励工程成本高；多数视觉奖励模型依赖静态前后图像，难以处理时序歧义和OOD情况下的鲁棒性差异。

Method: 提出Robo-Dopamine 2.0，包括两大创新：(1) 基于历史的成对奖励机制，利用源对齐的参考面板为合成OOD查询和实时查询提供辅助，同时保持端点信息完整；(2) 设计了OOD感知的有符号进度空间，能有效表示有效进展、鲁棒性、失败和恢复，并引入Signed-Hop课程和过渡感知的重放策略，实现粗到细的行为排序与奖励校准。此外，构建了OOD轨迹数据集和多任务基准测试。

Result: 实验表明，参考面板将视觉排序一致性(VOC)平均值从0.967提升至0.986，OOD鲁棒VOC从0.906至0.958。在相同配对奖励预算下，Signed-Hop训练（25%重放）VOC达0.9872，优于对照组0.9858。下游强化学习中，完整模型在RoboTwin上平均成功率86.8%，实际插入任务71/80成功。

Conclusion: Robo-Dopamine 2.0有效提升了视觉奖励模型对历史和OOD的鲁棒性，在强化学习和实际机器人操控中的表现大幅优于现有方法，为视觉-语言-动作模型在复杂环境下的应用提供了有力工具。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models improve robotic manipulation but remain vulnerable to compounding errors, scene changes, and off-trajectory states. Reinforcement learning can refine pretrained VLA policies, yet sparse success signals hinder exploration, while engineered dense rewards are costly and task-specific. Existing learned visual reward models often rely on static before-after observations, causing temporal ambiguity and weak discrimination between robustness-preserving variations and task-invalid failures under out-of-distribution (OOD) execution. We introduce Robo-Dopamine 2.0, a history- and OOD-aware process reward model with a pairwise prediction interface. It combines (1) history-conditioned pairwise rewards that use source-aligned reference panels for synthetic OOD queries and observed rollout history for online queries, while preserving the queried endpoints, and (2) an OOD-aware signed progress space that represents valid progress, robustness, failure, and recovery. A Signed-Hop Curriculum with transition-aware replay learns coarse execution ordering before fine-grained progress calibration. We also construct an OOD trajectory dataset and a five-family benchmark. Reference panels improve mean visual order consistency (VOC) from 0.967 to 0.986 and OOD-robust VOC from 0.906 to 0.958. With the same 400K pairwise-reward budget, Signed-Hop training with 25% replay reaches 0.9872 mean VOC, compared with 0.9858 for a matched-pool shuffled control. In downstream reinforcement learning, the full model achieves 86.8% mean RoboTwin success and 71/80 successful real-world insertions.

</details>


### [353] [GAINS: Leveraging Inconsistent Human Intervention Signals in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15707)
*Xinyi Zhang,Yinuo Zhao,Pei Ren,Lechun Jiang,Huiqian Jin,Lei Sun,Dapeng Wu,Zhengping Che,Chi Harold Liu,Jian Tang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了GAINS框架，在强化学习中应对人类干预信号时序不一致及延迟等不完美问题，基于分布式RL方法提升机器人操作任务的安全性和效率，并在仿真与真实环境中取得显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实机器人部署中，通过人类干预纠正操作策略很有潜力，但现实中人类干预信号常存在时序不一致与延迟等缺陷，对此问题的研究不足。当前多关注干预动作本身的不完美，但对干预信号的时序缺陷关注较少。

Method: 提出GAINS框架，将分布式强化学习与分位数Q网络相结合，用于建模由于稀疏奖励和不一致人类干预带来的回报不确定性；并据此设计出基于悲观探索的安全高效策略，提升在有人工干预情境下的学习表现。

Result: GAINS在4个仿真操作任务和2个真实世界场景中，对比先进的基于人类干预方法，成功率提高22%，在恢复环节的成功率提升高达43%。

Conclusion: 针对人类干预带来的回报不确定性建模对现实基于干预的学习部署至关重要，GAINS有效提升了机器人任务成功率和恢复能力。

Abstract: Correcting robot manipulation policies through human intervention holds great promise for real-world deployment, yet human operators are inherently imperfect in both the actions they provide and the timing of their intervention signals. While the former has been extensively discussed in reinforcement learning (RL), the latter remains underexplored. At high control frequencies, human intervention signals are often delayed and inconsistent across time and state space. In this work, we present GAINS, a framework for leveraging inconsistent human intervention signals in RL. At the core of GAINS, we employ distributional RL with quantile Q-networks to model the return variability induced by sparse task rewards and inconsistent human interventions. Building on this distributional representation, we introduce a pessimistic exploration strategy that promotes safe and sample-efficient learning under human corrections. We evaluate GAINS on four diverse simulated manipulation tasks and two challenging real-world scenarios against state-of-the-art intervention-based methods. GAINS achieves a 22% higher task success rate than RLIF and improves recovery success by up to 43% in failure scenarios. These results highlight the importance of modeling return variability induced by human imperfection for real-world deployment of intervention-based learning.

</details>


### [354] [Some Modifications to Our End-to-End UAV Planner](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15741)
*Junjie Lu,Bailing Tian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文介绍对YOPO一阶段规划器的多项优化，包括使用两段式MINCO参数化、提升多模态预测、引入更智能的动态可行性约束、采用排序损失等，以提升轨迹表达能力与安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一阶段规划器如YOPO虽然可以通过端到端的方式高效生成轨迹并优化多目标损失（如安全、平滑和到达目标），但其软约束优化存在代价项相互竞争、非凸性和轨迹表达能力有限等问题，容易影响机器人在复杂环境中的实际表现。

Method: 主要改进包括：（1）使用两段式MINCO参数化，增强轨迹光滑性和灵活性；（2）将多模态预测扩展到不同的同伦类别，通过运动原语锁定轨迹类别，无需显式飞行走廊构建；（3）对动态可行性施加速率和加速度障碍函数，并根据曲率限制速度，实现在复杂环境和急转弯时自适应减速；（4）使用排序损失取代分数回归，提升候选轨迹排序准确性。

Result: 经过上述优化，YOPO能生成更丰富的轨迹，具备更安全的避障能力，飞行路径更直接，动态行为更为合理。

Conclusion: 本工作通过结构化与损失层面的若干改动，克服了一阶段差分可微轨迹优化的局限性，显著提升了轨迹规划的安全性和多样性，为高效安全的机器人轨迹生成提供了新思路。

Abstract: The one-stage planner YOPO maps a single depth image and the robot state directly to a set of candidate trajectories, trained by backpropagating through differentiable trajectory costs. This yields dense, geometrically informative supervision, but inherits the pathologies of soft-constrained optimization: the safety cost competes with the smoothness and goal-reaching terms, is non-convex across homotopy classes, and the single-piece polynomial is limited in expressiveness. In this report, we summarize several effective modifications. We adopt a two-piece MINCO parameterization, trading time for smoothness without altering the trajectory's spatial profile. We further lift YOPO's multi-modal prediction to span distinct homotopy classes, treating each motion primitive as a homotopy anchor that confines the trajectory to a feasible basin - without explicit safe-flight-corridor construction or front-end search. For dynamic feasibility, we impose barrier penalties on velocity and acceleration together with a curvature-dependent speed limit whose gradient acts only on the velocity, producing an adaptive-speed behavior that decelerates in cluttered regions or sharp turns. We replace score regression with a ranking loss, preventing small score errors from reordering the candidate set. These yield richer trajectory representations, safer obstacle avoidance, and more direct flight paths.

</details>


### [355] [Making two action heads agree: coordination mechanisms and a runtime collapse certificate for flow-matching policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15748)
*Jinhui Sun,Wei Zhou,Bowen Yang,Xinliang Xiao,Li Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文分析了双分支运动预测系统在多模态任务中出现的协调困难，并系统性实验了若干协调机制及其影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器人等多模态任务中，使用关节和末端执行器空间的双分支解码能提供物理解释的失效信号，但各分支独立采样可能导致错误失效报警，因此亟需研究分支协调的方法与效益。

Method: 论文探讨了四类协调机制，包括共享辅助潜变量、共享噪声、解码器一致性正则化、离散分区，并结合理论分析和多个任务环境下的系统性实验，评估其协同表现、代价及副作用。提出了基于Gini-Simpson多样性的概率界，用于无标签评价协调而非模式坍缩。

Result: 辅助潜变量被证实在最优情况下不能提升协调；共享源噪声可增强或削弱协调，效果依赖表达映射；一致性正则虽可中等提升协调，但降低有效组合比；训练支持的离散分区可稳健实现高协调。分析数据表明，在LIBERO-Plus环境中，系统产生的错误报警率与多模态数量相关，但所用干预措施未能有效降低假警报。

Conclusion: 分支协调在多模态运动解码中至关重要，不同机制有各自权衡。离散分区机制最为有效且稳健。提供了一套理论和工具，用于定量分析协调质量并甄别协调与模式坍缩。本研究为任务中有效检测异常提供了路线，但现有方案的误报率仍需进一步降低。
